# Hydra Browsers v1 — Plan 07: Dashboard (`@hydra/web`) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the Hydra Browsers dashboard in `packages/web`: the macOS-style window with the sidebar, Login and Change password, the session page (toolbar, Live, Tokens, Sharing, Activity, Settings), and the admin screens, on the API of spec section 12 and index section 5.1.

**Architecture:** A Vite single-page app with React 19 and the React Router data router. `src/theme/tokens.css` holds the `STYLE.md` tokens as CSS variables and maps them for Tailwind CSS v4 (`@theme inline`). `src/components/os/` holds the `STYLE.md` components, built on Radix primitives. `src/api/` wraps `fetch` (same origin, login cookie, JSON body `{}` for requests that are not GET) and gives TanStack Query hooks that validate each response with the `@hydra/shared` schemas. `src/lib/sse.ts` keeps the two SSE streams of spec section 10.3, patches the query cache, and fetches the session data again after a reconnect. The Live view runs the dashboard side of the open flow of spec section 9.2: it ends the live grant of the previous session of the browser tab, gets a ticket, and loads the iframe.

**Tech Stack:** React 19.3.0, React Router 8.4.0, TanStack Query 5.104.1, Vite 8.3.2 with `@vitejs/plugin-react` 6.1.1, Tailwind CSS 4.3.3 (`@tailwindcss/vite`), Radix primitives (dialog, dropdown-menu, toggle-group, switch, checkbox, radio-group), `@tabler/icons-react` 3.48.0; Vitest 5.0.3, jsdom 30.1.1, Testing Library; TypeScript 6.0.3 and ESLint 10 from the root, `eslint-plugin-react-hooks` 7.1.1.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-hydra-browsers-design.md` (sections 3.2, 9.2 dashboard side, 9.4, 10.1 to 10.3, 12). Read also: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-10-00-index.md` (section 5.1 is the API contract), `STYLE.md` (binding for every component), `AGENTS.md`, and plan 01 (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-10-01-foundation.md`, which creates the empty `packages/web`).

## Global Constraints

- Exact versions only (spec section 3.4.1). Use the table "Versions" below; pnpm 12.9.0 and Node.js 24.21.0 come from the root.
- No commits (AGENTS.md rule 1). Each task ends with the Checkpoint step of index section 3.
- The spec is the source of truth. Do not edit it. `STYLE.md` changes only to add tokens (Task 2).
- Components use only the tokens and components of `STYLE.md`: the Tailwind utilities of the mapping (`bg-os-*`, `text-os-*`, `border-os-*`, `rounded-os-*`, `shadow-os-*`, `ease-os-*`, `font-sans`, `font-mono`, `rounded-xl`, `backdrop-blur-2xl`) and `(--os-…)` variables. No literal color, font size, radius, shadow, z-index, or duration in a component (AGENTS.md rule 3).
- The UI texts of `STYLE.md` section 7 exactly. The banner texts come from `MESSAGES` of `@hydra/shared`.
- The names of index section 5 exactly (`<Name>Schema`, `MESSAGES`, the constants). The dashboard imports types and schemas from `@hydra/shared` and does not copy them.
- Each API response is validated with its `@hydra/shared` schema. Each request that is not GET sends `Content-Type: application/json` and a JSON body, `{}` when it has no data (spec section 6.4).
- The theme follows `prefers-color-scheme` (spec section 10.1). Minimum viewport width 1024 px.
- Live iframe: `allow="clipboard-read; clipboard-write; fullscreen; autoplay"`, `sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-pointer-lock"` (spec section 9.4).
- The production build goes to `packages/web/dist` and has no inline script (CSP of spec section 6.8; plan 03 serves `dist`).
- Tests: Vitest, jsdom, Testing Library. `fetch` and `EventSource` are mocked (`src/test/mock-api.ts`, `src/test/mock-event-source.ts`). No MSW.
- Prettier: `printWidth` 100, `singleQuote`, `semi`, `trailingComma` `all`. The code blocks of this plan are already formatted, so copy them exactly.

## Review Focus

1. A pasted URL in the allowlist (`https://bank.example/login`): the New session sheet shows the allowlist error and sends no request. Pinned in Task 13 ("rejects a pasted URL in the allowlist before it sends anything").
2. The gateway is not reachable when the dashboard loads (for example during a gateway restart): an error panel with "Try again" that recovers. Pinned in Task 11 ("shows an error with Try again when the server cannot be reached at load").
3. A Restart while the Live view is open (SSE `starting`, then `running`): the iframe goes away, and a new ticket loads it again. Pinned in Task 12 ("removes the iframe while the session restarts and gets a new ticket when it runs again").
4. The login session ends while the user works (any request answers 401): Login opens, and after the next login the user is back on the same page. Pinned in Task 12 ("opens Login when the login ends, and returns to the page after the next login").
5. The open session is deleted in another browser tab (SSE `deleted`): the page shows Not found and the sidebar drops the session. Pinned in Task 12 ("shows Not found when the open session is deleted elsewhere"). This test found a real gap during the dry run: removing an observed query does not re-render the page, so `removeSession` keeps the detail with the state `deleted`.

## Spec deviations

1. **Table header contrast (STYLE.md section 5, "Table").** STYLE.md gives header text `--os-gray-accessible` on `--os-gray-5`. In the light theme this is 4.16:1, below WCAG AA (spec section 10.3: "Text contrast meets WCAG AA in both themes"). The plan follows the spec: the light header background is `--os-gray-6` (4.68:1), the dark one stays `--os-gray-5` (6.3:1), and the contrast test checks the pair. Plan 07 may only add tokens to STYLE.md, so the reviewers should update the Table rule.
2. **Development URLs with ports.** Spec section 4 gives `http://hydra.localhost` and `http://live.hydra.localhost` "for example". The Vite dev server and the gateway need two ports, so development uses `http://hydra.localhost:5173` (Vite, the dashboard origin `HYDRA_PUBLIC_URL`) and `http://live.hydra.localhost:8080` (the gateway, `HYDRA_LIVE_URL`). Both stay `*.localhost` origins of one site (ports do not change the site).

Notes for the reviewers of STYLE.md (no spec deviation): Task 2 adds four tokens (`--os-traffic-red-border`, `--os-traffic-yellow-border`, `--os-traffic-green-border`, `--os-duration-out`). The Tailwind mapping also resets the default colors, text sizes, and shadows (`--color-*: initial` and two more lines), so a class like `bg-red-500` gives no CSS (STYLE.md rule 1). The Live status bar sits on `--os-surface`, not on the black stage, because `--os-gray-accessible` on black is 4.0:1.

## Index issues

1. **`pnpm-workspace.yaml` (owned by plan 01).** `eslint-plugin-react-hooks` 7.1.1 (the only version with an ESLint 10 peer range) depends on `@babel/core` 7, which depends on `semver@6.3.1`, and `trustPolicy: no-downgrade` stops the install with `ERR_PNPM_TRUST_DOWNGRADE`. Task 1 adds a reviewed `trustPolicyExclude` entry with a reason comment (spec section 3.4.6). Index section 4 should list this change for plan 07.
2. **`packages/web/eslint.config.js`** is new. It imports the root config of plan 01 and adds the React rules (ESLint 10 uses the nearest config file, plan 01 index issue 5).
3. **Response shapes that index section 5.1 does not name.** Plan 07 expects: `GET /api/sessions` → `SessionResource[]`; `GET /api/users` → `UserSummary[]`; `GET /api/sessions/:sid/tokens` → `Token[]` (bare JSON arrays); `POST /api/sessions`, `PATCH /api/sessions/:sid`, `POST /api/sessions/:sid/{start,stop,restart}` → `SessionResource`; `POST /api/auth/login` → `Me`; `POST /api/users` → `CreateUserResponse`; `PATCH /api/users/:id` and `POST /api/users/:id/unlock` → `UserSummary`; `POST /api/users/:id/reset-password` → `ResetPasswordResponse`; `POST /api/sessions/:sid/members` and `PATCH …/members/:userId` → `Member`; `POST /api/sessions/:sid/tokens` → `CreateTokenResponse`; `POST /api/sessions/:sid/live-ticket` → `LiveTicketResponse`; `GET …/logs` → `LogsResponse`; `GET …/audit` → `AuditPage`. All other routes: 204 (the client also accepts a body there). The plans of the server match this list (checked on 2026-10-10: plan 03 for auth, users, members, and tokens; plan 04 for the session routes; plan 06 for the live routes). The index should copy it.
4. **S5 fallback file (index section 6).** "UI help text in `@hydra/web`" has no path. Plan 07 puts it in `packages/web/src/lib/allowlist-help.ts` (`ALLOWLIST_HELP`).
5. **Radix packages.** Plan 01's version table names the umbrella package `radix-ui` 1.6.7. Plan 07 installs only the six primitives it uses, from the same release day (2026-07-24), for a smaller dependency tree (see "Versions"). `@testing-library/jest-dom` 7.0.1 is also not in that table.
6. **Rights changes and the live ticket.** Spec section 6.5 says "The dashboard then requests a new ticket", but `GET /api/events` carries only `session.state` and `session.activity`, and plan 04 keeps the stream open on `rights.changed` (it reloads the user and filters the events; it closes the stream only for a disabled user). Thus the dashboard sees a rights change only when it fetches the session again (window focus, SSE reconnect), and then renews the ticket if the `live.control` right changed. Until then the live view shows the disconnected Neko client, because the live proxy ends the old grant at once. If plan 04 closed the stream of the user on `rights.changed`, the reconnect would renew the ticket at once. The index should decide.
7. **`tokenId` audit filter.** Index 5.1 does not define what `tokenId` matches. For live events, plan 07 matches `actorType === 'token' && actorId === tokenId`, or `data.tokenId === tokenId`. Plan 03 should use the same rule.
8. **Updated audit events.** The Activity view replaces an event when the session SSE stream sends the same `id` again (a `mcp.tool_call` that goes from `pending` to `ok`). Index 5.7 does not say whether `AuditWriter.update` publishes an `audit` bus event; plans 02, 03, and 05 should send updated events on the stream.
9. **`<name>` in the `claude mcp add` command.** Plan 02 restricts token names to `[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}` because it reads `<name>` (spec section 10.2) as the token name. Plan 07 follows this: the token name is the MCP server name in the command and in the JSON config.
10. **Development environment.** `pnpm dev` (plan 01) starts the server and the web `dev` scripts. The server must run with `HYDRA_PUBLIC_URL=http://hydra.localhost:5173` and `HYDRA_LIVE_URL=http://live.hydra.localhost:8080` (see "Spec deviations" 2). The owner of the development variables (plan 01 index issue 6 suggests plan 08) should use these values.

## Behaviors seen in the dry run (2026-10-10)

The plan was run task by task in a copy of the plan 01 workspace with the `@hydra/shared` source of plan 02. After each task, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm typecheck`, and `pnpm --filter @hydra/web test` passed. Know these behaviors before you start:

- Radix Dialog returns the focus only to a `Dialog.Trigger`. The sheets have no trigger, so `Sheet` remembers the focused element in `onOpenAutoFocus` and focuses it in `onCloseAutoFocus`.
- Radix waits for CSS animations before it unmounts, not for transitions (spec: transitions only). `Sheet` keeps itself open for `--os-duration-out` and then closes. In jsdom the token is not loaded, so the delay is 0 ms, and the tests use `waitFor` after Esc. The production CSS writes `150ms` as `.15s`; `Sheet` reads both units.
- TanStack Query notifies components after a timer. After an SSE event in a test, use `findBy…` or `waitFor`, not `getBy…`.
- jsdom 30 has no `ResizeObserver` (the setup file stubs it; Radix needs it for a switch or checkbox in a form), no `EventSource` (`MockEventSource`), no `requestFullscreen` (the Live test defines it), and no clipboard before `userEvent.setup()`.
- Radix ToggleGroup in `single` mode renders `role="radiogroup"` with `role="radio"` items and `aria-checked`. Arrow keys move the focus, Enter or Space selects. Radix RadioGroup selects on arrow keys in browsers, but in jsdom only the focus moves, so the tests use Space.
- Vitest gives an empty string for each CSS import (also `?raw`) in the root project run, and Vite rewrites `new URL('./x', import.meta.url)` to an asset URL. Thus the theme tests read files with `readFileSync(join(import.meta.dirname, …))`.
- `eslint-plugin-react-hooks` 7 adds the React Compiler rules (`set-state-in-effect`, `refs`, `purity`, …). The code changes state in event handlers, timers, async callbacks, or with the render-time pattern (`if (prev !== value) setPrev(value)`), never directly in an effect body.
- The Vite dev server accepts the host `hydra.localhost` (and every `*.localhost`) and answers 403 to other hosts. Node.js resolves `hydra.localhost` to `::1` on macOS, but Vite listens on `127.0.0.1`: a local MCP client may need `127.0.0.1 hydra.localhost` in `/etc/hosts` (README).
- The production bundle is about 620 kB (React DOM, React Router, and zod are most of it); `chunkSizeWarningLimit` is 1024 kB.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the trust policy exception**

`eslint-plugin-react-hooks` 7.1.1 needs `semver@6.3.1` (through `@babel/core` 7), and `trustPolicy: no-downgrade` stops the install with `ERR_PNPM_TRUST_DOWNGRADE … High-risk trust downgrade for "semver@6.3.1" (possible package takeover)`. Review result: `semver` 6.3.1 was published on 2023-07-10 by the npm CLI team, has no install scripts, and is the newest 6.x; newer `semver` releases have npm provenance, 6.3.1 has none. Spec section 3.4.6 permits a `trustPolicyExclude` entry with a reason.

In `pnpm-workspace.yaml`, after the line `trustLockfile: false`, insert:

```yaml

# Trust policy exceptions (spec section 3.4.6). Each entry needs a review and a reason.
# semver@6.3.1 (2023-07-10, npm CLI team, no install scripts) comes through @babel/core 7, a
# dependency of eslint-plugin-react-hooks 7.1.1 (lint of @hydra/web, dev only). Newer semver
# releases have npm provenance and 6.3.1 has none, so the policy sees a downgrade.
# Reviewed on 2026-10-10 (plan 07).
trustPolicyExclude:
  - semver@6.3.1
```

Do not reformat the rest of the file (`.prettierignore` of plan 01 excludes it).

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the failing smoke test**

It proves three things: the tests load `@hydra/shared` from source, they run in jsdom, and they have the jest-dom matchers and the `ResizeObserver` stub.

`packages/web/src/test/environment.test.ts`:

```ts
import { MESSAGES } from '@hydra/shared';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

describe('test environment', () => {
  it('loads @hydra/shared from source and has jsdom with the Testing Library matchers', () => {
    document.body.innerHTML = `<p>${MESSAGES.agentActive}</p>`;
    expect(document.querySelector('p')).toHaveTextContent('Agent active');
    expect(typeof ResizeObserver).toBe('function');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Run the smoke test to verify that it fails**

Run: `pnpm --filter @hydra/web exec vitest run src/test/environment.test.ts`

```ts
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest';
import { cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach } from 'vitest';

// jsdom has no ResizeObserver. Radix uses it for switches and checkboxes inside forms.
class ResizeObserverStub {
  observe(): void {}
  unobserve(): void {}
  disconnect(): void {}
}
if (!('ResizeObserver' in globalThis)) {
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'ResizeObserver', {
    value: ResizeObserverStub,
    writable: true,
  });
}

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the Vite config, the ESLint config, and the README**

The dev proxy keeps the `Host` and the `Origin` header (no `changeOrigin`): the gateway routes by host and checks `Origin` against `HYDRA_PUBLIC_URL` (spec sections 2 and 6.4). The proxy key `^/s/[^/]+/mcp` is a regular expression, so other `/s/…` paths stay with Vite. The README gives the development URLs and the credit of spec section 10.1.

`packages/web/vite.config.ts`:

```ts
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import { defaultClientConditions, defineConfig } from 'vite';

/**
 * Development (spec sections 4 and 11.7). Open the dashboard at http://hydra.localhost:5173.
 * The gateway runs on the host on port 8080 with
 *   HYDRA_PUBLIC_URL=http://hydra.localhost:5173
 *   HYDRA_LIVE_URL=http://live.hydra.localhost:8080
 * The proxy keeps the Host and the Origin header of the browser (no changeOrigin), so the host
 * routing and the CSRF check of the gateway see the dashboard origin. The live view iframe loads
 * the live origin from the gateway directly.
 */
const GATEWAY = 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  resolve: { conditions: ['@hydra/source', ...defaultClientConditions] },
  server: {
    host: '127.0.0.1',
    port: 5173,
    strictPort: true,
    proxy: {
      '/api': { target: GATEWAY, changeOrigin: false },
      '^/s/[^/]+/mcp': { target: GATEWAY, changeOrigin: false },
    },
  },
  // One bundle for an internal tool: react-dom, react-router, and zod are most of it.
  build: { outDir: 'dist', emptyOutDir: true, chunkSizeWarningLimit: 1024 },
});
```

```js
import reactHooks from 'eslint-plugin-react-hooks';
import { defineConfig } from 'eslint/config';
import root from '../../eslint.config.js';

// ESLint uses the config file nearest to each linted file, so the dashboard adds the React
// rules here (plan 01 owns the root config).
export default defineConfig(root, {
```

`packages/web/README.md`:

````markdown
# @hydra/web

The Hydra Browsers dashboard: Vite, React 19, React Router, TanStack Query, Tailwind CSS v4, Radix
primitives, and Tabler icons. `STYLE.md` at the repository root defines the tokens and the
components. Read it before you change this package (`AGENTS.md` rule 3).

The visual style comes from [macos-react](https://github.com/ridvanonal/macos-react) by Rıdvan
Önal. This package uses its design values and has its own components. No code is copied (spec
section 10.1).

## Commands

- `pnpm --filter @hydra/web dev`: the Vite dev server on `http://hydra.localhost:5173`.
- `pnpm --filter @hydra/web build`: the production build in `packages/web/dist`. The gateway serves
  it with the headers of spec section 6.8. The build has no inline scripts.
- `pnpm --filter @hydra/web test`: the tests (Vitest, jsdom, Testing Library).
- `pnpm --filter @hydra/web typecheck`: `tsc -p tsconfig.json`.

## Development URLs

Spec section 4 gives `http://hydra.localhost` and `http://live.hydra.localhost` for development.
Browsers treat `*.localhost` as a secure context, so the `Secure` and `__Host-` cookies work over
HTTP. In development, the Vite dev server and the gateway listen on different ports:

- Dashboard: `http://hydra.localhost:5173` (Vite). Vite sends `/api/*` and `/s/<sid>/mcp` to the
  gateway on `127.0.0.1:8080`. It keeps the `Host` and the `Origin` header of the browser, so the
  host routing and the CSRF check of the gateway see the dashboard origin.
- Live view: `http://live.hydra.localhost:8080` (the gateway). The live iframe loads it directly.

Start the gateway with these values:

```sh
HYDRA_PUBLIC_URL=http://hydra.localhost:5173
HYDRA_LIVE_URL=http://live.hydra.localhost:8080
```

Use Chrome or Firefox. They resolve `*.localhost` to the loopback address. An MCP client on the host
(Node.js) can resolve `hydra.localhost` to `::1`, but Vite listens on `127.0.0.1` only. Add
`127.0.0.1 hydra.localhost` to `/etc/hosts` if the client cannot connect to the MCP URL of the token
sheet.
````

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { contrastRatio, gradientColors, over, parseColor } from './contrast';
import { parseThemeTokens, type TokenMap } from './css-tokens';

// Read the files that the browser build uses, so that the test and the CSS cannot differ.
const tokensCss = readFileSync(join(import.meta.dirname, 'tokens.css'), 'utf8');

const SYSTEM_COLORS = [
  'red',
  'orange',
  'yellow',
  'green',
  'mint',
  'teal',
  'cyan',
  'blue',
  'indigo',
  'purple',
  'pink',
  'brown',
  'gray',
] as const;

const themes = parseThemeTokens(tokensCss);

function color(tokens: TokenMap, name: string) {
  const value = tokens.get(name);
  if (value === undefined) throw new Error(`Missing token ${name}`);
  return parseColor(value);
}

describe('contrastRatio', () => {
  it('gives 21:1 for black on white and 1:1 for equal colors', () => {
    expect(contrastRatio(parseColor('#000'), parseColor('#fff'))).toBeCloseTo(21, 5);
    expect(contrastRatio(parseColor('#777'), parseColor('#777'))).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe.each([
  ['light', themes.light],
  ['dark', themes.dark],
] as const)('%s theme (STYLE.md section 8)', (theme, tokens) => {
  const surface = color(tokens, '--os-surface');
  const wallpaper = gradientColors(tokens.get('--os-wallpaper') ?? '');
  const backgrounds = [
    { name: 'surface', color: surface },
    ...wallpaper.flatMap((paper, i) => [
      { name: `surface 50% over wallpaper ${String(i)}`, color: over(surface, paper, 0.5) },
      { name: `surface 70% over wallpaper ${String(i)}`, color: over(surface, paper, 0.7) },
    ]),
  ];
  const textTokens = ['--os-foreground', ...SYSTEM_COLORS.map((c) => `--os-${c}-accessible`)];

  it('has two wallpaper colors', () => {
    expect(wallpaper).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it.each(textTokens)('%s has 4.5:1 on all window backgrounds', (token) => {
    const fg = color(tokens, token);
    for (const bg of backgrounds) {
      const ratio = contrastRatio(fg, bg.color);
      expect(ratio, `${token} on ${bg.name}: ${ratio.toFixed(2)}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
    }
  });

  it.each(['--os-accent-fill', '--os-danger-fill'])('--os-on-fill has 4.5:1 on %s', (fill) => {
    const ratio = contrastRatio(color(tokens, '--os-on-fill'), color(tokens, fill));
    expect(ratio, ratio.toFixed(2)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
  });

  it.each(SYSTEM_COLORS)('the accessible %s (dot border) has 3:1 on the surface', (name) => {
    const ratio = contrastRatio(color(tokens, `--os-${name}-accessible`), surface);
    expect(ratio, ratio.toFixed(2)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
  });

  it('the focus ring (--os-accent) has 3:1 on the surface', () => {
    const ratio = contrastRatio(color(tokens, '--os-accent'), surface);
    expect(ratio, ratio.toFixed(2)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
  });

  // Pairs that the components add (plan 07, "Spec deviations").
  it('table header text has 4.5:1 on the header background', () => {
    const header = color(tokens, theme === 'light' ? '--os-gray-6' : '--os-gray-5');
    const ratio = contrastRatio(color(tokens, '--os-gray-accessible'), header);
    expect(ratio, ratio.toFixed(2)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
  });

  it.each(['blue', 'orange'])('%s banner text has 4.5:1 on the banner background', (name) => {
    const background = over(color(tokens, `--os-${name}`), surface, 0.12);
    const ratio = contrastRatio(color(tokens, `--os-${name}-accessible`), background);
    expect(ratio, ratio.toFixed(2)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);

});
```

`packages/web/src/theme/tokens.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parseThemeMapping, parseThemeTokens } from './css-tokens';

// Read the files that the browser build uses, so that the test and the CSS cannot differ.
const tokensCss = readFileSync(join(import.meta.dirname, 'tokens.css'), 'utf8');
const styleMd = readFileSync(join(import.meta.dirname, '../../../../STYLE.md'), 'utf8');

const SYSTEM_COLORS = [
  'red',
  'orange',
  'yellow',
  'green',
  'mint',
  'teal',
  'cyan',
  'blue',
  'indigo',
  'purple',
  'pink',
  'brown',
  'gray',
] as const;

const themes = parseThemeTokens(tokensCss);
const mapping = parseThemeMapping(tokensCss);

/** The rows of the token table under a STYLE.md heading, as the first code span of each cell. */
function tableRows(heading: string): string[][] {
  const start = styleMd.indexOf(heading);
  if (start < 0) throw new Error(`STYLE.md has no heading ${heading}`);
  const end = styleMd.indexOf('\n### ', start + heading.length);
  const section = styleMd.slice(start, end < 0 ? undefined : end);
  return section
    .split('\n')
    .filter((line) => line.startsWith('| `--os-'))
    .map((line) =>
      line
        .split('|')
        .slice(1, -1)
        .map((cell) => (/`([^`]+)`/.exec(cell)?.[1] ?? cell).trim().toLowerCase()),
    );
}

describe('tokens.css', () => {
  it('defines every token that STYLE.md names, and no other token', () => {
    const documented = new Set([...styleMd.matchAll(/`(--os-[a-z0-9-]+)`/g)].map((m) => m[1]));
    // STYLE.md section 2.1: "The accessible token of each color is `--os-<color>-accessible`".
    for (const name of SYSTEM_COLORS) documented.add(`--os-${name}-accessible`);
    const defined = new Set(themes.light.keys());
    expect([...documented].filter((name) => name !== undefined && !defined.has(name))).toEqual([]);
    expect([...defined].filter((name) => !documented.has(name))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('has the system color values of STYLE.md section 2.1', () => {
    const rows = tableRows('### 2.1 System colors');
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(SYSTEM_COLORS.length);
    for (const [token = '', light, dark, accessibleLight, accessibleDark] of rows) {
      expect(themes.light.get(token), token).toBe(light);
      expect(themes.dark.get(token), token).toBe(dark);
      expect(themes.light.get(`${token}-accessible`), token).toBe(accessibleLight);
      expect(themes.dark.get(`${token}-accessible`), token).toBe(accessibleDark);
    }
  });

  it('has the surface values of STYLE.md section 2.2', () => {
    const rows = tableRows('### 2.2 Surface tokens');
    expect(rows.length).toBeGreaterThan(15);
    for (const [token = '', light, dark] of rows) {
      expect(themes.light.get(token), token).toBe(light);
      expect(themes.dark.get(token), token).toBe(dark);
    }
  });

  it('has the wallpaper and traffic light values of STYLE.md section 2.3', () => {
    for (const [token = '', light, dark] of tableRows('### 2.3 Wallpaper and traffic lights')) {
      expect(themes.light.get(token), token).toBe(light);
      expect(themes.dark.get(token), token).toBe(dark === 'same' ? light : dark);
    }
  });

  it('maps the tokens for Tailwind (STYLE.md section 9)', () => {
    for (const name of SYSTEM_COLORS) {
      expect(mapping.get(`--color-os-${name}`)).toBe(`var(--os-${name})`);
      expect(mapping.get(`--color-os-${name}-accessible`)).toBe(`var(--os-${name}-accessible)`);
    }
    for (const [key, value] of mapping) {
      const ref = /^var\((--os-[a-z0-9-]+)\)$/.exec(value)?.[1];
      if (ref) expect(themes.light.has(ref), `${key} → ${ref}`).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(mapping.get('--color-os-surface')).toBe('var(--os-surface)');
    expect(mapping.get('--text-os-base')).toBe('13px');
    expect(mapping.get('--text-os-base--line-height')).toBe('18px');
    expect(mapping.get('--shadow-os-window')).toBe('var(--os-shadow-window)');
    expect(mapping.get('--font-sans')).toBe('var(--os-font-sans)');
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/theme/css-tokens.ts`:

```ts
/** Token values of one theme: CSS custom property name → raw value. */
export type TokenMap = ReadonlyMap<string, string>;

export interface ThemeTokens {
  light: TokenMap;
  dark: TokenMap;
}

function declarations(block: string): Map<string, string> {
  const map = new Map<string, string>();
  const withoutComments = block.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '');
  for (const match of withoutComments.matchAll(/(--[a-z0-9-]+)\s*:\s*([^;]+);/gi)) {
    const [, name, value] = match;
    if (name && value) map.set(name, value.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim());
  }
  return map;
}

function blockAfter(css: string, start: number): string {
  const open = css.indexOf('{', start);
  if (open < 0) throw new Error(`No block after offset ${String(start)}`);
  let depth = 0;
  for (let i = open; i < css.length; i += 1) {
    if (css[i] === '{') depth += 1;
    if (css[i] === '}') {
      depth -= 1;
      if (depth === 0) return css.slice(open + 1, i);
    }
  }
  throw new Error(`Unclosed block at offset ${String(open)}`);
}

/**
 * Reads tokens.css: the first `:root { … }` block gives the light values, and the
 * `:root` block inside `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` overrides them for dark.
 */
export function parseThemeTokens(css: string): ThemeTokens {
  const rootStart = css.search(/(^|\n):root\s*\{/);
  if (rootStart < 0) throw new Error('tokens.css has no :root block');
  const light = declarations(blockAfter(css, rootStart));
  const mediaStart = css.search(/@media\s*\(prefers-color-scheme:\s*dark\)/);
  if (mediaStart < 0) throw new Error('tokens.css has no dark block');
  const media = blockAfter(css, mediaStart);
  const darkRoot = media.search(/:root\s*\{/);
  if (darkRoot < 0) throw new Error('The dark block has no :root block');
  const dark = new Map(light);
  for (const [name, value] of declarations(blockAfter(media, darkRoot))) dark.set(name, value);
  return { light, dark };
}

/** Reads the `@theme inline { … }` block. */
export function parseThemeMapping(css: string): TokenMap {
  const start = css.search(/@theme\s+inline\s*\{/);
  if (start < 0) throw new Error('tokens.css has no @theme inline block');
  return declarations(blockAfter(css, start));
}
```

`packages/web/src/theme/contrast.ts`:

```ts
/** An sRGB color with channels 0–255 and alpha 0–1. */
export interface Rgba {
  r: number;
  g: number;
  b: number;
  a: number;
}

/** Parses `#rgb`, `#rrggbb`, and `rgb(r g b / a%)`. */
export function parseColor(value: string): Rgba {
  const v = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const hex = /^#([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/.exec(v);
  if (hex?.[1]) {
    const h = hex[1].length === 3 ? hex[1].replace(/./g, '$&$&') : hex[1];
    return {
      r: parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16),
      g: parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16),
      b: parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16),
      a: 1,
    };
  }
  const rgb = /^rgb\(\s*(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s*(?:\/\s*([\d.]+)(%?)\s*)?\)$/.exec(v);
  if (rgb) {
    const alpha = rgb[4] === undefined ? 1 : Number(rgb[4]) / (rgb[5] === '%' ? 100 : 1);
    return { r: Number(rgb[1]), g: Number(rgb[2]), b: Number(rgb[3]), a: alpha };
  }
  throw new Error(`Unsupported color: ${value}`);
}

/** Puts `top` with opacity `alpha` over the opaque color `bottom`. */
export function over(top: Rgba, bottom: Rgba, alpha = top.a): Rgba {
  return {
    r: top.r * alpha + bottom.r * (1 - alpha),
    g: top.g * alpha + bottom.g * (1 - alpha),
    b: top.b * alpha + bottom.b * (1 - alpha),
    a: 1,
  };
}

function channel(value: number): number {
  const c = value / 255;
  return c <= 0.04045 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
}

/** WCAG 2.x relative luminance. */
export function luminance(color: Rgba): number {
  return 0.2126 * channel(color.r) + 0.7152 * channel(color.g) + 0.0722 * channel(color.b);
}

/** WCAG 2.x contrast ratio, 1 to 21. */
export function contrastRatio(a: Rgba, b: Rgba): number {
  const [hi, lo] = [luminance(a), luminance(b)].sort((x, y) => y - x) as [number, number];
  return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05);
}

/** The color stops of a `linear-gradient(…)` value. */
export function gradientColors(value: string): Rgba[] {
  return [...value.matchAll(/#[0-9a-f]{3,6}\b|rgb\([^)]*\)/gi)].map((m) => parseColor(m[0]));
}
```

```css
 * Design tokens of STYLE.md section 2. Light values in :root, dark values in the
 * prefers-color-scheme block. contrast.test.ts and tokens.test.ts read this file.
 * Add a token here only together with STYLE.md (AGENTS.md rule 3).

:root {
  color-scheme: light dark;

  --os-red: #ff3b30;
  --os-red-accessible: #d70015;
  --os-orange: #ff9500;
  --os-orange-accessible: #c93400;
```

### Task 3: Controls: Button, IconButton, TextField, Switch, Checkbox, RadioGroup

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/components/os/cx.ts`, `packages/web/src/components/os/icon.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/Button.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/TextField.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/Switch.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/Checkbox.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/RadioGroup.tsx`
- Test: `packages/web/src/components/os/Button.test.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/TextField.test.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/Switch.test.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/Checkbox.test.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/RadioGroup.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (the utilities of the mapping).
- Produces: `cx(...parts)`; `ICON_STROKE = 1.75`, `OsIcon({ icon, size?: 12 | 14 | 16 | 18, className? })`; `Button(props: ComponentPropsWithRef<'button'> & { variant?: 'default' | 'primary' | 'destructive' | 'ghost'; size?: 'default' | 'small' })` (type `button` by default); `IconButton` (same props, `aria-label: string` required, small and ghost by default); `INPUT_CLASS`; `TextField({ label, error?, hint?, ...input props })`; `Switch({ label, checked, onCheckedChange, disabled? })`; `Checkbox({ label, checked, onCheckedChange, disabled? })`; `RadioGroup<T extends string>({ label, value, options: readonly { value: T; label: string }[], onValueChange, disabled?, orientation? })`.

The controls of STYLE.md section 5. Each one is a Radix primitive or a native element with the token classes. Icons are decorative (`aria-hidden`); icon-only buttons need an `aria-label` (the type requires it).

`packages/web/src/components/os/Button.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { IconPlus } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Button, IconButton } from './Button';
import { OsIcon } from './icon';

describe('Button', () => {
  it('is a type="button" button that calls onClick', async () => {
    const onClick = vi.fn();
    render(<Button onClick={onClick}>Start</Button>);
    const button = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start' });
    expect(button).toHaveAttribute('type', 'button');
    await userEvent.click(button);
    expect(onClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('uses the fill tokens for primary and destructive buttons', () => {
    render(
      <>
        <Button variant="primary">Create</Button>
        <Button variant="destructive">Delete</Button>
      </>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' })).toHaveClass(
      'bg-os-accent-fill',
      'text-os-on-fill',
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete' })).toHaveClass(
      'bg-os-danger-fill',
      'text-os-on-fill',
    );
  });

  it('does not call onClick when disabled', async () => {
    const onClick = vi.fn();
    render(
      <Button disabled onClick={onClick}>
        Stop
      </Button>,
    );
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Stop' }));
    expect(onClick).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('works with the keyboard', async () => {
    const onClick = vi.fn();
    render(<Button onClick={onClick}>Restart</Button>);
    await userEvent.tab();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Restart' })).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Enter}');
    expect(onClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

describe('IconButton', () => {
  it('takes its accessible name from aria-label and hides the icon', () => {
    render(
      <IconButton aria-label="New session">
        <OsIcon icon={IconPlus} />
      </IconButton>,
    );
    const button = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'New session' });
    expect(button.querySelector('svg')).toHaveAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    expect(button).toHaveClass('h-(--os-control-height-sm)', 'w-(--os-control-height-sm)');
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/TextField.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { TextField } from './TextField';

describe('TextField', () => {
  it('connects the label to the input', async () => {
    render(<TextField label="Email" />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Email');
    await userEvent.type(input, 'ana@example.com');
    expect(input).toHaveValue('ana@example.com');
  });

  it('marks the input invalid and describes it with the error', () => {
    render(<TextField label="Name" error="Use 1 to 64 characters" hint="Shown in the sidebar" />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Name');
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
    expect(input).toHaveAccessibleDescription('Use 1 to 64 characters');
    expect(screen.queryByText('Shown in the sidebar')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the hint when there is no error', () => {
    render(<TextField label="Timezone" hint="IANA name" />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Timezone')).toHaveAccessibleDescription('IANA name');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Timezone')).not.toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { Switch } from './Switch';

function Harness() {
  const [on, setOn] = useState(true);
  return <Switch label="Start now" checked={on} onCheckedChange={setOn} />;
}

describe('Switch', () => {
  it('is a labelled switch that toggles with a click and with Space', async () => {
    render(<Harness />);
    const control = screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Start now' });
    expect(control).toBeChecked();
    await userEvent.click(control);
    expect(control).not.toBeChecked();
    control.focus();
    await userEvent.keyboard(' ');
    expect(control).toBeChecked();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RadioGroup } from './RadioGroup';

const OPTIONS = [
  { value: '1280x720', label: '1280 × 720' },
  { value: '1600x900', label: '1600 × 900' },
  { value: '1920x1080', label: '1920 × 1080' },
] as const;

function Harness() {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<(typeof OPTIONS)[number]['value']>('1920x1080');
  return (
    <>
      <RadioGroup label="Screen" value={value} options={OPTIONS} onValueChange={setValue} />
      <output>{value}</output>
    </>
  );
}

describe('RadioGroup', () => {
  it('is a labelled radio group that works with clicks and the keyboard', async () => {
    render(<Harness />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('radiogroup', { name: 'Screen' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: '1920 × 1080' })).toBeChecked();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: '1280 × 720' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('1280x720');
    await userEvent.keyboard('{ArrowRight}');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: '1600 × 900' })).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.keyboard(' ');
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('1600x900');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the components**

The hover color of the fills is the formula of STYLE.md (`color-mix(in oklab, <fill> 92%, black)`) with the token `--os-black`. The switch track is `--os-gray-4` in light and `--os-gray-3` in dark (`dark:` follows `prefers-color-scheme` in Tailwind v4).

`packages/web/src/components/os/cx.ts`:

```ts
/** Joins class names and drops empty parts. */
export function cx(...parts: (string | false | null | undefined)[]): string {
  return parts.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
}
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/icon.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { TablerIcon } from '@tabler/icons-react';

/** Stroke width of all Tabler icons (STYLE.md section 6). */
export const ICON_STROKE = 1.75;

export interface OsIconProps {
  icon: TablerIcon;
  /** 16px in controls, 18px in the toolbar and the sidebar, 14px for the agent icon. */
  size?: 12 | 14 | 16 | 18;
  className?: string;
}

/** A decorative icon. Screen readers ignore it, so the control around it needs a label. */
export function OsIcon({ icon: Icon, size = 16, className }: OsIconProps) {
  return (
    <Icon
      size={size}
      stroke={ICON_STROKE}
      aria-hidden="true"
      focusable="false"
      className={className}
    />
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/Button.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ComponentPropsWithRef } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';

export type ButtonVariant = 'default' | 'primary' | 'destructive' | 'ghost';
export type ButtonSize = 'default' | 'small';

const BASE =
  'inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-os-control px-3 text-os-base font-medium whitespace-nowrap select-none transition-colors duration-(--os-duration-fast) ease-os-out disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-40';

const VARIANTS: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
  default: 'bg-os-tone text-os-foreground hover:bg-os-tone-strong active:bg-os-tone-strong',
  primary:
    'bg-os-accent-fill text-os-on-fill hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--os-accent-fill)_92%,var(--os-black))]',
  destructive:
    'bg-os-danger-fill text-os-on-fill hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--os-danger-fill)_92%,var(--os-black))]',
  ghost: 'bg-transparent text-os-foreground hover:bg-os-tone active:bg-os-tone-strong',
};

const SIZES: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  default: 'h-(--os-control-height)',
  small: 'h-(--os-control-height-sm)',
};

export interface ButtonProps extends ComponentPropsWithRef<'button'> {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
}

export function Button({
  variant = 'default',
  size = 'default',
  type = 'button',
  className,
  ...props
}: ButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      className={cx(BASE, VARIANTS[variant], SIZES[size], className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export interface IconButtonProps extends Omit<ButtonProps, 'aria-label'> {
  /** Icon-only buttons need a label (STYLE.md section 3). */
  'aria-label': string;
}

/** A square button that holds one icon. Small (24px) by default. */
export function IconButton({
  size = 'small',
  variant = 'ghost',
  className,
  ...props
}: IconButtonProps) {
  return (
    <Button
      size={size}
      variant={variant}
      className={cx(
        'px-0',
        size === 'small' ? 'w-(--os-control-height-sm)' : 'w-(--os-control-height)',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}
```

```tsx
/** Classes of the text input of STYLE.md section 5. */
export const INPUT_CLASS =
  'h-(--os-control-height) w-full min-w-0 rounded-os-control border border-os-separator bg-os-surface px-2 text-os-base text-os-foreground placeholder:text-os-gray-accessible disabled:opacity-40 aria-invalid:border-os-red-accessible';

export interface TextFieldProps extends Omit<ComponentPropsWithRef<'input'>, 'id'> {
  label: string;
  /** Error text under the input. It replaces the hint. */
  error?: string | null | undefined;
  hint?: string | undefined;
}

export function TextField({ label, error, hint, className, ...props }: TextFieldProps) {
  const id = useId();
  const messageId = `${id}-message`;
  const message = error ?? hint;
  return (
    <div className={cx('flex flex-col gap-1', className)}>
      <label htmlFor={id} className="text-os-base text-os-foreground">
        {label}
      </label>
      <input
        id={id}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={message ? messageId : undefined}
        className={INPUT_CLASS}
        {...props}
      />
      {message ? (
        <p
          id={messageId}
          className={cx('text-os-xs', error ? 'text-os-red-accessible' : 'text-os-gray-accessible')}
        >
          {message}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import * as SwitchPrimitive from '@radix-ui/react-switch';
import { useId } from 'react';

export interface SwitchProps {
  label: string;
  checked: boolean;
  onCheckedChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function Switch({ label, checked, onCheckedChange, disabled = false }: SwitchProps) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <SwitchPrimitive.Root
        id={id}
        checked={checked}
        onCheckedChange={onCheckedChange}
        disabled={disabled}
        className="group relative inline-flex h-5 w-9 shrink-0 items-center rounded-full bg-os-gray-4 transition-colors duration-(--os-duration-fast) ease-os-out disabled:opacity-40 data-[state=checked]:bg-os-accent dark:bg-os-gray-3"
      >
        <SwitchPrimitive.Thumb className="block size-4 translate-x-0.5 rounded-full bg-os-on-fill shadow-os-control transition-[translate] duration-(--os-duration-fast) ease-os-out data-[state=checked]:translate-x-4.5 motion-reduce:transition-none" />
      </SwitchPrimitive.Root>
      <label htmlFor={id} className="text-os-base text-os-foreground">
        {label}
      </label>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import * as CheckboxPrimitive from '@radix-ui/react-checkbox';
import { IconCheck } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useId } from 'react';
import { OsIcon } from './icon';

export interface CheckboxProps {
  label: string;
  checked: boolean;
  onCheckedChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function Checkbox({ label, checked, onCheckedChange, disabled = false }: CheckboxProps) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <CheckboxPrimitive.Root
        id={id}
        checked={checked}
        onCheckedChange={(value) => {
          onCheckedChange(value === true);
        }}
        disabled={disabled}
        className="flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-os-checkbox border border-os-gray-2 bg-os-surface disabled:opacity-40 data-[state=checked]:border-os-accent-fill data-[state=checked]:bg-os-accent-fill"
      >
        <CheckboxPrimitive.Indicator className="text-os-on-fill">
          <OsIcon icon={IconCheck} size={12} />
        </CheckboxPrimitive.Indicator>
      </CheckboxPrimitive.Root>
      <label htmlFor={id} className="text-os-base text-os-foreground">
        {label}
      </label>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/RadioGroup.tsx`:

```tsx
import * as RadioGroupPrimitive from '@radix-ui/react-radio-group';
import { useId } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';

export interface RadioOption<T extends string> {
  value: T;
  label: string;
}

export interface RadioGroupProps<T extends string> {
  label: string;
  value: T;
  options: readonly RadioOption<T>[];
  onValueChange: (value: T) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  orientation?: 'horizontal' | 'vertical';
}

export function RadioGroup<T extends string>({
  label,
  value,
  options,
  onValueChange,
  disabled = false,
  orientation = 'horizontal',
}: RadioGroupProps<T>) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <span id={`${id}-label`} className="text-os-base text-os-foreground">
        {label}
      </span>
      <RadioGroupPrimitive.Root
        aria-labelledby={`${id}-label`}
        value={value}
        onValueChange={(next) => {
          const option = options.find((o) => o.value === next);
          if (option) onValueChange(option.value);
        }}
        disabled={disabled}
        orientation={orientation}
        className={cx(
          'flex gap-x-4 gap-y-2',
          orientation === 'vertical' ? 'flex-col' : 'flex-row flex-wrap',
        )}
      >
        {options.map((option) => (
          <div key={option.value} className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <RadioGroupPrimitive.Item
              id={`${id}-${option.value}`}
              value={option.value}
              className="flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-os-gray-2 bg-os-surface disabled:opacity-40 data-[state=checked]:border-os-accent-fill data-[state=checked]:bg-os-accent-fill"
            >
              <RadioGroupPrimitive.Indicator className="block size-1.5 rounded-full bg-os-on-fill" />
            </RadioGroupPrimitive.Item>
            <label htmlFor={`${id}-${option.value}`} className="text-os-base text-os-foreground">
              {option.label}
            </label>
          </div>
        ))}
      </RadioGroupPrimitive.Root>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- Consumes: Task 3 (`cx`, `OsIcon`, `IconButton`), `SessionState` and `SESSION_STATES` of `@hydra/shared`.

A status dot never carries the meaning alone: it has a visually hidden text with the state (STYLE.md section 3). The banner background is `color-mix(in srgb, <color> 12%, var(--os-surface))`. The table header uses `--os-gray-6` in light (see "Spec deviations" 1).

```tsx
import { SESSION_STATES } from '@hydra/shared';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SESSION_STATE_LABELS, StatusDot } from './StatusDot';

describe('StatusDot', () => {
  it.each(SESSION_STATES)('has a visually hidden text for %s', (state) => {
    render(<StatusDot state={state} />);
    const text = screen.getByText(SESSION_STATE_LABELS[state]);

    const dot = () => container.querySelector('[data-state]');

    expect(dot()).toHaveAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    rerender(<StatusDot state="starting" />);

    rerender(<StatusDot state="stopping" />);
    expect(dot()).toHaveClass('bg-os-yellow');

  it('can show the state name', () => {

});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Table, type Column } from './Table';

interface Row {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  calls: number;
}

const COLUMNS: Column<Row>[] = [
  { key: 'name', header: 'Name', cell: (row) => row.name },
  { key: 'calls', header: 'Calls', cell: (row) => row.calls, align: 'right' },
];

describe('Table', () => {
  it('renders a labelled table with column headers and rows', () => {
    render(
      <Table
        label="Tokens"
        columns={COLUMNS}
        rows={[
          { id: 'a', name: 'ci', calls: 12 },
          { id: 'b', name: 'laptop', calls: 3 },
        ]}
        rowKey={(row) => row.id}
      />,
    );
    const table = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Tokens' });
    expect(
      within(table)
        .getAllByRole('columnheader')
        .map((th) => th.textContent),
    ).toEqual(['Name', 'Calls']);
    expect(within(table).getAllByRole('row')).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(screen.getByRole('cell', { name: '12' })).toHaveClass('text-right');
  });

  it('shows the empty text', () => {
    render(
      <Table
        label="Tokens"
        columns={COLUMNS}
        rows={[]}
        rowKey={(row) => row.id}
        empty="No tokens"
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('cell', { name: 'No tokens' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('reports row clicks', async () => {
    const onRowClick = vi.fn();
    render(
      <Table
        label="Events"
        columns={COLUMNS}
        rows={[{ id: 'a', name: 'ci', calls: 1 }]}
        rowKey={(row) => row.id}
        onRowClick={onRowClick}
      />,
    );
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('cell', { name: 'ci' }));
    expect(onRowClick).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ id: 'a', name: 'ci', calls: 1 });
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/StatusDot.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { SessionState } from '@hydra/shared';
import { cx } from './cx';

/** Visible names of the session states. */
export const SESSION_STATE_LABELS: Record<SessionState, string> = {
  stopped: 'Stopped',
  starting: 'Starting',
  running: 'Running',
  stopping: 'Stopping',
  error: 'Error',
  deleting: 'Deleting',
  deleted: 'Deleted',
};

const DOT_CLASSES: Record<SessionState, string> = {
  running: 'bg-os-green border-os-green-accessible',
  starting: 'bg-os-yellow border-os-yellow-accessible',
  stopping: 'bg-os-yellow border-os-yellow-accessible',
  stopped: 'bg-os-gray border-os-gray-accessible',
  error: 'bg-os-red border-os-red-accessible',
  deleting: 'bg-os-gray border-os-gray-accessible',
  deleted: 'bg-os-gray border-os-gray-accessible',
};

export interface StatusDotProps {
  state: SessionState;
  /** Shows the state name next to the dot. Without it, the name is visually hidden. */
  showLabel?: boolean;
}

export function StatusDot({ state, showLabel = false }: StatusDotProps) {
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        data-state={state}
        className={cx('size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full border', DOT_CLASSES[state])}
      />
      <span className={showLabel ? undefined : 'sr-only'}>{SESSION_STATE_LABELS[state]}</span>
    </span>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/Banner.tsx`:

```tsx
import { IconAlertTriangle, IconInfoCircle } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { OsIcon } from './icon';

export type BannerVariant = 'info' | 'warning';

const VARIANTS = {
  info: {
    className:
      'text-os-blue-accessible bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--os-blue)_12%,var(--os-surface))]',
    icon: IconInfoCircle,
  },
  warning: {
    className:
      'text-os-orange-accessible bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--os-orange)_12%,var(--os-surface))]',
    icon: IconAlertTriangle,
  },
} as const;

export interface BannerProps {
  variant: BannerVariant;
  children: ReactNode;
  /** `alert` for errors that need attention now. Default `status`. */
  role?: 'status' | 'alert';
  className?: string;
}

export function Banner({ variant, children, role = 'status', className }: BannerProps) {
  const style = VARIANTS[variant];
  return (
    <div
      role={role}
      className={cx(
        'flex items-center gap-2 rounded-os-menu px-3 py-2 text-os-base',
        style.className,
        className,
      )}
    >
      <OsIcon icon={style.icon} size={16} className="shrink-0" />
      <div className="min-w-0">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/CodeBlock.tsx`:

```tsx
import { IconCheck, IconCopy } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { IconButton } from './Button';
import { OsIcon } from './icon';

export interface CodeBlockProps {
  code: string;
  /** Accessible name of the block, for example "Claude Code command". */
  label: string;
}

export function CodeBlock({ code, label }: CodeBlockProps) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  function copy() {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(code).then(
      () => {
        setCopied(true);
      },
      () => {
        setCopied(false);
      },
    );
  }

  return (
    <figure aria-label={label} className="relative rounded-os-control bg-os-gray-6">
      <pre className="overflow-x-auto py-2 pr-10 pl-3 font-mono text-os-sm break-all whitespace-pre-wrap text-os-foreground">
        <code>{code}</code>
      </pre>
      <IconButton aria-label="Copy" onClick={copy} className="absolute top-1 right-1">
        <OsIcon icon={copied ? IconCheck : IconCopy} />
      </IconButton>
      <span role="status" className="sr-only">
        {copied ? 'Copied' : ''}
      </span>
    </figure>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/Table.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';

export interface Column<T> {
  key: string;
  header: string;
  cell: (row: T) => ReactNode;
  /** Numbers align right (STYLE.md section 5). */
  align?: 'left' | 'right';
  className?: string;
}

export interface TableProps<T> {
  /** Accessible name of the table. */
  label: string;
  columns: readonly Column<T>[];
  rows: readonly T[];
  rowKey: (row: T) => string;
  /** Shown in one row when `rows` is empty. */
  empty?: ReactNode;
  onRowClick?: (row: T) => void;
}

export function Table<T>({ label, columns, rows, rowKey, empty, onRowClick }: TableProps<T>) {
  return (
    <table aria-label={label} className="w-full border-collapse text-os-base text-os-foreground">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          {columns.map((column) => (
            <th
              key={column.key}
              scope="col"
              className={cx(
                'h-(--os-table-row-height) bg-os-gray-6 px-3 text-os-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap text-os-gray-accessible dark:bg-os-gray-5',
                column.align === 'right' ? 'text-right' : 'text-left',
              )}
            >
              {column.header}
            </th>
          ))}
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {rows.length === 0 ? (
          <tr className="border-t border-os-separator">
            <td colSpan={columns.length} className="px-3 py-6 text-center text-os-gray-accessible">
              {empty ?? 'Nothing here yet.'}
            </td>
          </tr>
        ) : (
          rows.map((row) => (
            <tr
              key={rowKey(row)}
              onClick={
                onRowClick
                  ? () => {
                      onRowClick(row);
                    }
                  : undefined
              }
              className={cx(
                'h-(--os-table-row-height) border-t border-os-separator hover:bg-os-tone',
                onRowClick && 'cursor-default',
              )}
            >
              {columns.map((column) => (
                <td
                  key={column.key}
                  className={cx(
                    'px-3 py-1',
                    column.align === 'right' && 'text-right tabular-nums',
                    column.className,
                  )}
                >
                  {column.cell(row)}
                </td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))
        )}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}
```

### Task 5: Overlays and editors: SegmentedControl, Sheet, Menu, ChipInput

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/components/os/SegmentedControl.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/Sheet.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/Menu.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/ChipInput.tsx`
- Test: `packages/web/src/components/os/SegmentedControl.test.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/Sheet.test.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/Menu.test.tsx`, `packages/web/src/components/os/ChipInput.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 (`cx`, `OsIcon`, `Button`, `IconButton`).
- Produces: `SegmentedControl<T extends string>({ label, value, segments: readonly { value: T; label: string }[], onValueChange })` (Radix ToggleGroup, a click on the selected segment keeps it); `Sheet({ open, onOpenChange, title, description?, wide?, returnFocusRef?, children })`, `SheetBody`, `SheetFooter`; `Menu`, `MenuTrigger` (Radix `Root`, `Trigger`), `MenuContent({ children, align?, side? })`, `MenuItem({ children, onSelect, destructive?, disabled? })`, `MenuLabel`, `MenuSeparator`, `MenuRadioGroup<T>({ label, value, options, onValueChange })`; `ChipInput({ label, value, onChange, placeholder?, hint?, error?, disabled? })`.

The tests pin the keyboard rules of STYLE.md section 3: arrow keys in the segmented control, Esc closes a sheet or a menu, and the focus goes back to the control that opened it (also when a menu item opened the sheet: `returnFocusRef`).

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SegmentedControl } from './SegmentedControl';

const VIEWS = [
  { value: 'live', label: 'Live' },
  { value: 'tokens', label: 'Tokens' },
  { value: 'settings', label: 'Settings' },
] as const;

function Harness() {
  const [view, setView] = useState<(typeof VIEWS)[number]['value']>('live');
  return (
    <>
      <SegmentedControl label="Views" value={view} segments={VIEWS} onValueChange={setView} />
      <output>{view}</output>
    </>
  );
}

describe('SegmentedControl', () => {
  it('is a labelled radio group with the selected segment checked', () => {
    render(<Harness />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('radiogroup', { name: 'Views' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Live' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Tokens' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'false');
  });

  it('moves the focus with the arrow keys and selects with Enter', async () => {
    render(<Harness />);
    await userEvent.tab();
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Live' })).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{ArrowRight}');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Tokens' })).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{ArrowRight}');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Settings' })).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{ArrowRight}');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Live' })).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{ArrowLeft}{Enter}');
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('settings');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Settings' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');
  });

  it('keeps the selection when the selected segment is clicked again', async () => {
    render(<Harness />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Live' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('live');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Live' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/Sheet.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { Sheet, SheetBody, SheetFooter } from './Sheet';

function Harness({ useReturnRef = false }: { useReturnRef?: boolean }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const other = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  return (
    <>
      <Button
        onClick={() => {
          setOpen(true);
        }}
      >
        New token
      </Button>
      <Button ref={other}>More</Button>
      <Sheet
        open={open}
        onOpenChange={setOpen}
        title="New token"
        description="The token permits the MCP endpoint of this session."
        {...(useReturnRef ? { returnFocusRef: other } : {})}
      >
        <SheetBody>
          <input aria-label="Name" />
        </SheetBody>
        <SheetFooter>
          <Button
            onClick={() => {
              setOpen(false);
            }}
          >
            Cancel
          </Button>
        </SheetFooter>
      </Sheet>
    </>
  );
}

describe('Sheet', () => {
  it('opens a modal dialog named by its title and described by its description', async () => {
    render(<Harness />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'New token' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'New token' });
    expect(dialog).toHaveAccessibleDescription(
      'The token permits the MCP endpoint of this session.',
    );
    expect(dialog).toHaveClass('w-120', 'rounded-b-xl');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Name')).toHaveFocus();
  });

  it('closes with Esc and returns the focus to the opener', async () => {
    render(<Harness />);
    const opener = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'New token' });
    await userEvent.click(opener);
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    });
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(opener).toHaveFocus();
    });
  });

  it('closes with Cancel', async () => {
    render(<Harness />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'New token' }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    });
  });

  it('can return the focus to another control', async () => {
    render(<Harness useReturnRef />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'New token' }));
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'More' })).toHaveFocus();
    });
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { IconDots } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { IconButton } from './Button';
import { OsIcon } from './icon';
import { Menu, MenuContent, MenuItem, MenuRadioGroup, MenuTrigger } from './Menu';

function MoreMenu({ onDelete }: { onDelete: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Menu>
      <MenuTrigger asChild>
        <IconButton aria-label="More">
          <OsIcon icon={IconDots} />
        </IconButton>
      </MenuTrigger>
      <MenuContent>
        <MenuItem onSelect={onDelete} destructive>
          Delete
        </MenuItem>
      </MenuContent>
    </Menu>
  );
}

function RoleMenu() {
  const [role, setRole] = useState<'viewer' | 'operator'>('viewer');
  return (
    <Menu>
      <MenuTrigger asChild>
        <button type="button">Role: {role}</button>
      </MenuTrigger>
      <MenuContent>
        <MenuRadioGroup
          label="Role"
          value={role}
          onValueChange={setRole}
          options={[
            { value: 'viewer', label: 'Viewer' },
            { value: 'operator', label: 'Operator' },
          ]}
        />
      </MenuContent>
    </Menu>
  );
}

describe('Menu', () => {
  it('opens with the keyboard and runs the selected item', async () => {
    const onDelete = vi.fn();
    render(<MoreMenu onDelete={onDelete} />);
    await userEvent.tab();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Enter}');
    const item = await screen.findByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Delete' });
    expect(item).toHaveClass('text-os-red-accessible');
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Enter}');
    expect(onDelete).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.queryByRole('menu')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    });
  });

  it('closes with Esc and returns the focus to the trigger', async () => {
    render(<MoreMenu onDelete={vi.fn()} />);
    const trigger = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'More' });
    await userEvent.click(trigger);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('menu')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.queryByRole('menu')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    });
    expect(trigger).toHaveFocus();
  });

  it('selects a radio item', async () => {
    render(<RoleMenu />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Role: viewer' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('menuitemradio', { name: 'Viewer' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'aria-checked',
      'true',
    );
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitemradio', { name: 'Operator' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Role: operator' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/ChipInput.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ChipInput } from './ChipInput';

function Harness({ initial = [] as string[] }) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string[]>(initial);
  return (
    <>
      <ChipInput label="Allowlist" value={value} onChange={setValue} />
      <output>{value.join('|')}</output>
    </>
  );
}

function chips() {
  return screen.getByRole('status').textContent;
}

describe('ChipInput', () => {
  it('adds a chip with Enter and with a comma', async () => {
    render(<Harness />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Allowlist');
    await userEvent.type(input, 'example.com{Enter}');
    expect(chips()).toBe('example.com');
    await userEvent.type(input, '.github.com,');
    expect(chips()).toBe('example.com|.github.com');
    expect(input).toHaveValue('');
    const list = screen.getByRole('list', { name: 'Allowlist entries' });
    expect(list.children).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('removes the last chip with Backspace in an empty input', async () => {
    render(<Harness initial={['a.com', 'b.com']} />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Allowlist');
    await userEvent.click(input);
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Backspace}');
    expect(chips()).toBe('a.com');
  });

  it('removes a chip with its remove button', async () => {
    render(<Harness initial={['a.com', 'b.com']} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove a.com' }));
    expect(chips()).toBe('b.com');
  });

  it('splits pasted text, ignores duplicates, and adds the rest on blur', () => {
    render(<Harness initial={['a.com']} />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Allowlist');
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: 'a.com, b.com, c.com' } });
    expect(chips()).toBe('a.com|b.com');
    expect(input).toHaveValue(' c.com');
    fireEvent.blur(input);
    expect(chips()).toBe('a.com|b.com|c.com');
  });

  it('does not submit the form when Enter adds a chip', async () => {
    let submitted = 0;
    render(
      <form
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          submitted += 1;
        }}
      >
        <Harness />
      </form>,
    );
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Allowlist'), 'x.com{Enter}');
    expect(submitted).toBe(0);
    expect(chips()).toBe('x.com');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the components**

The sheet moves down from the bottom of the toolbar: `translate-y` and opacity with `@starting-style` (`starting:`) on the way in, and `data-phase="closing"` on the way out. With `prefers-reduced-motion` only the opacity changes.

`packages/web/src/components/os/SegmentedControl.tsx`:

```tsx
import * as ToggleGroup from '@radix-ui/react-toggle-group';

export interface Segment<T extends string> {
  value: T;
  label: string;
}

export interface SegmentedControlProps<T extends string> {
  /** Accessible name of the group, for example "Views". */
  label: string;
  value: T;
  segments: readonly Segment<T>[];
  onValueChange: (value: T) => void;
}

/** Radix ToggleGroup (single). Arrow keys move the focus, Enter or Space selects. */
export function SegmentedControl<T extends string>({
  label,
  value,
  segments,
  onValueChange,
}: SegmentedControlProps<T>) {
  return (
    <ToggleGroup.Root
      type="single"
      aria-label={label}
      value={value}
      onValueChange={(next) => {
        const segment = segments.find((s) => s.value === next);
        if (segment) onValueChange(segment.value);
      }}
      className="inline-flex h-(--os-control-height) items-center gap-0.5 rounded-os-control bg-os-tone p-0.5"
    >
      {segments.map((segment) => (
        <ToggleGroup.Item
          key={segment.value}
          value={segment.value}
          className="h-full rounded-os-control px-3 text-os-base font-medium text-os-foreground transition-colors duration-(--os-duration-fast) ease-os-out data-[state=on]:bg-os-segment-selected data-[state=on]:shadow-os-control"
        >
          {segment.label}
        </ToggleGroup.Item>
      ))}
    </ToggleGroup.Root>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/components/os/Sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
import * as Dialog from '@radix-ui/react-dialog';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';

/** Reads `--os-duration-out` (STYLE.md section 2.7). 0 when the token is not loaded (tests). */
function exitDurationMs(): number {
  const raw = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement)
    .getPropertyValue('--os-duration-out')
    .trim();
  const value = Number.parseFloat(raw);
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return 0;
  return raw.endsWith('ms') ? value : value * 1000;
}

/** Keeps the sheet mounted while its exit transition runs. */
function useExitPresence(open: boolean): boolean {
  const [closing, setClosing] = useState(false);
  const [wasOpen, setWasOpen] = useState(open);
  if (wasOpen !== open) {
    setWasOpen(open);
    setClosing(!open);
  }
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!closing) return undefined;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setClosing(false);
    }, exitDurationMs());
    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [closing]);
  return open || closing;
}

export interface SheetProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  /** 560px instead of 480px (the token sheet). */
  wide?: boolean;
  /**
   * The control that gets the focus after the sheet closes. Use it when the opener is a menu
   * item. Without it, the focus goes back to the element that had the focus when the sheet
   * opened (Radix Dialog alone returns it only to a Dialog.Trigger).
   */
  returnFocusRef?: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>;
  children: ReactNode;
}

/** Radix Dialog that moves down from the bottom of the toolbar (STYLE.md section 5). */
export function Sheet({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  title,
  description,
  wide = false,
  returnFocusRef,
  children,
}: SheetProps) {
  const present = useExitPresence(open);
  const opener = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  const phase = open ? 'open' : 'closing';
  return (
    <Dialog.Root
      open={present}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (!next) onOpenChange(false);
      }}
    >
      <Dialog.Portal>
        <Dialog.Overlay
          data-phase={phase}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-(--os-z-overlay) bg-os-overlay opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-(--os-duration-base) ease-os-out starting:opacity-0 data-[phase=closing]:opacity-0 data-[phase=closing]:duration-(--os-duration-out) data-[phase=closing]:ease-os-in"
        />
        <Dialog.Content
          data-phase={phase}
          {...(description ? {} : { 'aria-describedby': undefined })}
          onOpenAutoFocus={() => {
            const active = document.activeElement;
            opener.current = active instanceof HTMLElement ? active : null;
          }}
          onCloseAutoFocus={(event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            (returnFocusRef?.current ?? opener.current)?.focus();
          }}
          className={cx(
            'fixed inset-x-0 top-[calc(var(--os-window-inset)+var(--os-toolbar-height))] z-(--os-z-sheet) mx-auto flex max-h-[calc(100vh-2*var(--os-window-inset)-var(--os-toolbar-height))] flex-col rounded-b-xl bg-os-surface text-os-foreground shadow-os-menu',
            'translate-y-0 opacity-100 transition-[translate,opacity] duration-(--os-duration-base) ease-os-out starting:-translate-y-2 starting:opacity-0',
            'data-[phase=closing]:-translate-y-2 data-[phase=closing]:opacity-0 data-[phase=closing]:duration-(--os-duration-out) data-[phase=closing]:ease-os-in',
            'motion-reduce:translate-y-0 motion-reduce:starting:translate-y-0 motion-reduce:data-[phase=closing]:translate-y-0',
            wide ? 'w-140' : 'w-120',
          )}
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 px-5 pt-4">
            <Dialog.Title className="text-os-base font-semibold">{title}</Dialog.Title>
            {description ? (
              <Dialog.Description className="text-os-base text-os-gray-accessible">
                {description}
              </Dialog.Description>
            ) : null}
          </div>
          {children}
        </Dialog.Content>
      </Dialog.Portal>
    </Dialog.Root>
  );
}

/** The scrolling middle part of a sheet. */
export function SheetBody({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto px-5 py-4">{children}</div>;
}

/** Buttons on the right: Cancel first, then the action (STYLE.md section 5). */
export function SheetFooter({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 px-5 pb-4">{children}</div>;
}
```

```tsx
import * as DropdownMenu from '@radix-ui/react-dropdown-menu';
import { IconCheck } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { OsIcon } from './icon';

export const Menu = DropdownMenu.Root;
export const MenuTrigger = DropdownMenu.Trigger;

const ITEM =
  'flex h-(--os-control-height-sm) cursor-default items-center gap-2 rounded-os-checkbox px-2 select-none data-disabled:opacity-40';

export interface MenuContentProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  align?: 'start' | 'center' | 'end';
  side?: 'top' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'left';
}

export function MenuContent({ children, align = 'end', side = 'bottom' }: MenuContentProps) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenu.Portal>
      <DropdownMenu.Content
        align={align}
        side={side}
        sideOffset={4}
        collisionPadding={8}
        className="z-(--os-z-menu) max-h-(--radix-dropdown-menu-content-available-height) min-w-40 overflow-y-auto rounded-os-menu bg-os-surface p-1 text-os-sm text-os-foreground shadow-os-menu"
      >
        {children}
      </DropdownMenu.Content>
    </DropdownMenu.Portal>
  );
}

export interface MenuItemProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  onSelect: () => void;
  destructive?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function MenuItem({
  children,
  onSelect,
  destructive = false,
  disabled = false,
}: MenuItemProps) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenu.Item
      disabled={disabled}
      onSelect={onSelect}
      className={cx(
        ITEM,
        destructive
          ? 'text-os-red-accessible data-highlighted:bg-os-danger-fill data-highlighted:text-os-on-fill'
          : 'data-highlighted:bg-os-accent-fill data-highlighted:text-os-on-fill',
      )}
    >
      {children}
    </DropdownMenu.Item>
  );
}

/** Text in a menu that is not an item, for example the email of the user. */
export function MenuLabel({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenu.Label className="px-2 py-1 text-os-xs text-os-gray-accessible">
      {children}
    </DropdownMenu.Label>
  );
}

export function MenuSeparator() {
  return <DropdownMenu.Separator className="my-1 h-px bg-os-separator" />;
}

export interface MenuRadioGroupProps<T extends string> {
  /** Accessible name of the group. */
  label: string;
  value: T;
  options: readonly { value: T; label: string }[];
  onValueChange: (value: T) => void;
}

export function MenuRadioGroup<T extends string>({
  label,
  value,
  options,
  onValueChange,
}: MenuRadioGroupProps<T>) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenu.RadioGroup
      aria-label={label}
      value={value}
      onValueChange={(next) => {
        const option = options.find((o) => o.value === next);
        if (option) onValueChange(option.value);
      }}
    >
      {options.map((option) => (
        <DropdownMenu.RadioItem
          key={option.value}
          value={option.value}
          className={cx(
            ITEM,
            'relative pl-6 data-highlighted:bg-os-accent-fill data-highlighted:text-os-on-fill',
          )}
        >
          <DropdownMenu.ItemIndicator className="absolute left-1.5">
            <OsIcon icon={IconCheck} size={12} />
          </DropdownMenu.ItemIndicator>
          {option.label}
        </DropdownMenu.RadioItem>
      ))}
    </DropdownMenu.RadioGroup>
  );
}
```
