`packages/web/src/components/os/ChipInput.tsx`:

```tsx
import { IconX } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useId, useState, type ChangeEvent, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { OsIcon } from './icon';

export interface ChipInputProps {
  label: string;
  value: readonly string[];
  onChange: (value: string[]) => void;
  placeholder?: string;
  hint?: string;
  error?: string | null | undefined;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

function addEntries(current: readonly string[], raw: string): string[] {
  const next = [...current];
  for (const part of raw.split(',')) {
    const entry = part.trim();
    if (entry !== '' && !next.includes(entry)) next.push(entry);
  }
  return next;
}

/**
 * The allowlist editor (STYLE.md section 5). Enter or "," adds a chip. Backspace in an empty
 * input removes the last chip. Leaving the input also adds the typed text.
 */
export function ChipInput({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder,
  hint,
  error,
  disabled = false,
}: ChipInputProps) {
  const id = useId();
  const messageId = `${id}-message`;
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
  const message = error ?? hint;

  function commit(raw: string) {
    const next = addEntries(value, raw);
    if (next.length !== value.length) onChange(next);
    setDraft('');
  }

  function handleChange(event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const text = event.target.value;
    const lastComma = text.lastIndexOf(',');
    if (lastComma < 0) {
      setDraft(text);
      return;
    }
    const next = addEntries(value, text.slice(0, lastComma));
    if (next.length !== value.length) onChange(next);
    setDraft(text.slice(lastComma + 1));
  }

  function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    if (event.key === 'Enter' && draft.trim() !== '') {
      event.preventDefault();
      commit(draft);
    } else if (event.key === 'Backspace' && draft === '' && value.length > 0) {
      event.preventDefault();
      onChange(value.slice(0, -1));
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <label htmlFor={id} className="text-os-base text-os-foreground">
        {label}
      </label>
      <div
        className={cx(
          'flex min-h-(--os-control-height) flex-wrap items-center gap-1 rounded-os-control border bg-os-surface px-1 py-0.5',
          error ? 'border-os-red-accessible' : 'border-os-separator',
          disabled && 'opacity-40',
        )}
      >
        <ul aria-label={`${label} entries`} className="contents">
          {value.map((entry) => (
            <li
              key={entry}
              className="flex h-5.5 items-center gap-1 rounded-os-control bg-os-tone pr-0.5 pl-2 text-os-sm"
            >
              <span>{entry}</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label={`Remove ${entry}`}
                disabled={disabled}
                onClick={() => {
                  onChange(value.filter((v) => v !== entry));
                }}
                className="flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-os-checkbox hover:bg-os-tone-strong"
              >
                <OsIcon icon={IconX} size={12} />
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        <input
          id={id}
          value={draft}
          disabled={disabled}
          placeholder={value.length === 0 ? placeholder : undefined}
          aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
          aria-describedby={message ? messageId : undefined}
          onChange={handleChange}
          onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
          onBlur={() => {
            if (draft.trim() !== '') commit(draft);
          }}
          className="h-5.5 min-w-24 flex-1 bg-transparent px-1 text-os-base text-os-foreground placeholder:text-os-gray-accessible"
        />
      </div>
      {message ? (
        <p
          id={messageId}
          className={cx('text-os-xs', error ? 'text-os-red-accessible' : 'text-os-gray-accessible')}
        >
          {message}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { IconUsers } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { AppWindow, ContentPane } from './AppWindow';
import { Sidebar, SidebarItem, SidebarSection } from './Sidebar';
import { Toolbar } from './Toolbar';

function renderLayout(path: string) {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[path]}>
      <AppWindow
        sidebar={
          <Sidebar footer={<span>Ana</span>}>
            <SidebarSection title="My sessions">
              <SidebarItem
                to="/sessions/q2w3e4r5t6y7"
                label="Banking"
                state="running"
                agentConnected
              />
              <SidebarItem to="/sessions/z7x6c5v4b3n2" label="Shop" state="stopped" />
            </SidebarSection>
            <SidebarSection title="Admin">
              <SidebarItem to="/admin/users" label="Users" icon={IconUsers} />
            </SidebarSection>
          </Sidebar>
        }
      >
        <Toolbar title="Banking" subtitle="Running" actions={<button type="button">Stop</button>} />
        <ContentPane>content</ContentPane>
      </AppWindow>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

describe('AppWindow, Sidebar, and Toolbar', () => {
  it('has decorative traffic lights', () => {
    renderLayout('/');
    expect(screen.getByTestId('traffic-lights')).toHaveAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
  });

  it('renders the sidebar sections as labelled regions', () => {
    renderLayout('/');
    const nav = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Sidebar' });
    const mine = within(nav).getByRole('region', { name: 'My sessions' });
    expect(
      within(mine)
        .getAllByRole('link')
        .map((a) => a.textContent),
    ).toEqual(['RunningBankingAgent connected', 'StoppedShop']);
    expect(within(nav).getByRole('region', { name: 'Admin' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('marks the item of the current page', () => {
    renderLayout('/sessions/q2w3e4r5t6y7/live');
    const current = screen.getByRole('link', { current: 'page' });
    expect(current).toHaveTextContent('Banking');
    expect(current).toHaveClass('bg-os-accent-fill', 'text-os-on-fill');
  });

  it('shows the toolbar title as the page heading and the content as main', () => {
    renderLayout('/');
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Banking' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('main')).toHaveTextContent('content');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Stop' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';

export interface AppWindowProps {
  sidebar: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
}

/** The one app window over the wallpaper (STYLE.md section 4). */
export function AppWindow({ sidebar, children }: AppWindowProps) {
  return (
    <div className="absolute inset-(--os-window-inset) flex overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-os-surface/70 shadow-os-window backdrop-blur-2xl">
      {sidebar}
      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** The opaque content pane under the toolbar. */
export function ContentPane({ children, className }: { children?: ReactNode; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <main className={cx('flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-auto bg-os-surface', className)}>
      {children}
    </main>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { TablerIcon } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { IconRobot } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useId, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { NavLink } from 'react-router';
import type { SessionState } from '@hydra/shared';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { OsIcon } from './icon';
import { StatusDot } from './StatusDot';
import { TrafficLights } from './TrafficLights';

export interface SidebarProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  /** The user menu at the bottom (spec section 10.2). */
  footer?: ReactNode;
}

export function Sidebar({ children, footer }: SidebarProps) {
  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Sidebar"
      className="flex h-full w-(--os-sidebar-width) shrink-0 flex-col border-r border-os-separator bg-os-surface/50 backdrop-blur-2xl"
    >
      <div className="flex h-(--os-toolbar-height) shrink-0 items-center pl-5">
        <TrafficLights />
      </div>
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto px-2 pb-2">{children}</div>
      {footer ? <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-os-separator p-2">{footer}</div> : null}
    </nav>
  );
}

export interface SidebarSectionProps {
  title: string;
  /** For example the New-session button. */
  action?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
}

export function SidebarSection({ title, action, children }: SidebarSectionProps) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={id}>
      <div className="flex h-6 items-center justify-between px-2">
        <h2 id={id} className="text-os-xs font-semibold text-os-gray-accessible">
          {title}
        </h2>
        {action}
      </div>
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">{children}</ul>
    </section>
  );
}

/** Secondary text inside a section, for example "No sessions". */
export function SidebarNote({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return <li className="px-2 py-1 text-os-xs text-os-gray-accessible">{children}</li>;
}

export interface SidebarItemProps {
  to: string;
  label: string;
  icon?: TablerIcon;
  state?: SessionState;
  /** Shows the agent icon (spec: when activeAgentCount is 1 or more). */
  agentConnected?: boolean;
}

export function SidebarItem({ to, label, icon, state, agentConnected = false }: SidebarItemProps) {
  return (
    <li>
      <NavLink
        to={to}
        className={({ isActive }) =>
          cx(
            'flex h-(--os-row-height) items-center gap-2 rounded-os-control px-2 text-os-base',
            isActive ? 'bg-os-accent-fill text-os-on-fill' : 'text-os-foreground hover:bg-os-tone',
          )
        }
      >
        {state ? <StatusDot state={state} /> : null}
        {icon ? <OsIcon icon={icon} size={18} /> : null}
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{label}</span>
        {agentConnected ? (
          <>
            <OsIcon icon={IconRobot} size={14} />
            <span className="sr-only">Agent connected</span>
          </>
        ) : null}
      </NavLink>
    </li>
  );
}
```

### Task 7: API client: `apiRequest`, `ApiError`, query keys, and the fetch mock

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/api/client.ts`, `packages/web/src/api/keys.ts`
- Create (test helpers): `packages/web/src/test/mock-api.ts`, `packages/web/src/test/fixtures.ts`
- Test: `packages/web/src/api/client.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ApiErrorSchema`, `ErrorCode`, `MeSchema`, and the API types of `@hydra/shared` (index section 5.1).
- Produces: `apiRequest<T>(path, { method?: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE', body?, schema?: { parse(data): T }, query?, signal? }): Promise<T>` (same origin, `credentials: 'same-origin'`, `Accept: application/json`; not GET: `Content-Type: application/json` and `JSON.stringify(body ?? {})`; 204 or no schema → `undefined`); `class ApiError extends Error { status; code: ErrorCode | 'network_error' | 'invalid_response' | 'http_error'; fields; retryAfter }`; `errorMessage(error)`, `isApiError(error, code?)`; `AuditFilters`; `queryKeys` (`me`, `sessions`, `sessionLists`, `sessionList(all)`, `session(sid)`, `members(sid)`, `tokens(sid)`, `auditAll(sid)`, `audit(sid, filters)`, `logs(sid, container)`, `users`). Test helpers: `mockApi(): MockApi` (`on(route, ...answers)`, `calls(route)`, `requests`, `unmatched`), `apiError(status, code, message, fields?)`, and the fixtures (`makeMe`, `makeSession`, `makeToken`, `makeMember`, `makeUser`, `makeAuditEvent`, `SID`, `SID_2`, `ANA_ID`, `BEN_ID`, `VIEWER_RIGHTS`, `OPERATOR_RIGHTS`, `OWNER_RIGHTS`, `T0`).

The client is the only place that calls `fetch`. It turns each failure into `ApiError`: an error body of spec section 12.2 (parsed with `ApiErrorSchema`), an HTTP error without such a body, a network error, or a response that fails its schema. The fetch mock answers routes like `"GET /api/sessions"`; `on(route, a, b)` answers the first call with `a` and all later calls with `b`. The fixtures satisfy the schemas of plan 02 (ULID IDs, 12-character `sid`, integer times).

`packages/web/src/test/mock-api.ts`:

```ts
import { vi } from 'vitest';

export interface MockResponse {
  status?: number;
  body?: unknown;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
}

export interface RecordedRequest {
  method: string;
  path: string;
  query: URLSearchParams;
  body: unknown;
  headers: Headers;
  credentials: RequestCredentials | undefined;
}

type Handler = MockResponse | ((request: RecordedRequest) => MockResponse | Promise<MockResponse>);

/** An error body of spec section 12.2. */
export function apiError(
  status: number,
  code: string,
  message: string,
  fields?: Record<string, string>,
): MockResponse {
  return { status, body: { error: { code, message, ...(fields ? { fields } : {}) } } };
}

/**
 * A fetch mock with routes like "GET /api/sessions" (path without the query string).
 * `on(route, a, b)` answers the first call with `a` and all later calls with `b`.
 * A request without a route gets 404 `not_found` and is kept in `unmatched`.
 */
export class MockApi {
  readonly requests: RecordedRequest[] = [];
  readonly unmatched: string[] = [];
  private readonly routes = new Map<string, Handler[]>();

  on(route: string, ...handlers: Handler[]): this {
    this.routes.set(route, handlers);
    return this;
  }

  calls(route: string): RecordedRequest[] {
    return this.requests.filter((r) => `${r.method} ${r.path}` === route);
  }

  install(): void {
    vi.stubGlobal(
      'fetch',
      vi.fn((input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => this.handle(input, init)),
    );
  }

  private async handle(input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> {
    const url = new URL(
      typeof input === 'string' ? input : input instanceof URL ? input.href : input.url,
      'http://hydra.localhost',
    );
    const method = (init.method ?? 'GET').toUpperCase();
    const request: RecordedRequest = {
      method,
      path: url.pathname,
      query: url.searchParams,
      body: typeof init.body === 'string' ? (JSON.parse(init.body) as unknown) : undefined,
      headers: new Headers(init.headers),
      credentials: init.credentials,
    };
    this.requests.push(request);
    const route = `${method} ${url.pathname}`;
    const handlers = this.routes.get(route);
    let result: MockResponse;
    if (!handlers || handlers.length === 0) {
      this.unmatched.push(route);
      result = apiError(404, 'not_found', `No mock for ${route}`);
    } else {
      const handler = handlers.length > 1 ? handlers.shift() : handlers[0];
      result = typeof handler === 'function' ? await handler(request) : (handler ?? {});
    }
    const status = result.status ?? 200;
    if (status === 204 || result.body === undefined) {
      return new Response(null, { status: status === 200 ? 204 : status, headers: result.headers });
    }
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(result.body), {
      status,
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...result.headers },
    });
  }
}

export function mockApi(): MockApi {
  const api = new MockApi();
  api.install();
  return api;
}
```

`packages/web/src/api/client.test.ts`:

```ts
import { MeSchema } from '@hydra/shared';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { apiError, mockApi, type MockApi } from '../test/mock-api';
import { makeMe } from '../test/fixtures';
import { ApiError, apiRequest, errorMessage } from './client';

let api: MockApi;

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
});

describe('apiRequest', () => {
  it('sends GET requests on the same origin with the cookie and validates the response', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', { body: makeMe() });
    const me = await apiRequest('/api/auth/me', { schema: MeSchema });
    expect(me.email).toBe('ana@example.com');
    const [request] = api.calls('GET /api/auth/me');
    expect(request?.credentials).toBe('same-origin');
    expect(request?.body).toBeUndefined();
    expect(request?.headers.get('Content-Type')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('sends {} as the JSON body of a request without data (spec section 6.4)', async () => {
    api.on('POST /api/sessions/q2w3e4r5t6y7/stop', { status: 204 });
    api.on('DELETE /api/sessions/q2w3e4r5t6y7/live-grant', { status: 204 });
    await apiRequest('/api/sessions/q2w3e4r5t6y7/stop', { method: 'POST' });
    await apiRequest('/api/sessions/q2w3e4r5t6y7/live-grant', { method: 'DELETE' });
    for (const request of api.requests) {
      expect(request.body).toEqual({});
      expect(request.headers.get('Content-Type')).toBe('application/json');
    }
  });

  it('sends the data as JSON and drops empty query values', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/sessions/q2w3e4r5t6y7/audit', {
      body: { events: [], nextBefore: null },
    });
    api.on('PATCH /api/users/u1', { status: 204 });
    await apiRequest('/api/sessions/q2w3e4r5t6y7/audit', {
      query: { kind: 'mcp.tool_call', tool: '', before: undefined, limit: 50 },
    });
    await apiRequest('/api/users/u1', { method: 'PATCH', body: { role: 'admin' } });
    expect(api.requests[0]?.query.toString()).toBe('kind=mcp.tool_call&limit=50');
    expect(api.requests[1]?.body).toEqual({ role: 'admin' });
  });

  it('turns an error body into ApiError', async () => {
    api.on(
      'POST /api/sessions',
      apiError(400, 'validation_failed', 'Invalid input', { name: 'Use 1 to 64 characters' }),
    );
    const error = await apiRequest('/api/sessions', { method: 'POST', body: {} }).catch(
      (e: unknown) => e,
    );
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(error).toMatchObject({
      status: 400,
      code: 'validation_failed',
      message: 'Invalid input',
      fields: { name: 'Use 1 to 64 characters' },
    });
  });

  it('reads Retry-After from a 429', async () => {
    api.on('POST /api/auth/login', {
      ...apiError(429, 'rate_limited', 'Too many attempts'),
      headers: { 'Retry-After': '900' },
    });
    await expect(
      apiRequest('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', body: { email: 'a', password: 'b' } }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'rate_limited', retryAfter: 900 });
  });

  it('gives http_error for an error without an API body', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/users', { status: 502, body: 'Bad gateway' });
    await expect(apiRequest('/api/users')).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 502,
      code: 'http_error',
    });
  });

  it('gives invalid_response when the body does not match the schema', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', { body: { id: 1 } });
    await expect(apiRequest('/api/auth/me', { schema: MeSchema })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'invalid_response',
    });
  });

  it('gives network_error when fetch fails', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new TypeError('Failed to fetch')));
    await expect(apiRequest('/api/auth/me')).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 0,
      code: 'network_error',
    });
  });
});

describe('errorMessage', () => {
  it('uses the message of the error', () => {
    expect(errorMessage(new ApiError(409, 'conflict', 'The email exists'))).toBe(
      'The email exists',
    );
    expect(errorMessage('x')).toBe('Something went wrong.');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { ApiErrorSchema, type ErrorCode } from '@hydra/shared';

/** Error codes of spec section 12.2, plus the codes of failures that happen in the client. */
export type ClientErrorCode = ErrorCode | 'network_error' | 'invalid_response' | 'http_error';

/** The error that every API call throws. It wraps the `ApiError` body of @hydra/shared. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code: ClientErrorCode;
  readonly fields: Readonly<Record<string, string>> | undefined;
  /** Seconds from the Retry-After header of a 429 response. */
  readonly retryAfter: number | null;

  constructor(
    status: number,
    code: ClientErrorCode,
    message: string,
    fields?: Record<string, string>,
    retryAfter: number | null = null,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
    this.fields = fields;
    this.retryAfter = retryAfter;
  }
}

/** A zod schema of @hydra/shared, or anything else with `parse`. */
export interface Parser<T> {
  parse: (data: unknown) => T;
}

export type HttpMethod = 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE';

export interface RequestOptions<T> {
  method?: HttpMethod;
  /** JSON body. Requests that are not GET send `{}` when it is missing (spec section 6.4). */
  body?: unknown;
  /** Validates the response body. Without it, the call returns undefined. */
  schema?: Parser<T>;
  query?: Record<string, string | number | undefined>;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}

function withQuery(path: string, query: RequestOptions<unknown>['query']): string {
  if (!query) return path;
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(query)) {
    if (value !== undefined && value !== '') params.set(key, String(value));
  }
  const text = params.toString();
  return text === '' ? path : `${path}?${text}`;
}

async function toApiError(response: Response): Promise<ApiError> {
  const retryHeader = response.headers.get('Retry-After');
  const retryAfter = retryHeader !== null && /^\d+$/.test(retryHeader) ? Number(retryHeader) : null;
  let body: unknown;
  try {
    body = await response.json();
  } catch {
    body = null;
  }
  const parsed = ApiErrorSchema.safeParse(body);
  if (parsed.success) {
    const { code, message, fields } = parsed.data.error;
    return new ApiError(response.status, code, message, fields, retryAfter);
  }
  return new ApiError(
    response.status,
    'http_error',
    `The request failed with HTTP ${String(response.status)}.`,
    undefined,
    retryAfter,
  );
}

/**
 * Calls the dashboard API on the same origin with the login session cookie.
 * Throws ApiError for HTTP errors, network errors, and responses that fail the schema.
 */
export async function apiRequest<T = undefined>(
  path: string,
  options: RequestOptions<T> = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const method = options.method ?? 'GET';
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: 'application/json' };
  const init: RequestInit = { method, headers, credentials: 'same-origin' };
  if (options.signal) init.signal = options.signal;
  if (method !== 'GET') {
    headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
    init.body = JSON.stringify(options.body ?? {});
  }

  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(withQuery(path, options.query), init);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof DOMException && error.name === 'AbortError') throw error;
    throw new ApiError(0, 'network_error', 'The server cannot be reached. Check your connection.');
  }

  if (!response.ok) throw await toApiError(response);
  if (response.status === 204 || !options.schema) return undefined as T;

  let data: unknown;
  try {
    data = await response.json();
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError(
      response.status,
      'invalid_response',
      'The server sent a response that is not JSON.',
    );
  }
  try {
    return options.schema.parse(data);
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError(
      response.status,
      'invalid_response',
      'The server sent an unexpected response.',
    );
  }
}

/** A text for people from any error. */
export function errorMessage(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof Error && error.message !== '') return error.message;
  return 'Something went wrong.';
}

export function isApiError(error: unknown, code?: ClientErrorCode): error is ApiError {
  return error instanceof ApiError && (code === undefined || error.code === code);
}
```

```ts
import type { AuditKind } from '@hydra/shared';

/** Filters of the Activity view (spec section 12.1, GET /api/sessions/:sid/audit). */
export interface AuditFilters {
  kind?: AuditKind;
  tool?: string;
  status?: 'pending' | 'ok' | 'error';
  tokenId?: string;
}

/** All TanStack Query keys. Each key under `sessions` belongs to the session data. */
export const queryKeys = {
  me: ['me'] as const,
  sessions: ['sessions'] as const,
  sessionLists: ['sessions', 'list'] as const,
  sessionList: (all: boolean) => ['sessions', 'list', all ? 'all' : 'mine'] as const,
  session: (sid: string) => ['sessions', 'detail', sid] as const,
  members: (sid: string) => ['sessions', 'members', sid] as const,
  tokens: (sid: string) => ['sessions', 'tokens', sid] as const,
  auditAll: (sid: string) => ['sessions', 'audit', sid] as const,
  audit: (sid: string, filters: AuditFilters) => ['sessions', 'audit', sid, filters] as const,
  logs: (sid: string, container: 'neko' | 'mcp') => ['sessions', 'logs', sid, container] as const,
  users: ['users'] as const,
};
```

```ts
describe('session cache', () => {
  it('patches the detail and every list that has the session', () => {
    const qc = client();
    qc.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessionList(false), [makeSession(), makeSession({ id: SID_2 })]);
    qc.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessionList(true), [makeSession()]);

      'stopping',
    );
    expect(qc.getQueryData<SessionResource[]>(queryKeys.sessionList(false))?.[1]?.state).toBe(
      'running',
    );
    expect(qc.getQueryData<SessionResource[]>(queryKeys.sessionList(true))?.[0]?.state).toBe(
      'stopping',
    );
  });

  it('reports an unknown session', () => {
    const qc = client();
    qc.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessionList(false), [makeSession()]);
    expect(patchSession(qc, SID_2, { state: 'running' })).toBe(false);
  });

    const qc = client();
    qc.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessionList(false), [makeSession()]);
    qc.setQueryData(queryKeys.session(SID), makeSession());

    removeSession(qc, SID);

      qc.getQueryData<SessionResource[]>(queryKeys.sessionList(false))?.map((s) => s.id),
    ).toEqual([SID_2]);
    expect(qc.getQueryData<SessionResource>(queryKeys.session(SID))?.state).toBe('deleted');
  });
});

describe('addAuditEvent', () => {
  function seed(qc: QueryClient, filters: Parameters<typeof queryKeys.audit>[1]) {
    const data: InfiniteData<AuditPage, number | undefined> = {
      pages: [{ events: [makeAuditEvent({ id: 5, status: 'pending' })], nextBefore: null }],
      pageParams: [undefined],
    };
    qc.setQueryData(queryKeys.audit(SID, filters), data);
  }
  function events(qc: QueryClient, filters: Parameters<typeof queryKeys.audit>[1]) {
    return qc
      .getQueryData<InfiniteData<AuditPage>>(queryKeys.audit(SID, filters))

    seed(qc, {});
    seed(qc, { kind: 'session.state' });
    addAuditEvent(qc, SID, makeAuditEvent({ id: 6 }));
    expect(events(qc, {})).toEqual(['6:ok', '5:pending']);

    expect(events(qc, { tool: 'browser_type', tokenId: 'other' })).toEqual(['5:pending']);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';

let api: MockApi;

});

function setup() {

    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>
  );

}

describe('API hooks', () => {

    api.on('GET /api/sessions', { body: [makeSession()] });
    const { wrapper } = setup();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useSessions({ all: true }), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(result.current.data).toHaveLength(1);

    api.on('POST /api/sessions/q2w3e4r5t6y7/start', apiError(401, 'unauthenticated', 'Log in'));
    const { wrapper, queryClient } = setup();

    await act(async () => {

    });

    await act(() => result.current.mutateAsync('stop'));
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData(queryKeys.session(SID))).toMatchObject({ state: 'stopping' });
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/app/query-client.ts`:

```ts
import { MutationCache, QueryCache, QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';
import { queryKeys } from '../api/keys';

function shouldRetry(failureCount: number, error: unknown): boolean {
  if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status >= 400 && error.status < 500) return false;
  return failureCount < 2;
}

/**
 * The TanStack Query client. A 401 anywhere ends the login on the client (the route guard then
 * opens Login). A `password_change_required` 403 loads the user again, so the guard opens
 * Change password.
 */
export function createQueryClient(options: { retry?: boolean } = {}): QueryClient {
  const handleAuthError = (error: unknown) => {
    if (!(error instanceof ApiError)) return;
    if (error.code === 'unauthenticated') {
      queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.me, null);
    } else if (error.code === 'password_change_required') {
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.me });
    }
  };
  const queryClient: QueryClient = new QueryClient({
    queryCache: new QueryCache({ onError: handleAuthError }),
    mutationCache: new MutationCache({ onError: handleAuthError }),
    defaultOptions: {
      queries: {
        retry: options.retry === false ? false : shouldRetry,
        staleTime: 5_000,
        refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
      },
      mutations: { retry: false },
    },
  });
  return queryClient;
}
```

`packages/web/src/api/cache.ts`:

```ts
import type { InfiniteData, QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { AuditEvent, AuditPage, SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { queryKeys, type AuditFilters } from './keys';

function sessionListKeys(queryClient: QueryClient) {
  return queryClient
    .getQueryCache()
    .findAll({ queryKey: queryKeys.sessionLists })
    .map((query) => query.queryKey);
}

/** Puts a session from an API response into the detail cache and into each cached list. */
export function storeSession(queryClient: QueryClient, session: SessionResource): void {
  queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.session(session.id), session);
  for (const key of sessionListKeys(queryClient)) {
    queryClient.setQueryData<SessionResource[]>(key, (list) => {
      if (!list) return list;
      return list.some((s) => s.id === session.id)
        ? list.map((s) => (s.id === session.id ? session : s))
        : [...list, session];
    });
  }
}

/**
 * Changes fields of a cached session (SSE events). Returns false when no cached list has the
 * session, so the caller can fetch the lists again.
 */
export function patchSession(
  queryClient: QueryClient,
  sid: string,
  patch: Partial<Pick<SessionResource, 'state' | 'errorReason' | 'agentActivity'>>,
): boolean {
  queryClient.setQueryData<SessionResource>(queryKeys.session(sid), (session) =>
    session ? { ...session, ...patch } : session,
  );
  let found = false;
  for (const key of sessionListKeys(queryClient)) {
    queryClient.setQueryData<SessionResource[]>(key, (list) => {
      if (!list?.some((s) => s.id === sid)) return list;
      found = true;
      return list.map((s) => (s.id === sid ? { ...s, ...patch } : s));
    });
  }
  return found;
}

/**
 * Removes a deleted session from the lists. The detail keeps the state `deleted`, so an open
 * session page updates at once (removing an observed query would not re-render it).
 */
export function removeSession(queryClient: QueryClient, sid: string): void {
  for (const key of sessionListKeys(queryClient)) {
    queryClient.setQueryData<SessionResource[]>(key, (list) => list?.filter((s) => s.id !== sid));
  }
  queryClient.setQueryData<SessionResource>(queryKeys.session(sid), (session) =>
    session ? { ...session, state: 'deleted' } : session,
  );
  queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.members(sid) });
  queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.tokens(sid) });
  queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.auditAll(sid) });
}

/** The client-side copy of the server filters, for events that arrive on the SSE stream. */
export function auditEventMatches(event: AuditEvent, filters: AuditFilters): boolean {
  if (filters.kind !== undefined && event.kind !== filters.kind) return false;
  if (filters.status !== undefined && event.status !== filters.status) return false;
  if (filters.tool !== undefined && event.data.tool !== filters.tool) return false;
  if (
    filters.tokenId !== undefined &&
    !(event.actorType === 'token' && event.actorId === filters.tokenId) &&
    event.data.tokenId !== filters.tokenId
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

/**
 * Adds or replaces an audit event in each cached Activity query of the session. An event with a
 * known ID replaces the old copy (a `pending` tool call that is now `ok`), or leaves the query
 * when it no longer matches its filters.
 */
export function addAuditEvent(queryClient: QueryClient, sid: string, event: AuditEvent): void {
  const queries = queryClient.getQueryCache().findAll({ queryKey: queryKeys.auditAll(sid) });
  for (const query of queries) {
    const filters = (query.queryKey[3] ?? {}) as AuditFilters;
    const matches = auditEventMatches(event, filters);
    queryClient.setQueryData<InfiniteData<AuditPage, number | undefined>>(
      query.queryKey,
      (data) => {
        if (!data) return data;
        const known = data.pages.some((page) => page.events.some((e) => e.id === event.id));
        if (known) {
          return {
            ...data,
            pages: data.pages.map((page) => ({
              ...page,
              events: matches
                ? page.events.map((e) => (e.id === event.id ? event : e))
                : page.events.filter((e) => e.id !== event.id),
            })),
          };
        }
        if (!matches) return data;
        const [first, ...rest] = data.pages;
        if (!first) return data;
        return { ...data, pages: [{ ...first, events: [event, ...first.events] }, ...rest] };
      },
    );
  }
}
```

`packages/web/src/api/auth.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { MeSchema, type ChangePasswordRequest, type LoginRequest, type Me } from '@hydra/shared';
import { ApiError, apiRequest } from './client';
import { queryKeys } from './keys';

/** The current user, or null without a login session (401). */
export async function fetchMe(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Me | null> {
  try {
    return await apiRequest('/api/auth/me', { schema: MeSchema, ...(signal ? { signal } : {}) });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 401) return null;
    throw error;
  }
}

export function useMe() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.me,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => fetchMe(signal),
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });
}

export function useLogin() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: LoginRequest) =>
      apiRequest('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', body: input, schema: MeSchema }),
    onSuccess: (me) => {
      queryClient.removeQueries({ predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] !== 'me' });
      queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.me, me);
    },
  });
}

export function useLogout() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => apiRequest('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' }),
    onSettled: () => {
      queryClient.removeQueries({ predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] !== 'me' });
      queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.me, null);
    },
  });
}

export function useChangePassword() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: ChangePasswordRequest) =>
      apiRequest('/api/auth/password', { method: 'POST', body: input }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.setQueryData<Me | null>(queryKeys.me, (me) =>
        me ? { ...me, mustChangePassword: false } : me,
      );
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.me });
    },
  });
}
```

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  LogsResponseSchema,
  SessionResourceSchema,
  type CreateSessionRequest,
  type UpdateSessionRequest,
} from '@hydra/shared';
import { apiRequest } from './client';
import { removeSession, storeSession } from './cache';
import { queryKeys } from './keys';

const SessionListSchema = SessionResourceSchema.array();

/** Own and shared sessions, or all sessions for an admin with `all` (spec section 12.1). */
export function useSessions(options: { all?: boolean } = {}) {
  const all = options.all ?? false;
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.sessionList(all),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      apiRequest('/api/sessions', {
        schema: SessionListSchema,
        signal,
        ...(all ? { query: { all: 1 } } : {}),
      }),
  });
}

export function useSession(sid: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.session(sid),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}`, { schema: SessionResourceSchema, signal }),
  });
}

export function useCreateSession() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: CreateSessionRequest) =>
      apiRequest('/api/sessions', { method: 'POST', body: input, schema: SessionResourceSchema }),
    onSuccess: (session) => {
      storeSession(queryClient, session);
    },
    onError: () => {
      // A create with start=true can fail at the start step after the row exists.
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.sessionLists });
    },
  });
}

export function useUpdateSession(sid: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: UpdateSessionRequest) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        body: input,
        schema: SessionResourceSchema,
      }),
    onSuccess: (session) => {
      storeSession(queryClient, session);
    },
  });
}

export type LifecycleAction = 'start' | 'stop' | 'restart';

/** Start, Stop, Restart, and Retry start (spec section 7.9). */
export function useSessionAction(sid: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (action: LifecycleAction) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/${action}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        schema: SessionResourceSchema,
      }),
    onSuccess: (session) => {
      storeSession(queryClient, session);
    },
  });
}

export function useDeleteSession(sid: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (confirmName: string) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/delete`, { method: 'POST', body: { confirmName } }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      removeSession(queryClient, sid);
    },
  });
}

/** The last 50 log lines of one container (Diagnostics, right `session.logs`). */
export function useLogs(sid: string, container: 'neko' | 'mcp', enabled: boolean) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.logs(sid, container),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/logs`, {
        schema: LogsResponseSchema,
        query: { container },
        signal,
      }),
    enabled,
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}
```

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  MemberSchema,
  MembersResponseSchema,
  type AddMemberRequest,
  type MemberRole,
} from '@hydra/shared';
import { apiRequest } from './client';
import { queryKeys } from './keys';

export function useMembers(sid: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.members(sid),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/members`, { schema: MembersResponseSchema, signal }),
  });
}

function useMembersMutation<TInput>(sid: string, request: (input: TInput) => Promise<unknown>) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: request,
    onSettled: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.members(sid) }),
  });
}

export function useAddMember(sid: string) {
  return useMembersMutation(sid, (input: AddMemberRequest) =>
    apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/members`, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: input,
      schema: MemberSchema,
    }),
  );
}

export function useUpdateMember(sid: string) {
  return useMembersMutation(sid, (input: { userId: string; role: MemberRole }) =>
    apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/members/${input.userId}`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      body: { role: input.role },
      schema: MemberSchema,
    }),
  );
}

export function useRemoveMember(sid: string) {
  return useMembersMutation(sid, (userId: string) =>
    apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/members/${userId}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
  );
}
```

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { CreateTokenResponseSchema, TokenSchema, type CreateTokenRequest } from '@hydra/shared';
import { apiRequest } from './client';
import { queryKeys } from './keys';

const TokenListSchema = TokenSchema.array();

export function useTokens(sid: string, enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.tokens(sid),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/tokens`, { schema: TokenListSchema, signal }),
    enabled,
  });
}

/** Returns the token secret one time (spec section 6.6). */
export function useCreateToken(sid: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: CreateTokenRequest) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/tokens`, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: input,
        schema: CreateTokenResponseSchema,
      }),
    onSettled: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.tokens(sid) }),
  });
}

export function useRevokeToken(sid: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (tokenId: string) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/tokens/${tokenId}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
    onSettled: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.tokens(sid) }),
  });
}
```

```ts
import { useInfiniteQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { AuditPageSchema } from '@hydra/shared';
import { apiRequest } from './client';
import { queryKeys, type AuditFilters } from './keys';

export const AUDIT_PAGE_SIZE = 50;

/** Pages of audit events, newest first. "Load older" fetches the next page. */
export function useAuditPages(sid: string, filters: AuditFilters) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.audit(sid, filters),
    queryFn: ({ pageParam, signal }) =>
      apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/audit`, {
        schema: AuditPageSchema,
        signal,
        query: { ...filters, before: pageParam, limit: AUDIT_PAGE_SIZE },
      }),
    initialPageParam: undefined as number | undefined,
    getNextPageParam: (lastPage) => lastPage.nextBefore ?? undefined,
  });
}
```

```ts
import { LiveTicketResponseSchema } from '@hydra/shared';
import { apiRequest } from './client';

/** Step 1 of the open flow (spec section 9.2): a one-use URL for the iframe. */
export async function createLiveTicket(sid: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<string> {
  const response = await apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/live-ticket`, {
    method: 'POST',
    schema: LiveTicketResponseSchema,
    ...(signal ? { signal } : {}),
  });
  return response.url;
}

/** Ends the live grants of this login session for the session (spec section 9.2). */
export async function endLiveGrant(sid: string): Promise<void> {
  await apiRequest(`/api/sessions/${sid}/live-grant`, { method: 'DELETE' });
}
```

### Task 9: SSE: `EventStream`, the user and the session streams

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/lib/sse.ts`, `packages/web/src/lib/events.tsx`
- Create (test helpers): `packages/web/src/test/mock-event-source.ts`
- Test: `packages/web/src/lib/sse.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 8 (`patchSession`, `removeSession`, `addAuditEvent`, `queryKeys`); `SessionStateEventSchema`, `SessionActivityEventSchema`, `AuditEventSchema` of `@hydra/shared`.
- Produces: `RETRY_DELAYS_MS`; `class EventStream { start(); stop(); ensureOpen() }` with `{ url, handlers, onReconnect?, onGiveUp? }`; `createUserEventStream(queryClient, { onReconnect })` (`GET /api/events`); `createSessionEventStream(queryClient, sid)` (`GET /api/sessions/<sid>/events`, event `audit`); `UserEventsProvider` (opens the user stream, reconnects on window focus), `useEventsReconnectCount(): number`, `useSessionEvents(sid)`. Test helper: `MockEventSource` (`install()`, `latest(url)`, `open()`, `emit(type, data)`, `drop()`, `fail()`).

Spec section 10.3. The browser reconnects an EventSource by itself after a network error (`error`, then `open`); after an HTTP error answer it gives up (`readyState` `CLOSED`), and `EventStream` opens a new one after 1, 2, 5, 10, then 30 s. Each `open` after an `error` is a reconnect: the user stream marks all session queries stale, so the screens fetch again, and counts the reconnect (the Live view gets a new ticket, Task 12). A give-up also refetches `me`, so an ended login shows the Login screen. Events that fail their schema are ignored.

`packages/web/src/test/mock-event-source.ts`:

```ts
import { vi } from 'vitest';

/** A controllable EventSource. Tests drive it with open, emit, drop, and fail. */
export class MockEventSource extends EventTarget {
  static readonly CONNECTING = 0;
  static readonly OPEN = 1;
  static readonly CLOSED = 2;
  static instances: MockEventSource[] = [];

  readonly url: string;
  readyState = MockEventSource.CONNECTING;

  constructor(url: string | URL) {
    super();
    this.url = String(url);
    MockEventSource.instances.push(this);
  }

  close(): void {
    this.readyState = MockEventSource.CLOSED;
  }

  /** The connection is open (also after a browser reconnect). */
  open(): void {
    this.readyState = MockEventSource.OPEN;
    this.dispatchEvent(new Event('open'));
  }

  /** The server sends one event with a JSON payload. */
  emit(type: string, data: unknown): void {
    this.dispatchEvent(new MessageEvent(type, { data: JSON.stringify(data) }));
  }

  /** The connection drops. The browser connects again by itself. */
  drop(): void {
    this.readyState = MockEventSource.CONNECTING;
    this.dispatchEvent(new Event('error'));
  }

  /** The server answers with an error status. The browser gives up. */
  fail(): void {
    this.readyState = MockEventSource.CLOSED;
    this.dispatchEvent(new Event('error'));
  }

  static install(): void {
    MockEventSource.instances = [];
    vi.stubGlobal('EventSource', MockEventSource);
  }

  /** The newest open source for a URL. */
  static latest(url: string): MockEventSource {
    const source = MockEventSource.instances
      .filter((s) => s.url === url && s.readyState !== MockEventSource.CLOSED)
      .at(-1);
    if (!source) throw new Error(`No open EventSource for ${url}`);
    return source;
  }
}
```

`packages/web/src/lib/sse.test.ts`:

```ts
import { QueryClient, type InfiniteData } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { AuditPage, SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { queryKeys } from '../api/keys';
import { makeAuditEvent, makeSession, SID, SID_2 } from '../test/fixtures';
import { MockEventSource } from '../test/mock-event-source';
import { createSessionEventStream, createUserEventStream, EventStream } from './sse';

let queryClient: QueryClient;

beforeEach(() => {
  MockEventSource.install();
  queryClient = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

function sessions() {
  return queryClient.getQueryData<SessionResource[]>(queryKeys.sessionList(false));
}

describe('createUserEventStream', () => {
  it('updates the state and the agent activity of a cached session', () => {
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessionList(false), [makeSession({ state: 'starting' })]);
    const stream = createUserEventStream(queryClient, { onReconnect: vi.fn() });
    stream.start();
    const source = MockEventSource.latest('/api/events');
    source.open();
    source.emit('session.state', { sessionId: SID, state: 'running', errorReason: null });
    source.emit('session.activity', {
      sessionId: SID,
      agentActivity: {
        activeAgentCount: 2,
        lastToolCall: { tool: 'browser_click', status: 'ok', durationMs: 230, startedAt: 1 },
      },
    });
    expect(sessions()?.[0]).toMatchObject({
      state: 'running',
      agentActivity: { activeAgentCount: 2 },
    });
    stream.stop();
  });

  it('removes a deleted session and fetches the lists for an unknown one', () => {
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessionList(false), [makeSession()]);
    const stream = createUserEventStream(queryClient, { onReconnect: vi.fn() });
    stream.start();
    const source = MockEventSource.latest('/api/events');
    source.emit('session.state', { sessionId: SID_2, state: 'starting', errorReason: null });
    expect(queryClient.getQueryState(queryKeys.sessionList(false))?.isInvalidated).toBe(true);
    source.emit('session.state', { sessionId: SID, state: 'deleted', errorReason: null });
    expect(sessions()).toEqual([]);
    stream.stop();
  });

  it('ignores events that fail the schema', () => {
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessionList(false), [makeSession()]);
    const stream = createUserEventStream(queryClient, { onReconnect: vi.fn() });
    stream.start();
    MockEventSource.latest('/api/events').emit('session.state', { sessionId: SID, state: 'odd' });
    expect(sessions()?.[0]?.state).toBe('running');
    stream.stop();
  });

  it('fetches the session data again after a reconnect (spec section 10.3)', () => {
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessionList(false), [makeSession()]);
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.session(SID), makeSession());
    const onReconnect = vi.fn();
    const stream = createUserEventStream(queryClient, { onReconnect });
    stream.start();
    const source = MockEventSource.latest('/api/events');
    source.open();
    expect(onReconnect).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    source.drop();
    source.open();
    expect(onReconnect).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(queryClient.getQueryState(queryKeys.sessionList(false))?.isInvalidated).toBe(true);
    expect(queryClient.getQueryState(queryKeys.session(SID))?.isInvalidated).toBe(true);
    stream.stop();
  });

  it('opens a new connection with a delay when the browser gave up, and checks the login', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.me, null);
    const onReconnect = vi.fn();
    const stream = createUserEventStream(queryClient, { onReconnect });
    stream.start();
    MockEventSource.latest('/api/events').fail();
    expect(queryClient.getQueryState(queryKeys.me)?.isInvalidated).toBe(true);
    expect(MockEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(1);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1_000);
    expect(MockEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(2);
    MockEventSource.latest('/api/events').open();
    expect(onReconnect).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    stream.stop();
  });
});

describe('EventStream', () => {
  it('connects at once on ensureOpen after the browser gave up', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const stream = new EventStream({ url: '/api/events', handlers: {} });
    stream.start();
    MockEventSource.latest('/api/events').fail();
    stream.ensureOpen();
    expect(MockEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(2);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000);
    expect(MockEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(2);
    stream.stop();
  });

  it('does nothing after stop', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const stream = new EventStream({ url: '/api/events', handlers: {} });
    stream.start();
    const source = MockEventSource.latest('/api/events');
    stream.stop();
    expect(source.readyState).toBe(MockEventSource.CLOSED);
    stream.ensureOpen();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000);
    expect(MockEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe('createSessionEventStream', () => {
  it('adds audit events to the Activity cache and fetches it again after a reconnect', () => {
    const data: InfiniteData<AuditPage, number | undefined> = {
      pages: [{ events: [makeAuditEvent({ id: 1 })], nextBefore: null }],
      pageParams: [undefined],
    };
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.audit(SID, {}), data);
    const stream = createSessionEventStream(queryClient, SID);
    stream.start();
    const source = MockEventSource.latest(`/api/sessions/${SID}/events`);
    source.open();
    source.emit('audit', makeAuditEvent({ id: 2 }));
    const cached = queryClient.getQueryData<InfiniteData<AuditPage>>(queryKeys.audit(SID, {}));
    expect(cached?.pages[0]?.events.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual([2, 1]);
    source.drop();
    source.open();
    expect(queryClient.getQueryState(queryKeys.audit(SID, {}))?.isInvalidated).toBe(true);
    stream.stop();
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/lib/sse.ts`:

```ts
import type { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  AuditEventSchema,
  SessionActivityEventSchema,
  SessionStateEventSchema,
} from '@hydra/shared';
import { addAuditEvent, patchSession, removeSession } from '../api/cache';
import { queryKeys } from '../api/keys';

/** Waits before a new connection when the browser gave up (HTTP error answer). */
export const RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [1_000, 2_000, 5_000, 10_000, 30_000] as const;

export interface EventStreamOptions {
  url: string;
  /** Event name → handler of the parsed JSON data. */
  handlers: Record<string, (data: unknown) => void>;
  /** Runs when the stream is open again after an error (spec section 10.3). */
  onReconnect?: () => void;
  /** Runs when the browser gave up, before the next try. */
  onGiveUp?: () => void;
}

/**
 * One SSE connection with reconnect. The browser reconnects by itself after a network error.
 * After an HTTP error answer (readyState CLOSED), this class opens a new EventSource with the
 * delays of RETRY_DELAYS_MS.
 */
export class EventStream {
  private readonly options: EventStreamOptions;
  private source: EventSource | null = null;
  private retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  private attempt = 0;
  private hadError = false;
  private stopped = true;

  constructor(options: EventStreamOptions) {
    this.options = options;
  }

  start(): void {
    this.stopped = false;
    this.connect();
  }

  stop(): void {
    this.stopped = true;
    if (this.retryTimer !== null) clearTimeout(this.retryTimer);
    this.retryTimer = null;
    this.source?.close();
    this.source = null;
  }

  /** Connects at once if the browser gave up, for example when the window gets the focus. */
  ensureOpen(): void {
    if (this.stopped) return;
    if (this.source && this.source.readyState !== EventSource.CLOSED) return;
    if (this.retryTimer !== null) clearTimeout(this.retryTimer);
    this.retryTimer = null;
    this.connect();
  }

  private connect(): void {
    const source = new EventSource(this.options.url);
    this.source = source;
    source.addEventListener('open', () => {
      this.attempt = 0;
      if (this.hadError) {
        this.hadError = false;
        this.options.onReconnect?.();
      }
    });
    source.addEventListener('error', () => {
      this.hadError = true;
      if (source.readyState === EventSource.CLOSED && this.source === source) this.retryLater();
    });
    for (const [name, handler] of Object.entries(this.options.handlers)) {
      source.addEventListener(name, (event) => {
        let data: unknown;
        try {
          data = JSON.parse((event as MessageEvent<string>).data);
        } catch {
          return;
        }
        handler(data);
      });
    }
  }

  private retryLater(): void {
    this.source?.close();
    this.source = null;
    this.options.onGiveUp?.();
    if (this.stopped) return;
    const delay = RETRY_DELAYS_MS[Math.min(this.attempt, RETRY_DELAYS_MS.length - 1)];
    this.attempt += 1;
    this.retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.retryTimer = null;
      if (!this.stopped) this.connect();
    }, delay);
  }
}

/**
 * GET /api/events: `session.state` and `session.activity` of all sessions that the user can read.
 * It updates the session caches. After a reconnect it fetches the session data again.
 */
export function createUserEventStream(
  queryClient: QueryClient,
  callbacks: { onReconnect: () => void },
): EventStream {
  return new EventStream({
    url: '/api/events',
    handlers: {
      'session.state': (data) => {
        const parsed = SessionStateEventSchema.safeParse(data);
        if (!parsed.success) return;
        const { sessionId, state, errorReason } = parsed.data;
        if (state === 'deleted') {
          removeSession(queryClient, sessionId);
          return;
        }
        const found = patchSession(queryClient, sessionId, { state, errorReason });
        if (!found) void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.sessionLists });
        // restartRequired can change with the state.
        void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.session(sessionId) });
      },
      'session.activity': (data) => {
        const parsed = SessionActivityEventSchema.safeParse(data);
        if (!parsed.success) return;
        patchSession(queryClient, parsed.data.sessionId, {
          agentActivity: parsed.data.agentActivity,
        });
      },
    },
    onReconnect: () => {
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.sessions });
      callbacks.onReconnect();
    },
    onGiveUp: () => {
      // A 401 means that the login ended. Loading the user again shows the Login screen.
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.me });
    },
  });
}

/** GET /api/sessions/<sid>/events: audit events for the Activity view (right `audit.read`). */
export function createSessionEventStream(queryClient: QueryClient, sid: string): EventStream {
  return new EventStream({
    url: `/api/sessions/${sid}/events`,
    handlers: {
      audit: (data) => {
        const parsed = AuditEventSchema.safeParse(data);
        if (parsed.success) addAuditEvent(queryClient, sid, parsed.data);
      },
    },
    onReconnect: () => {
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.auditAll(sid) });
    },
  });
}
```

`packages/web/src/lib/events.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { createSessionEventStream, createUserEventStream } from './sse';

/** Counts the reconnects of the user SSE stream. The Live view gets a new ticket when it changes. */
const ReconnectCountContext = createContext(0);

/** Keeps GET /api/events open while the user is logged in (spec section 10.3). */
export function UserEventsProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [reconnects, setReconnects] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const stream = createUserEventStream(queryClient, {
      onReconnect: () => {
        setReconnects((count) => count + 1);
      },
    });
    stream.start();
    const wake = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') stream.ensureOpen();
    };
    window.addEventListener('focus', wake);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', wake);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('focus', wake);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', wake);
      stream.stop();
    };
  }, [queryClient]);

  return <ReconnectCountContext value={reconnects}>{children}</ReconnectCountContext>;
}

export function useEventsReconnectCount(): number {
  return useContext(ReconnectCountContext);
}

/** Keeps GET /api/sessions/<sid>/events open while the Activity view is open. */
export function useSessionEvents(sid: string): void {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  useEffect(() => {
    const stream = createSessionEventStream(queryClient, sid);
    stream.start();
    const wake = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') stream.ensureOpen();
    };
    window.addEventListener('focus', wake);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', wake);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('focus', wake);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', wake);
      stream.stop();
    };
  }, [queryClient, sid]);
}
```

- Consumes: Task 8 (`endLiveGrant`); `AgentActivity` of `@hydra/shared`.
- Produces: `class LiveTabRegistry { beforeOpen(sid): Promise<void>; opened(sid) }`, `LiveTabProvider`, `useLiveTab()`; `formatDateTime(ms | null)`, `formatEventTime(ms)`, `formatToolCall(call)` (`browser_click · ok · 230 ms`), `formatAgentCount(n)`, `permitsAllDomains(allowlist)`; `mcpEndpoint(origin, sid)`, `claudeMcpAddCommand(name, endpoint, secret)`, `mcpServersJson(name, endpoint, secret)`; `ALLOWLIST_HELP`; `useIsRecent(timestamp | null, windowMs): boolean`.

`LiveTabRegistry` is the "previous session" memory of spec section 9.2: before a browser tab opens the live view of another session, it calls `DELETE /api/sessions/<previous sid>/live-grant` and waits for it; a second caller waits for the same request. `ALLOWLIST_HELP` is the S5 fallback text (index section 6). `useIsRecent` re-renders once when the 10 s of "Agent active" end (no interval timer).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { LiveTabRegistry } from './live-tab';

describe('LiveTabRegistry', () => {
  it('ends the grant of the previous session before another session opens', async () => {
    const endGrant = vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve());
    const tab = new LiveTabRegistry(endGrant);
    await tab.beforeOpen('aaaaaaaaaaaa');
    expect(endGrant).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    tab.opened('aaaaaaaaaaaa');
    await tab.beforeOpen('aaaaaaaaaaaa');
    expect(endGrant).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await tab.beforeOpen('bbbbbbbbbbbb');
    expect(endGrant).toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith('aaaaaaaaaaaa');
  });

  it('lets a second caller wait for the same pending end, and ignores errors', async () => {
    let finish: () => void = () => undefined;
    const endGrant = vi.fn(
      () =>
        new Promise<void>((_resolve, reject) => {
          finish = () => {
            reject(new Error('offline'));
          };
        }),
    );
    const tab = new LiveTabRegistry(endGrant);
    tab.opened('aaaaaaaaaaaa');
    const first = tab.beforeOpen('bbbbbbbbbbbb');
    const second = tab.beforeOpen('bbbbbbbbbbbb');
    let settled = false;
    void second.then(() => {
      settled = true;
    });
    await Promise.resolve();
    expect(settled).toBe(false);
    finish();
    await expect(first).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    await expect(second).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(endGrant).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/lib/live-tab.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { endLiveGrant } from '../api/live';

/**
 * Remembers the session of the live view of this browser tab. Before the tab opens the live
 * view of another session, it ends the grant of the previous one (spec section 9.2).
 */
export class LiveTabRegistry {
  private readonly endGrant: (sid: string) => Promise<void>;
  private lastSid: string | null = null;
  private ending: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();

  constructor(endGrant: (sid: string) => Promise<void>) {
    this.endGrant = endGrant;
  }

  /** Resolves when the grant of the previous session (if it is not `sid`) has ended. */
  beforeOpen(sid: string): Promise<void> {
    const previous = this.lastSid;
    if (previous !== null && previous !== sid) {
      this.lastSid = null;
      this.ending = this.endGrant(previous).catch(() => undefined);
    }
    return this.ending;
  }

  opened(sid: string): void {
    this.lastSid = sid;
  }
}

const LiveTabContext = createContext<LiveTabRegistry | null>(null);

export function LiveTabProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [registry] = useState(() => new LiveTabRegistry(endLiveGrant));
  return <LiveTabContext value={registry}>{children}</LiveTabContext>;
}

export function useLiveTab(): LiveTabRegistry {
  const registry = useContext(LiveTabContext);
  if (!registry) throw new Error('useLiveTab needs a LiveTabProvider');
  return registry;
}
```

`packages/web/src/lib/format.ts`:

```ts
import type { AgentActivity } from '@hydra/shared';

const dateTime = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' });
const timeWithSeconds = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
  dateStyle: 'short',
  timeStyle: 'medium',
});

/** A date and time in the local format, or "Never". */
export function formatDateTime(ms: number | null): string {
  return ms === null ? 'Never' : dateTime.format(ms);
}

/** A date and time with seconds (Activity view). */
export function formatEventTime(ms: number): string {
  return timeWithSeconds.format(ms);
}

/** For example "browser_click · ok · 230 ms" (spec section 10.2). */
export function formatToolCall(call: NonNullable<AgentActivity['lastToolCall']>): string {
  const parts = [call.tool, call.status];
  if (call.durationMs !== null) parts.push(`${String(Math.round(call.durationMs))} ms`);
  return parts.join(' · ');
}

export function formatAgentCount(count: number): string {
  if (count === 0) return 'No agent connected';
  return count === 1 ? '1 agent connected' : `${String(count)} agents connected`;
}

/** True when the allowlist permits all domains (spec section 7.2). */
export function permitsAllDomains(allowlist: readonly string[]): boolean {
  return allowlist.length === 1 && allowlist[0] === '*';
}
```

`packages/web/src/lib/mcp-config.ts`:

```ts
/** The MCP endpoint of a session (spec section 8.1). `origin` is HYDRA_PUBLIC_URL. */
export function mcpEndpoint(origin: string, sid: string): string {
  return `${origin}/s/${sid}/mcp`;
}

/**
 * The Claude Code command of spec section 10.2. `name` is the token name: the shared
 * CreateTokenRequestSchema permits only letters, digits, "-", and "_", so it is safe in a shell.
 */
export function claudeMcpAddCommand(name: string, endpoint: string, secret: string): string {
  return `claude mcp add --transport http ${name} ${endpoint} --header "Authorization: Bearer ${secret}"`;
}

/** The JSON `mcpServers` config for other MCP clients (spec section 10.2). */
export function mcpServersJson(name: string, endpoint: string, secret: string): string {
  const config = {
    mcpServers: {
      [name]: {
        type: 'http',
        url: endpoint,
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${secret}` },
      },
    },
  };
  return JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
}
```

`packages/web/src/lib/use-recent.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

/** True while `timestamp` is less than `windowMs` ago. It changes to false on time. */
export function useIsRecent(timestamp: number | null, windowMs: number): boolean {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());
  useEffect(() => {
    if (timestamp === null) return undefined;
    const remaining = timestamp + windowMs - Date.now();
    if (remaining <= 0) return undefined;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      setNow(Date.now());
    }, remaining + 50);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [timestamp, windowMs]);
  return timestamp !== null && now - timestamp < windowMs;
}
```

Test helpers: `renderApp(path)` → `{ user, router, queryClient, … }`, `mockLoggedIn(api, { me?, sessions? })`.

Spec sections 6.3, 10.2 (Login, Sidebar, user menu), and 10.3. A user without a login session goes to Login and comes back to the same page after the login. While `mustChangePassword` is true, Change password is the only screen (spec section 6.3), and the user SSE stream stays closed (the API would answer 403). The sidebar puts the sessions with `myRole` `owner` under "My sessions" and those with `operator` or `viewer` under "Shared with me"; "Admin" is only for admins. `GET /api/sessions` without `all` returns own and shared sessions only (spec section 12.1).

`packages/web/src/test/render.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import type { Me, SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { createMemoryRouter } from 'react-router';
import { RouterProvider } from 'react-router/dom';
import { createQueryClient } from '../app/query-client';
import { appRoutes } from '../routes';
import { makeMe } from './fixtures';
import type { MockApi } from './mock-api';

/** Renders the whole app at `path` with a fresh query client (no retries). */
export function renderApp(path: string) {
  const queryClient = createQueryClient({ retry: false });
  const router = createMemoryRouter(appRoutes, { initialEntries: [path] });
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  const view = render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return { ...view, user, router, queryClient };
}

/** The routes that every logged-in screen loads: the user, the session list, the sessions. */
export function mockLoggedIn(
  api: MockApi,
  options: { me?: Me; sessions?: SessionResource[] } = {},
): void {
  const sessions = options.sessions ?? [];
  api.on('GET /api/auth/me', { body: options.me ?? makeMe() });
  api.on('GET /api/sessions', { body: sessions });
  for (const session of sessions) {
    api.on(`GET /api/sessions/${session.id}`, { body: session });
  }
}
```

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeMe } from '../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, mockApi, type MockApi } from '../test/mock-api';
import { MockEventSource } from '../test/mock-event-source';
import { mockLoggedIn, renderApp } from '../test/render';

let api: MockApi;

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
});

describe('Login', () => {
  it('sends a user without a login session to Login, and back after the login', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', apiError(401, 'unauthenticated', 'Log in'));
    api.on('POST /api/auth/login', { body: makeMe() });
    api.on('GET /api/sessions', { body: [] });
    const { user, router } = renderApp('/some/page?tab=2');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Log in' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/login');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), ' ana@example.com ');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Password'), 'correct horse battery');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Log in' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('navigation', { name: 'Sidebar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/some/page');
    expect(router.state.location.search).toBe('?tab=2');
    expect(api.calls('POST /api/auth/login')[0]?.body).toEqual({
      email: 'ana@example.com',
      password: 'correct horse battery',
    });
  });

  it('shows the spec text for a failed login', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', apiError(401, 'unauthenticated', 'Log in'));
    api.on(
      'POST /api/auth/login',
      apiError(401, 'invalid_credentials', 'Invalid email or password'),
    );
    const { user } = renderApp('/login');
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Email'), 'ana@example.com');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Password'), 'wrong');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Log in' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Invalid email or password');
  });

  it('shows the server message when the login is rate limited', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', apiError(401, 'unauthenticated', 'Log in'));
    api.on(
      'POST /api/auth/login',
      apiError(429, 'rate_limited', 'Too many attempts. Wait 15 minutes.'),
    );
    const { user } = renderApp('/login');
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Email'), 'ana@example.com');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Password'), 'x');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Log in' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'Too many attempts. Wait 15 minutes.',
    );
  });

  it('opens Change password after a login with a one-time password', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', apiError(401, 'unauthenticated', 'Log in'));
    api.on('POST /api/auth/login', { body: makeMe({ mustChangePassword: true }) });
    const { user, router } = renderApp('/login');
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Email'), 'ana@example.com');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Password'), 'one-time-password');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Log in' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Change password' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/change-password');
  });

  it('shows an error with Try again when the server cannot be reached at load', async () => {
    api.on(
      'GET /api/auth/me',
      () => {
        throw new TypeError('Failed to fetch');
      },
      { body: makeMe() },
    );
    api.on('GET /api/sessions', { body: [] });
    const { user } = renderApp('/');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'The server cannot be reached. Check your connection.',
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('navigation', { name: 'Sidebar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('sends a logged-in user from Login to the start page', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api);
    const { router } = renderApp('/login');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('navigation', { name: 'Sidebar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeMe, makeSession, SID } from '../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, mockApi, type MockApi } from '../test/mock-api';
import { MockEventSource } from '../test/mock-event-source';
import { mockLoggedIn, renderApp } from '../test/render';

let api: MockApi;

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
});

describe('Change password', () => {
  it('is the only screen while mustChangePassword is true, and opens the app after it', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    // The first answer has the one-time password flag, the answers after the change do not.
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', { body: makeMe({ mustChangePassword: true }) }, { body: makeMe() });
    api.on('POST /api/auth/password', { status: 204 });
    const { user, router } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Change password' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/change-password');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('navigation', { name: 'Sidebar' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(MockEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Log out' })).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Current password'), 'one-time-password');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('New password'), 'a long new password');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Repeat the new password'), 'a long new password');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change password' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('navigation', { name: 'Sidebar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/');
    expect(api.calls('POST /api/auth/password')[0]?.body).toEqual({
      current: 'one-time-password',
      new: 'a long new password',
    });
  });

  it('checks the length and the repeat before it sends the request', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { me: makeMe({ mustChangePassword: true }) });
    const { user } = renderApp('/change-password');
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Current password'), 'old');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('New password'), 'short');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Repeat the new password'), 'short');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change password' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('12 to 128 characters');
    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('New password'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('New password'), 'a long new password');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change password' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('not the same');
    expect(api.calls('POST /api/auth/password')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('shows the password_rejected message of the server', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api);
    api.on(
      'POST /api/auth/password',
      apiError(400, 'password_rejected', 'This password is too common.'),
    );
    const { user } = renderApp('/change-password');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Current password'), 'old password 1');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('New password'), 'password1234');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Repeat the new password'), 'password1234');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change password' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('This password is too common.');
  });
});
```
