```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  makeMe,
  makeSession,
  OPERATOR_RIGHTS,
  SID,
  SID_2,
  VIEWER_RIGHTS,
} from '../../test/fixtures';
import { mockApi, type MockApi } from '../../test/mock-api';
import { MockEventSource } from '../../test/mock-event-source';
import { mockLoggedIn, renderApp } from '../../test/render';

let api: MockApi;

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
});

const SESSIONS = [
  makeSession({ name: 'Banking', state: 'running' }),
  makeSession({
    id: SID_2,
    name: 'Shop',
    state: 'stopped',
    myRole: 'viewer',
    rights: VIEWER_RIGHTS,
  }),
  makeSession({
    id: 'a2b3c4d5e6f7',
    name: 'Support',
    state: 'error',
    myRole: 'operator',
    rights: OPERATOR_RIGHTS,
  }),
];

describe('Sidebar', () => {
  it('lists own and shared sessions with their state', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: SESSIONS });
    renderApp('/');
    const mine = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'My sessions' });
    expect(await within(mine).findByRole('link', { name: /Banking/ })).toHaveTextContent(
      'RunningBanking',
    );
    const shared = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Shared with me' });
    expect(
      within(shared)
        .getAllByRole('link')
        .map((a) => a.textContent),
    ).toEqual(['StoppedShop', 'ErrorSupport']);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('region', { name: 'Admin' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the Admin section to admins', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { me: makeMe({ role: 'admin' }) });
    renderApp('/');
    const admin = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Admin' });
    expect(within(admin).getByRole('link', { name: 'Users' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'href',
      '/admin/users',
    );
    expect(within(admin).getByRole('link', { name: 'All sessions' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'href',
      '/admin/sessions',
    );
  });

  it('updates the state and the agent icon from the user SSE stream', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: SESSIONS });
    renderApp('/');
    const link = await screen.findByRole('link', { name: /Banking/ });
    const source = MockEventSource.latest('/api/events');
    act(() => {
      source.open();
      source.emit('session.state', { sessionId: SID, state: 'stopping', errorReason: null });
      source.emit('session.activity', {
        sessionId: SID,
        agentActivity: { activeAgentCount: 1, lastToolCall: null },
      });
    });
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(link).toHaveTextContent('StoppingBankingAgent connected');
    });
  });

  it('logs out from the user menu', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api);
    api.on('POST /api/auth/logout', { status: 204 });
    const { user, router } = renderApp('/');
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /Ana/ }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('ana@example.com')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Log out' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Log in' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/login');
    expect(api.calls('POST /api/auth/logout')[0]?.body).toEqual({});
  });

  it('opens Change password from the user menu', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api);
    const { user, router } = renderApp('/');
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /Ana/ }));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Change password' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Change password' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/change-password');
  });

  it('says when the sessions cannot be loaded', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', { body: makeMe() });
    api.on('GET /api/sessions', { status: 500, body: 'Internal Server Error' });
    renderApp('/');
    expect(await screen.findByText('The sessions cannot be loaded.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the guards, the panel, Login, and Change password**

Login shows the spec text `MESSAGES.invalidCredentials` for `invalid_credentials`, and the server message for other errors (for example `rate_limited`). After a good login, `useLogin` stores the user, and the redirect at the top of `LoginPage` opens the next screen; a navigation in `onSuccess` would race with it. Change password checks the length (12 to 128) and the repeat before it sends; the server checks the other rules (`password_rejected`).

`packages/web/src/routes/guards.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Me } from '@hydra/shared';
import { createContext, useContext, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router';
import { useMe } from '../api/auth';
import { errorMessage } from '../api/client';
import { Button } from '../components/os';
import { AuthPanel } from './auth-panel';
import { NotFoundPage } from './shell/NotFoundPage';

const CurrentUserContext = createContext<Me | null>(null);

/** The logged-in user. Only for components under RequireLogin. */
export function useCurrentUser(): Me {
  const me = useContext(CurrentUserContext);
  if (!me) throw new Error('useCurrentUser needs RequireLogin');
  return me;
}

export interface RequireLoginProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  /** The Change password screen is the only screen while mustChangePassword is true. */
  allowPasswordChange?: boolean;
}

/** Sends people without a login session to Login, and forces Change password (spec 10.2). */
export function RequireLogin({ children, allowPasswordChange = false }: RequireLoginProps) {
  const me = useMe();
  const location = useLocation();

  if (me.data === undefined) {
    if (me.isError) {
      return (
        <AuthPanel title="Hydra Browsers">
          <p role="alert" className="text-os-base text-os-red-accessible">
            {errorMessage(me.error)}
          </p>
          <Button
            onClick={() => {
              void me.refetch();
            }}
          >
            Try again
          </Button>
        </AuthPanel>
      );
    }
    return (
      <AuthPanel title="Hydra Browsers">
        <p role="status" className="text-os-base text-os-gray-accessible">
          Loading…
        </p>
      </AuthPanel>
    );
  }
  if (me.data === null) {
    return (
      <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: `${location.pathname}${location.search}` }} />
    );
  }
  if (me.data.mustChangePassword && !allowPasswordChange) {
    return <Navigate to="/change-password" replace />;
  }
  return <CurrentUserContext value={me.data}>{children}</CurrentUserContext>;
}

/** Admin screens. Members get the Not found page. */
export function RequireAdmin({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const me = useCurrentUser();
  if (me.role !== 'admin') return <NotFoundPage />;
  return children;
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/login.tsx`:

```tsx
import { MESSAGES } from '@hydra/shared';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router';
import { useLogin, useMe } from '../api/auth';
import { errorMessage, isApiError } from '../api/client';
import { Button, TextField } from '../components/os';
import { AuthPanel } from './auth-panel';

/** The page to open after the login: the page that sent the user to Login, or the start. */
function returnPath(state: unknown): string {
  if (typeof state === 'object' && state !== null && 'from' in state) {
    const { from } = state;
    if (typeof from === 'string' && from.startsWith('/') && !from.startsWith('//')) return from;
  }
  return '/';
}

export function LoginPage() {
  const me = useMe();
  const login = useLogin();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');

  // After a good login, useLogin stores the user, and this redirect opens the next screen.
  if (me.data) {
    const next = me.data.mustChangePassword ? '/change-password' : returnPath(location.state);
    return <Navigate to={next} replace />;
  }

  const error = login.error
    ? isApiError(login.error, 'invalid_credentials')
      ? MESSAGES.invalidCredentials
      : errorMessage(login.error)
    : null;

  return (
    <AuthPanel title="Hydra Browsers">
      <form
        className="flex flex-col gap-3"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          login.mutate({ email: email.trim(), password });
        }}
      >
        <TextField
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          autoComplete="username"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setEmail(event.target.value);
          }}
        />
        <TextField
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          autoComplete="current-password"
          required
          value={password}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setPassword(event.target.value);
          }}
        />
        {error ? (
          <p role="alert" className="text-os-xs text-os-red-accessible">
            {error}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={login.isPending}>
          Log in
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthPanel>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/change-password.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { useChangePassword, useLogout } from '../api/auth';
import { errorMessage } from '../api/client';
import { Button, TextField } from '../components/os';
import { AuthPanel } from './auth-panel';
import { useCurrentUser } from './guards';

export function ChangePasswordPage() {
  const me = useCurrentUser();
  const change = useChangePassword();
  const logout = useLogout();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState('');
  const [next, setNext] = useState('');
  const [repeat, setRepeat] = useState('');
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const forced = me.mustChangePassword;
  const error = problem ?? (change.error ? errorMessage(change.error) : null);

  return (
    <AuthPanel title="Change password">
      {forced ? (
        <p className="text-os-base text-os-foreground">
          You logged in with a one-time password. Choose a new password to continue.
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <form
        className="flex flex-col gap-3"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          if (next.length < 12 || next.length > 128) {
            setProblem('The new password must have 12 to 128 characters.');
            return;
          }
          if (next !== repeat) {
            setProblem('The two new passwords are not the same.');
            return;
          }
          setProblem(null);
          change.mutate(
            { current, new: next },
            {
              onSuccess: () => {
                void navigate('/', { replace: true });
              },
            },
          );
        }}
      >
        <TextField
          label="Current password"
          type="password"
          autoComplete="current-password"
          required
          value={current}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setCurrent(event.target.value);
          }}
        />
        <TextField
          label="New password"
          type="password"
          autoComplete="new-password"
          required
          hint="12 to 128 characters. A common password or your email is not accepted."
          value={next}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setNext(event.target.value);
          }}
        />
        <TextField
          label="Repeat the new password"
          type="password"
          autoComplete="new-password"
          required
          value={repeat}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setRepeat(event.target.value);
          }}
        />
        {error ? (
          <p role="alert" className="text-os-xs text-os-red-accessible">
            {error}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          {forced ? (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              onClick={() => {
                logout.mutate(undefined, {
                  onSettled: () => {
                    void navigate('/login', { replace: true });
                  },
                });
              }}
            >
              Log out
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <Button
              onClick={() => {
                void navigate('/');
              }}
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
          )}
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={change.isPending}>
            Change password
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </AuthPanel>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/shell/AppSidebar.tsx` (first version):

```tsx
import type { SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { IconLayoutGrid, IconUsers } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useSessions } from '../../api/sessions';
import { Sidebar, SidebarItem, SidebarNote, SidebarSection } from '../../components/os';
import { useCurrentUser } from '../guards';
import { UserMenu } from './UserMenu';

function SessionItems({
  sessions,
  empty,
}: {
  sessions: readonly SessionResource[] | undefined;
  empty: string;
}) {
  if (sessions === undefined) return null;
  if (sessions.length === 0) return <SidebarNote>{empty}</SidebarNote>;
  return sessions.map((session) => (
    <SidebarItem
      key={session.id}
      to={`/sessions/${session.id}`}
      label={session.name}
      state={session.state}
      agentConnected={session.agentActivity.activeAgentCount >= 1}
    />
  ));
}

/** "My sessions", "Shared with me", "Admin", and the user menu (spec section 10.2). */
export function AppSidebar() {
  const me = useCurrentUser();
  const sessions = useSessions();
  const mine = sessions.data?.filter((s) => s.myRole === 'owner');
  const shared = sessions.data?.filter((s) => s.myRole === 'operator' || s.myRole === 'viewer');

  return (
    <Sidebar footer={<UserMenu />}>
      <SidebarSection title="My sessions">
        {sessions.isPending ? <SidebarNote>Loading…</SidebarNote> : null}
        {sessions.isError ? <SidebarNote>The sessions cannot be loaded.</SidebarNote> : null}
        <SessionItems sessions={mine} empty="No sessions" />
      </SidebarSection>
      <SidebarSection title="Shared with me">
        <SessionItems sessions={shared} empty="Nothing is shared with you" />
      </SidebarSection>
      {me.role === 'admin' ? (
        <SidebarSection title="Admin">
          <SidebarItem to="/admin/users" label="Users" icon={IconUsers} />
          <SidebarItem to="/admin/sessions" label="All sessions" icon={IconLayoutGrid} />
        </SidebarSection>
      ) : null}
    </Sidebar>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { IconSelector, IconUserCircle } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { useLogout } from '../../api/auth';
import { Menu, MenuContent, MenuItem, MenuLabel, MenuTrigger, OsIcon } from '../../components/os';
import { useCurrentUser } from '../guards';

/** The user menu at the bottom of the sidebar: change password, logout (spec section 10.2). */
export function UserMenu() {
  const me = useCurrentUser();
  const logout = useLogout();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  return (
    <Menu>
      <MenuTrigger asChild>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="flex h-(--os-row-height) w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-os-control px-2 text-left text-os-base text-os-foreground hover:bg-os-tone"
        >
          <OsIcon icon={IconUserCircle} size={18} />
          <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{me.displayName}</span>
          <OsIcon icon={IconSelector} />
        </button>
      </MenuTrigger>
      <MenuContent align="start" side="top">
        <MenuLabel>{me.email}</MenuLabel>
        <MenuItem
          onSelect={() => {
            void navigate('/change-password');
          }}
        >
          Change password
        </MenuItem>
        <MenuItem
          onSelect={() => {
            logout.mutate(undefined, {
              onSettled: () => {
                void navigate('/login', { replace: true });
              },
            });
          }}
        >
          Log out
        </MenuItem>
      </MenuContent>
    </Menu>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/index.tsx` (first version):

```tsx
import type { RouteObject } from 'react-router';
import { ChangePasswordPage } from './change-password';
import { RequireLogin } from './guards';
import { LoginPage } from './login';
import { AppShell } from './shell/AppShell';
import { HomePage } from './shell/HomePage';
import { NotFoundPage } from './shell/NotFoundPage';

/** All routes (spec section 3.2). main.tsx and the tests build their routers from it. */
export const appRoutes: RouteObject[] = [
  { path: '/login', element: <LoginPage /> },
  {
    path: '/change-password',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin allowPasswordChange>
        <ChangePasswordPage />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
  },
  {
    path: '/',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin>
        <AppShell />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
    children: [
      { index: true, element: <HomePage /> },
      { path: '*', element: <NotFoundPage /> },
    ],
  },
];
```

`packages/web/src/main.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { StrictMode } from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { createBrowserRouter } from 'react-router';
import { RouterProvider } from 'react-router/dom';
import { createQueryClient } from './app/query-client';
import { appRoutes } from './routes';
import './theme/global.css';

const queryClient = createQueryClient();
const router = createBrowserRouter(appRoutes);

const container = document.getElementById('root');
if (!container) throw new Error('index.html has no #root element');

createRoot(container).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </QueryClientProvider>
  </StrictMode>,
);
```

### Task 12: Session page: layout, toolbar, Delete, and the Live view

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/views.ts`, `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/outlet.ts`, `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/layout.tsx`, `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/SessionToolbar.tsx`, `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/DeleteSessionSheet.tsx`, `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/live.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/web/src/routes/index.tsx` (adds `sessions/:sid` with the index route and `live`)
- Test: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/layout.test.tsx`, `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/live.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3 to 11 (`useSession`, `useSessionAction`, `useDeleteSession`, `createLiveTicket`, `useLiveTab`, `useEventsReconnectCount`, `useIsRecent`, the formatters, `NotFoundPage`); `MESSAGES` of `@hydra/shared`.
- Produces: `SESSION_VIEWS`, `SessionView`, `SessionViewDef`, `allowedViews(rights)`, `viewFromPath(pathname)`; `useSessionResource(): SessionResource` (the outlet context of the session page); `SessionLayout`, `SessionIndex`; `SessionToolbar({ session, view, views })`; `DeleteSessionSheet({ session, open, onOpenChange, returnFocusRef })`; `LiveView`, `AGENT_ACTIVE_MS`, `LIVE_IFRAME_ALLOW`, `LIVE_IFRAME_SANDBOX`.

The toolbar of spec section 10.2: name and state, the views that the user has rights for (Live `live.watch`, Tokens `token.create`, Sharing and Settings `session.read`, Activity `audit.read`), Start or Retry start (`stopped`, `error`), Stop, Restart (`starting`, `running`), and a "More" menu with Delete (`session.delete`). A URL of a view without the right redirects to the first allowed view. A deleted session shows Not found.

The Live view (spec sections 9.2 and 9.4): when the session is `running`, the stage asks `LiveTabRegistry.beforeOpen(sid)` (which ends the grant of the previous session of the browser tab), gets a ticket, and loads the iframe. A new ticket comes when the session runs again, after a reconnect of the user SSE stream, when the `live.control` right changes (another Neko profile), and with "Try again". When the session is not running, the area shows the state, the error reason, and Start or Retry start for `session.lifecycle`. Banners: "Agent active" (last tool call less than 10 s ago), "More than one agent is connected", "Allowlist permits all domains". The status bar shows the agents, the last tool call, and the full-screen button. The iframe keeps the aspect ratio of the session screen with container query units (`100cqw`, `100cqh`).

```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeMe, makeSession, SID, VIEWER_RIGHTS } from '../../test/fixtures';

let api: MockApi;

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
  api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`, {
    body: { url: `http://live.hydra.localhost/s/${SID}/?ticket=t1` },
  });
});

function views() {
  return within(screen.getByRole('radiogroup', { name: 'Views' }))
    .getAllByRole('radio')
    .map((radio) => radio.textContent);
}

describe('Session toolbar', () => {
  it('shows all views and the lifecycle buttons to the owner', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/stop`, { body: makeSession({ state: 'stopping' }) });
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Banking' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(views()).toEqual(['Live', 'Tokens', 'Sharing', 'Activity', 'Settings']);
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Live' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'More' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Stop' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByRole('banner')).toHaveTextContent('Stopping');
    });
    expect(api.calls(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/stop`)[0]?.body).toEqual({});
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Stop' })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Restart' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('shows Retry start for a session in error, and the error of a failed start', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, {
      sessions: [makeSession({ state: 'error', errorReason: 'ImagePullBackOff' })],
    });
    api.on(
      `POST /api/sessions/${SID}/start`,
      apiError(409, 'capacity_reached', '10 sessions hold a media port.'),
    );
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Banking' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const toolbar = screen.getByRole('banner');

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('10 sessions hold a media port.');
  });

  it('opens the URL of a view with the segmented control', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });

    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe(`/sessions/${SID}/sharing`);
  });

  it('limits a viewer to the views and buttons of the viewer rights', async () => {

    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);

    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Restart' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'More' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

  it('opens the first allowed view for /sessions/:sid', async () => {
```

```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { makeSession, OPERATOR_RIGHTS, SID, SID_2, VIEWER_RIGHTS } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, mockApi, type MockApi } from '../../test/mock-api';
import { MockEventSource } from '../../test/mock-event-source';
import { mockLoggedIn, renderApp } from '../../test/render';

let api: MockApi;

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
});

function ticketUrl(sid: string, ticket: string) {
  return `http://live.hydra.localhost/s/${sid}/?ticket=${ticket}`;
}

describe('Live view', () => {
  it('opens the iframe with the ticket URL and the attributes of spec section 9.4', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, {
      sessions: [makeSession({ settings: { ...makeSession().settings, screen: '1600x900' } })],
    });
    api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`, { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 't1') } });
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    const frame = await screen.findByTitle('Live view of Banking');
    expect(frame).toHaveAttribute('src', ticketUrl(SID, 't1'));
    expect(frame).toHaveAttribute('allow', 'clipboard-read; clipboard-write; fullscreen; autoplay');
    expect(frame).toHaveAttribute(
      'sandbox',
      'allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-pointer-lock',
    );
    expect(frame.style.aspectRatio).toBe('1600 / 900');
    expect(api.calls(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`)[0]?.body).toEqual({});
  });

  it('shows the banners and the status bar from agentActivity and the allowlist', async () => {
    const session = makeSession({
      settings: { ...makeSession().settings, allowlist: ['*'] },
      agentActivity: {
        activeAgentCount: 2,
        lastToolCall: {
          tool: 'browser_click',
          status: 'ok',
          durationMs: 230,
          startedAt: Date.now(),
        },
      },
    });
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [session] });
    api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`, { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 't1') } });
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Agent active')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('More than one agent is connected')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Allowlist permits all domains')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('2 agents connected')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('browser_click · ok · 230 ms')).toHaveClass('font-mono');
  });

  it('does not show "Agent active" for an old tool call', async () => {
    const session = makeSession({
      agentActivity: {
        activeAgentCount: 1,
        lastToolCall: {
          tool: 'browser_type',
          status: 'error',
          durationMs: 12,
          startedAt: Date.now() - 60_000,
        },
      },
    });
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [session] });
    api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`, { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 't1') } });
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    expect(await screen.findByText('1 agent connected')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Agent active')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('opens the stage in full screen', async () => {
    const requestFullscreen = vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve());
    Object.defineProperty(HTMLElement.prototype, 'requestFullscreen', {
      configurable: true,
      value: requestFullscreen,
    });
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`, { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 't1') } });
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    await screen.findByTitle('Live view of Banking');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Full screen' }));
    expect(requestFullscreen).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(requestFullscreen.mock.contexts[0]).toBe(screen.getByTestId('live-stage'));
  });

  it('ends the grant of the previous session before it opens another one (spec 9.2)', async () => {
    const other = makeSession({ id: SID_2, name: 'Shop' });
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession(), other] });
    api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`, { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 'a') } });
    api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID_2}/live-ticket`, { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID_2, 'b') } });
    api.on(`DELETE /api/sessions/${SID}/live-grant`, { status: 204 });
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    await screen.findByTitle('Live view of Banking');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('link', { name: /Shop/ }));
    await screen.findByTitle('Live view of Shop');
    expect(screen.queryByTitle('Live view of Banking')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    const order = api.requests
      .filter((r) => r.path.includes('live-'))
      .map((r) => `${r.method} ${r.path}`);
    expect(order).toEqual([
      `POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`,
      `DELETE /api/sessions/${SID}/live-grant`,
      `POST /api/sessions/${SID_2}/live-ticket`,
    ]);
    expect(api.calls(`DELETE /api/sessions/${SID}/live-grant`)[0]?.body).toEqual({});
  });

  it('gets a new ticket after a reconnect of the user SSE stream', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(
      `POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`,
      { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 'first') } },
      { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 'second') } },
    );
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    const frame = await screen.findByTitle('Live view of Banking');
    expect(frame).toHaveAttribute('src', ticketUrl(SID, 'first'));
    const source = MockEventSource.latest('/api/events');
    act(() => {
      source.open();
      source.drop();
      source.open();
    });
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTitle('Live view of Banking')).toHaveAttribute(
        'src',
        ticketUrl(SID, 'second'),
      );
    });
  });

  it('gets a new ticket when the live.control right changes', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession({ myRole: 'operator', rights: OPERATOR_RIGHTS })] });
    api.on(
      `POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`,
      { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 'operator') } },
      { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 'viewer') } },
    );
    const { queryClient } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    await screen.findByTitle('Live view of Banking');
    act(() => {
      queryClient.setQueryData(
        ['sessions', 'detail', SID],
        makeSession({ myRole: 'viewer', rights: VIEWER_RIGHTS }),
      );
    });
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTitle('Live view of Banking')).toHaveAttribute(
        'src',
        ticketUrl(SID, 'viewer'),
      );
    });
  });

  it('removes the iframe while the session restarts and gets a new ticket when it runs again', async () => {
    let state: 'running' | 'starting' = 'running';
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}`, () => ({ body: makeSession({ state }) }));
    api.on(
      `POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`,
      { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 'before') } },
      { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 'after') } },
    );
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    await screen.findByTitle('Live view of Banking');
    const source = MockEventSource.latest('/api/events');
    state = 'starting';
    act(() => {
      source.open();
      source.emit('session.state', { sessionId: SID, state: 'starting', errorReason: null });
    });
    expect(await screen.findByText(/The session is starting/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByTitle('Live view of Banking')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    state = 'running';
    act(() => {
      source.emit('session.state', { sessionId: SID, state: 'running', errorReason: null });
    });
    expect(await screen.findByTitle('Live view of Banking')).toHaveAttribute(
      'src',
      ticketUrl(SID, 'after'),
    );
  });

  it('shows the state and Start when the session is not running', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession({ state: 'stopped' })] });
    api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/start`, { body: makeSession({ state: 'starting' }) });
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    expect(await screen.findByText('The session is stopped.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByTitle('Live view of Banking')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    const starts = screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: 'Start' });
    expect(starts).toHaveLength(2);
    await user.click(starts[1] as HTMLElement);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/The session is starting/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(api.calls(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`)).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Full screen' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('shows the error reason and no Start button to a viewer', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, {
      sessions: [
        makeSession({
          state: 'error',
          errorReason: 'ErrImagePull: not found',
          myRole: 'viewer',
          rights: VIEWER_RIGHTS,
        }),
      ],
    });
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    expect(await screen.findByText('ErrImagePull: not found')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Retry start' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows a failed ticket request and tries again', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(
      `POST /api/sessions/${SID}/live-ticket`,
      apiError(409, 'invalid_state', 'The session is not running.'),
      { body: { url: ticketUrl(SID, 't2') } },
    );
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('The session is not running.');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' }));
    expect(await screen.findByTitle('Live view of Banking')).toHaveAttribute(
      'src',
      ticketUrl(SID, 't2'),
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the views and the outlet hook**

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/views.ts`:

```ts
import type { Action } from '@hydra/shared';

/** The five views of a session and the right that each view needs (spec sections 6.5, 10.2). */
export const SESSION_VIEWS = [
  { value: 'live', label: 'Live', right: 'live.watch' },
  { value: 'tokens', label: 'Tokens', right: 'token.create' },
  { value: 'sharing', label: 'Sharing', right: 'session.read' },
  { value: 'activity', label: 'Activity', right: 'audit.read' },
  { value: 'settings', label: 'Settings', right: 'session.read' },
] as const satisfies readonly { value: string; label: string; right: Action }[];

export type SessionViewDef = (typeof SESSION_VIEWS)[number];
export type SessionView = SessionViewDef['value'];

export function allowedViews(rights: readonly Action[]): SessionViewDef[] {
  return SESSION_VIEWS.filter((view) => rights.includes(view.right));
}

/** The view of a path like /sessions/<sid>/tokens, or null. */
export function viewFromPath(pathname: string): SessionView | null {
  const last = pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean).at(-1);
  return SESSION_VIEWS.find((view) => view.value === last)?.value ?? null;
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/outlet.ts`:

```ts
import type { SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router';

/** The session of the current page. SessionLayout passes it to the views. */
export function useSessionResource(): SessionResource {
  return useOutletContext<SessionResource>();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the toolbar, the Delete sheet, and the layout**

The Delete sheet opens from a menu item, so it returns the focus to the "More" button (`returnFocusRef`). `SessionToolbar` gets `key={session.id}`, so an action error of one session does not show on another one.

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/SessionToolbar.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Action, SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { IconDots } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessage } from '../../api/client';
import { useSessionAction } from '../../api/sessions';
import {
  Banner,
  Button,
  IconButton,
  Menu,
  MenuContent,
  MenuItem,
  MenuTrigger,
  OsIcon,
  SegmentedControl,
  StatusDot,
  Toolbar,
} from '../../components/os';
import { DeleteSessionSheet } from './DeleteSessionSheet';
import type { SessionView, SessionViewDef } from './views';

export interface SessionToolbarProps {
  session: SessionResource;
  view: SessionView | null;
  views: readonly SessionViewDef[];
}

/** Name and state, the views, Start/Stop, Restart, and Delete (spec section 10.2). */
export function SessionToolbar({ session, view, views }: SessionToolbarProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const action = useSessionAction(session.id);
  const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = useState(false);
  const moreRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const can = (right: Action) => session.rights.includes(right);
  const { state } = session;
  const startable = state === 'stopped' || state === 'error';
  const busy = action.isPending;

  return (
    <>
      <Toolbar
        title={session.name}
        subtitle={<StatusDot state={state} showLabel />}
        center={
          view ? (
            <SegmentedControl
              label="Views"
              value={view}
              segments={views}
              onValueChange={(next) => {
                void navigate(`/sessions/${session.id}/${next}`);
              }}
            />
          ) : null
        }
        actions={
          <>
            {can('session.lifecycle') ? (
              <>
                {startable ? (
                  <Button
                    variant="primary"
                    disabled={busy}
                    onClick={() => {
                      action.mutate('start');
                    }}
                  >
                    {state === 'error' ? 'Retry start' : 'Start'}
                  </Button>
                ) : (
                  <Button
                    disabled={busy || state === 'stopping' || state === 'deleting'}
                    onClick={() => {
                      action.mutate('stop');
                    }}
                  >
                    Stop
                  </Button>
                )}
                <Button
                  disabled={busy || (state !== 'starting' && state !== 'running')}
                  onClick={() => {
                    action.mutate('restart');
                  }}
                >
                  Restart
                </Button>
              </>
            ) : null}
            {can('session.delete') ? (
              <Menu>
                <MenuTrigger asChild>
                  <IconButton ref={moreRef} size="default" aria-label="More">
                    <OsIcon icon={IconDots} size={18} />
                  </IconButton>
                </MenuTrigger>
                <MenuContent>
                  <MenuItem
                    destructive
                    onSelect={() => {
                      setDeleteOpen(true);
                    }}
                  >
                    Delete
                  </MenuItem>
                </MenuContent>
              </Menu>
            ) : null}
          </>
        }
      />
      {action.error ? (
        <div className="border-b border-os-separator px-4 py-2">
          <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
            {errorMessage(action.error)}
          </Banner>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {can('session.delete') ? (
        <DeleteSessionSheet
          session={session}
          open={deleteOpen}
          onOpenChange={setDeleteOpen}
          returnFocusRef={moreRef}
        />
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/DeleteSessionSheet.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { useState, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessage } from '../../api/client';
import { useDeleteSession } from '../../api/sessions';
import { Button, Sheet, SheetBody, SheetFooter, TextField } from '../../components/os';

export interface DeleteSessionSheetProps {
  session: SessionResource;
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  returnFocusRef: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>;
}

/** The user types the exact session name (spec sections 7.9 and 10.2). */
export function DeleteSessionSheet({
  session,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  returnFocusRef,
}: DeleteSessionSheetProps) {
  const remove = useDeleteSession(session.id);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [confirmName, setConfirmName] = useState('');
  const matches = confirmName === session.name;

  function close() {
    onOpenChange(false);
    setConfirmName('');
    remove.reset();
  }

  return (
    <Sheet
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (!next) close();
      }}
      title={`Delete “${session.name}”?`}
      description="Hydra stops the browser, revokes all agent tokens, and deletes the profile with its logins. You cannot undo this."
      returnFocusRef={returnFocusRef}
    >
      <form
        className="flex min-h-0 flex-col"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          if (!matches) return;
          remove.mutate(confirmName, {
            onSuccess: () => {
              onOpenChange(false);
              void navigate('/', { replace: true });
            },
          });
        }}
      >
        <SheetBody>
          <TextField
            label="Type the session name to confirm"
            autoComplete="off"
            value={confirmName}
            error={remove.error ? errorMessage(remove.error) : null}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setConfirmName(event.target.value);
            }}
          />
        </SheetBody>
        <SheetFooter>
          <Button onClick={close}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="destructive" disabled={!matches || remove.isPending}>
            Delete
          </Button>
        </SheetFooter>
      </form>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/layout.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation, useParams } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessage, isApiError } from '../../api/client';
import { useSession } from '../../api/sessions';
import { Banner, ContentPane, Toolbar } from '../../components/os';
import { NotFoundPage } from '../shell/NotFoundPage';
import { useSessionResource } from './outlet';
import { SessionToolbar } from './SessionToolbar';
import { allowedViews, viewFromPath } from './views';

/** /sessions/:sid: the toolbar and the selected view. A view without the right redirects. */
export function SessionLayout() {
  const { sid = '' } = useParams();
  const session = useSession(sid);
  const location = useLocation();

  if (session.data === undefined) {
    if (session.isError) {
      if (isApiError(session.error, 'not_found')) return <NotFoundPage />;
      return (
        <>
          <Toolbar title="Session" />
          <ContentPane className="p-6">
            <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
              {errorMessage(session.error)}
            </Banner>
          </ContentPane>
        </>
      );
    }
    return (
      <>
        <Toolbar title="Loading…" />
        <ContentPane />
      </>
    );
  }

  // A deleted session (SSE event or 404) has no page any more.
  if (session.data.state === 'deleted') return <NotFoundPage />;

  const views = allowedViews(session.data.rights);
  const view = viewFromPath(location.pathname);
  if (view !== null && !views.some((v) => v.value === view)) {
    const first = views[0];
    return <Navigate to={first ? `/sessions/${sid}/${first.value}` : '/'} replace />;
  }

  return (
    <>
      <SessionToolbar key={session.data.id} session={session.data} view={view} views={views} />
      <ContentPane>
        <Outlet context={session.data} />
      </ContentPane>
    </>
  );
}

/** /sessions/:sid opens the first view that the user has rights for. */
export function SessionIndex() {
  const session = useSessionResource();
  const first = allowedViews(session.rights)[0];
  return <Navigate to={first ? first.value : '/'} replace />;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the Live view**

The ticket is component state, not a query. `cancelled()` is a function, so TypeScript does not narrow it to a constant between the `await` calls. `LiveStage` has `key={session.id}`, so a new session mounts a new stage.

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/live.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Screen, SessionResource, SessionState } from '@hydra/shared';
import { MESSAGES } from '@hydra/shared';
import { IconMaximize } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { errorMessage } from '../../api/client';
import { createLiveTicket } from '../../api/live';
import { useSessionAction } from '../../api/sessions';
import { Banner, Button, IconButton, OsIcon, StatusDot } from '../../components/os';
import { useEventsReconnectCount } from '../../lib/events';
import { formatAgentCount, formatToolCall, permitsAllDomains } from '../../lib/format';
import { useLiveTab } from '../../lib/live-tab';
import { useIsRecent } from '../../lib/use-recent';
import { useSessionResource } from './outlet';

/** "Agent active" while the last tool call started less than 10 s ago (spec section 9.4). */
export const AGENT_ACTIVE_MS = 10_000;

/** The iframe attributes of spec section 9.4. */
export const LIVE_IFRAME_ALLOW = 'clipboard-read; clipboard-write; fullscreen; autoplay';
export const LIVE_IFRAME_SANDBOX = 'allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-pointer-lock';

const SCREEN_SIZES: Record<Screen, { width: number; height: number }> = {
  '1280x720': { width: 1280, height: 720 },
  '1600x900': { width: 1600, height: 900 },
  '1920x1080': { width: 1920, height: 1080 },
};

const STATE_TEXT: Record<SessionState, string> = {
  stopped: 'The session is stopped.',
  starting: 'The session is starting. The live view opens when it is running.',
  running: 'The session is running.',
  stopping: 'The session is stopping.',
  error: 'The session could not start.',
  deleting: 'The session is being deleted.',
  deleted: 'The session was deleted.',
};

interface TicketState {
  key: string;
  url: string | null;
  error: string | null;
}

/**
 * The open flow of spec section 9.2: end the grant of the previous session of this browser
 * tab, then get a one-use ticket URL. A new `renewKey` gets a new ticket.
 */
function useLiveTicket(
  sid: string,
  renewKey: string,
): { url: string | null; error: string | null } {
  const tab = useLiveTab();
  const [ticket, setTicket] = useState<TicketState>({ key: '', url: null, error: null });

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const cancelled = () => controller.signal.aborted;
    const open = async () => {
      try {
        await tab.beforeOpen(sid);
        if (cancelled()) return;
        const url = await createLiveTicket(sid, controller.signal);
        if (cancelled()) return;
        tab.opened(sid);
        setTicket({ key: renewKey, url, error: null });
      } catch (error) {
        if (!cancelled()) setTicket({ key: renewKey, url: null, error: errorMessage(error) });
      }
    };
    void open();
    return () => {
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [tab, sid, renewKey]);

  return ticket.key === renewKey ? ticket : { url: null, error: null };
}

function LiveStage({
  session,
  stageRef,
}: {
  session: SessionResource;
  stageRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>;
}) {
  const reconnects = useEventsReconnectCount();
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
  // A new Neko profile (live.control), an SSE reconnect, or "Try again" needs a new ticket.
  const profile = session.rights.includes('live.control') ? 'control' : 'watch';
  const ticket = useLiveTicket(session.id, `${profile}:${String(reconnects)}:${String(attempt)}`);
  const { width, height } = SCREEN_SIZES[session.settings.screen];

  return (
    <div
      ref={stageRef}
      data-testid="live-stage"
      className="@container-size grid min-h-0 flex-1 place-items-center bg-os-black"
    >
      {ticket.url ? (
        <iframe
          key={ticket.url}
          title={`Live view of ${session.name}`}
          src={ticket.url}
          allow={LIVE_IFRAME_ALLOW}
          sandbox={LIVE_IFRAME_SANDBOX}
          className="block border-0"
          style={{
            aspectRatio: `${String(width)} / ${String(height)}`,
            width: `min(100cqw, calc(100cqh * ${String(width)} / ${String(height)}))`,
          }}
        />
      ) : ticket.error ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 text-center">
          <p role="alert" className="text-os-base text-os-on-fill">
            {ticket.error}
          </p>
          <Button
            variant="primary"
            onClick={() => {
              setAttempt((n) => n + 1);
            }}
          >
            Try again
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p role="status" className="text-os-base text-os-on-fill">
          Connecting…
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function NotRunning({ session }: { session: SessionResource }) {
  const action = useSessionAction(session.id);
  const { state } = session;
  const canStart =
    session.rights.includes('session.lifecycle') && (state === 'stopped' || state === 'error');
  return (
    <div className="grid min-h-0 flex-1 place-items-center bg-os-surface p-6">
      <div className="flex max-w-120 flex-col items-center gap-3 text-center">
        <StatusDot state={state} showLabel />
        <p className="text-os-base text-os-foreground">{STATE_TEXT[state]}</p>
        {state === 'error' && session.errorReason ? (
          <p className="font-mono text-os-sm break-all text-os-red-accessible">
            {session.errorReason}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {canStart ? (
          <Button
            variant="primary"
            disabled={action.isPending}
            onClick={() => {
              action.mutate('start');
            }}
          >
            {state === 'error' ? 'Retry start' : 'Start'}
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        {action.error ? (
          <p role="alert" className="text-os-xs text-os-red-accessible">
            {errorMessage(action.error)}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** The Live view (spec sections 9.4 and 10.2). */
export function LiveView() {
  const session = useSessionResource();
  const stageRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const { activeAgentCount, lastToolCall } = session.agentActivity;
  const agentActive = useIsRecent(lastToolCall?.startedAt ?? null, AGENT_ACTIVE_MS);
  const running = session.state === 'running';
  const allowAll = permitsAllDomains(session.settings.allowlist);

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
      {agentActive || activeAgentCount > 1 || allowAll ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-b border-os-separator p-3">
          {agentActive ? <Banner variant="info">{MESSAGES.agentActive}</Banner> : null}
          {activeAgentCount > 1 ? (
            <Banner variant="warning">{MESSAGES.multipleAgents}</Banner>
          ) : null}
          {allowAll ? <Banner variant="warning">{MESSAGES.allowAll}</Banner> : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {running ? (
        <LiveStage key={session.id} session={session} stageRef={stageRef} />
      ) : (
        <NotRunning session={session} />
      )}
      <div className="flex h-(--os-statusbar-height) shrink-0 items-center gap-4 border-t border-os-separator bg-os-surface px-3 text-os-xs text-os-gray-accessible">
        <span>{formatAgentCount(activeAgentCount)}</span>
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-mono">
          {lastToolCall ? formatToolCall(lastToolCall) : 'No tool call yet'}
        </span>
        <IconButton
          aria-label="Full screen"
          disabled={!running}
          onClick={() => {
            stageRef.current?.requestFullscreen().catch(() => undefined);
          }}
        >
          <OsIcon icon={IconMaximize} />
        </IconButton>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { RouteObject } from 'react-router';
import { ChangePasswordPage } from './change-password';
import { RequireLogin } from './guards';
import { LoginPage } from './login';
import { SessionIndex, SessionLayout } from './sessions/layout';
import { LiveView } from './sessions/live';
import { AppShell } from './shell/AppShell';
import { HomePage } from './shell/HomePage';
import { NotFoundPage } from './shell/NotFoundPage';

/** All routes (spec section 3.2). main.tsx and the tests build their routers from it. */
export const appRoutes: RouteObject[] = [
  { path: '/login', element: <LoginPage /> },
  {
    path: '/change-password',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin allowPasswordChange>
        <ChangePasswordPage />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
  },
  {
    path: '/',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin>
        <AppShell />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
    children: [
      { index: true, element: <HomePage /> },
      {
        path: 'sessions/:sid',
        element: <SessionLayout />,
        children: [
          { index: true, element: <SessionIndex /> },
          { path: 'live', element: <LiveView /> },
        ],
      },
      { path: '*', element: <NotFoundPage /> },
    ],
  },
];
```

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/session-form.tsx`, `packages/web/src/routes/shell/NewSessionSheet.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/web/src/routes/shell/AppSidebar.tsx` (the New session button and the sheet)
- Test: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/session-form.test.ts`, `packages/web/src/routes/shell/NewSessionSheet.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3 to 12 (`useCreateSession`, `ChipInput`, `RadioGroup`, `Switch`, `ALLOWLIST_HELP`); `CreateSessionRequestSchema`, `UpdateSessionRequestSchema`, `SCREENS` of `@hydra/shared`.
- Produces: `SessionForm`, `SessionFormErrors`, `FormResult<T>`, `EMPTY_SESSION_FORM`, `SCREEN_OPTIONS`, `issuesToErrors(issues)`, `serverFieldErrors(fields)`, `toCreateRequest(form, start)`, `formFromSession(session)`, `toUpdateRequest(form, session)` (only the changed fields), `SessionFields({ value, onChange, errors, disabled?, serverDefaults? })` (Settings uses them in Task 17); `NewSessionSheet({ open, onOpenChange })`.

The New session sheet of spec section 10.2: name, allowlist (chip input), timezone (with the IANA names as suggestions), language, screen, and "Start now". An empty timezone or language uses the server default (`HYDRA_DEFAULT_TIMEZONE`, `HYDRA_DEFAULT_LANGUAGE`, spec sections 4 and 7.2). The sheet checks the request with the shared schema before it sends, and shows the `fields` of a `validation_failed` answer at the fields. After a create it opens the Live view of the new session.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeSession } from '../../test/fixtures';
import {
  EMPTY_SESSION_FORM,
  formFromSession,
  serverFieldErrors,
  toCreateRequest,
  toUpdateRequest,
} from './session-form';

describe('session form', () => {
  it('leaves out an empty timezone and language, so the server defaults apply', () => {
    const result = toCreateRequest(
      { ...EMPTY_SESSION_FORM, name: ' Banking ', allowlist: ['example.com'] },
      true,
    );
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      data: { name: 'Banking', allowlist: ['example.com'], screen: '1920x1080', start: true },
    });
  });

  it('reports the fields that fail the shared schema', () => {
    const result = toCreateRequest(
      { ...EMPTY_SESSION_FORM, allowlist: ['*', 'example.com'] },
      true,
    );
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(Object.keys(result.errors).sort()).toEqual(['allowlist', 'name']);
    }
  });

  it('puts only the changed fields into the patch', () => {
    const session = makeSession();
    const form = { ...formFromSession(session), screen: '1280x720' as const };
    expect(toUpdateRequest(form, session)).toEqual({ ok: true, data: { screen: '1280x720' } });
    expect(toUpdateRequest(formFromSession(session), session)).toEqual({ ok: true, data: {} });
  });

  it('maps the fields of a validation_failed answer', () => {
    expect(serverFieldErrors({ 'allowlist.2': 'Not a host', other: 'x' })).toEqual({
      allowlist: 'Not a host',
    });
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeSession, SID } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, mockApi, type MockApi } from '../../test/mock-api';
import { MockEventSource } from '../../test/mock-event-source';
import { mockLoggedIn, renderApp } from '../../test/render';

let api: MockApi;

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
});

async function openSheet() {
  const app = renderApp('/');
  await app.user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'New session' }));
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'New session' });
  return { ...app, dialog };
}

describe('New session sheet', () => {
  it('creates a session and opens its Live view', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api);
    const created = makeSession({ state: 'starting' });
    api.on('POST /api/sessions', { status: 201, body: created });
    api.on(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}`, { body: created });
    const { user, dialog, router } = await openSheet();
    const form = within(dialog);
    await user.type(form.getByLabelText('Name'), 'Banking');
    await user.type(form.getByLabelText('Allowlist'), 'example.com{Enter}.bank.example{Enter}');
    await user.type(form.getByLabelText('Timezone'), 'Europe/Rome');
    await user.click(form.getByRole('radio', { name: '1600 × 900' }));
    await user.click(form.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Start now' }));
    await user.click(form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Banking' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    expect(api.calls('POST /api/sessions')[0]?.body).toEqual({
      name: 'Banking',
      allowlist: ['example.com', '.bank.example'],
      timezone: 'Europe/Rome',
      screen: '1600x900',
      start: false,
    });
  });

  it('checks the fields before it sends the request', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api);
    const { user, dialog } = await openSheet();
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' }));
    expect(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Name')).toHaveAccessibleDescription(
      'Use 1 to 64 characters.',
    );
    expect(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Allowlist')).toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
    expect(api.calls('POST /api/sessions')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('rejects a pasted URL in the allowlist before it sends anything', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api);
    const { user, dialog } = await openSheet();
    const form = within(dialog);
    await user.type(form.getByLabelText('Name'), 'Bank');
    await user.type(form.getByLabelText('Allowlist'), 'https://bank.example/login{Enter}');
    await user.click(form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' }));
    expect(form.getByLabelText('Allowlist')).toHaveAccessibleDescription(
      'Add 1 to 100 hosts, for example example.com or .example.com, or only *.',
    );
    expect(api.calls('POST /api/sessions')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('shows the field errors and the other errors of the server', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api);
    api.on(
      'POST /api/sessions',
      apiError(400, 'validation_failed', 'Invalid input', { timezone: 'Unknown timezone' }),
      apiError(409, 'capacity_reached', '10 sessions are running. Stop one first.'),
    );
    const { user, dialog } = await openSheet();
    const form = within(dialog);
    await user.type(form.getByLabelText('Name'), 'Shop');
    await user.type(form.getByLabelText('Allowlist'), 'shop.example{Enter}');
    await user.click(form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' }));
    expect(await form.findByText('Unknown timezone')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' }));
    expect(await form.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('10 sessions are running');
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/session-form.tsx`:

```tsx
import {
  CreateSessionRequestSchema,
  SCREENS,
  UpdateSessionRequestSchema,
  type CreateSessionRequest,
  type Screen,
  type SessionResource,
  type UpdateSessionRequest,
} from '@hydra/shared';
import { useId } from 'react';
import { ChipInput, RadioGroup, TextField } from '../../components/os';
import { ALLOWLIST_HELP } from '../../lib/allowlist-help';

export interface SessionForm {
  name: string;
  allowlist: string[];
  timezone: string;
  language: string;
  screen: Screen;
}

export type SessionFormErrors = Partial<Record<keyof SessionForm, string>>;

export type FormResult<T> = { ok: true; data: T } | { ok: false; errors: SessionFormErrors };

export const EMPTY_SESSION_FORM: SessionForm = {
  name: '',
  allowlist: [],
  timezone: '',
  language: '',
  screen: '1920x1080',
};

export const SCREEN_OPTIONS = SCREENS.map((screen) => ({
  value: screen,
  label: screen.replace('x', ' × '),
}));

const FIELD_MESSAGES: Record<keyof SessionForm, string> = {
  name: 'Use 1 to 64 characters.',
  allowlist: 'Add 1 to 100 hosts, for example example.com or .example.com, or only *.',
  timezone: 'Use an IANA timezone, for example Europe/Rome.',
  language: 'Use a language tag, for example en-US.',
  screen: 'Choose a screen size.',
};

const TIMEZONES = Intl.supportedValuesOf('timeZone');

function isField(key: PropertyKey | undefined): key is keyof SessionForm {
  return typeof key === 'string' && key in FIELD_MESSAGES;
}

/** Field errors from the zod issues of the client check. */
export function issuesToErrors(
  issues: readonly { path: readonly PropertyKey[] }[],
): SessionFormErrors {
  const errors: SessionFormErrors = {};
  for (const issue of issues) {
    const key = issue.path[0];
    if (isField(key)) errors[key] = FIELD_MESSAGES[key];
  }
  return errors;
}

/** Field errors from `fields` of a validation_failed answer (spec section 12.2). */
export function serverFieldErrors(fields: Readonly<Record<string, string>>): SessionFormErrors {
  const errors: SessionFormErrors = {};
  for (const [key, message] of Object.entries(fields)) {
    const field = key.split('.')[0];
    if (isField(field)) errors[field] = message;
  }
  return errors;
}

/** The New session request. An empty timezone or language uses the server default. */
export function toCreateRequest(
  form: SessionForm,
  start: boolean,
): FormResult<CreateSessionRequest> {
  const request = {
    name: form.name.trim(),
    allowlist: form.allowlist,
    screen: form.screen,
    start,
    ...(form.timezone.trim() === '' ? {} : { timezone: form.timezone.trim() }),
    ...(form.language.trim() === '' ? {} : { language: form.language.trim() }),
  };
  const parsed = CreateSessionRequestSchema.safeParse(request);
  return parsed.success
    ? { ok: true, data: parsed.data }
    : { ok: false, errors: issuesToErrors(parsed.error.issues) };
}

export function formFromSession(session: SessionResource): SessionForm {
  return { name: session.name, ...session.settings, allowlist: [...session.settings.allowlist] };
}

/** The PATCH body with only the changed fields. */
export function toUpdateRequest(
  form: SessionForm,
  session: SessionResource,
): FormResult<UpdateSessionRequest> {
  const current = formFromSession(session);
  const request: UpdateSessionRequest = {};
  const name = form.name.trim();
  if (name !== current.name) request.name = name;
  if (form.allowlist.join('\n') !== current.allowlist.join('\n'))
    request.allowlist = form.allowlist;
  if (form.timezone.trim() !== current.timezone) request.timezone = form.timezone.trim();
  if (form.language.trim() !== current.language) request.language = form.language.trim();
  if (form.screen !== current.screen) request.screen = form.screen;
  const parsed = UpdateSessionRequestSchema.safeParse(request);
  return parsed.success
    ? { ok: true, data: parsed.data }
    : { ok: false, errors: issuesToErrors(parsed.error.issues) };
}

export interface SessionFieldsProps {
  value: SessionForm;
  onChange: (value: SessionForm) => void;
  errors: SessionFormErrors;
  disabled?: boolean;
  /** New session: an empty timezone or language means the server default. */
  serverDefaults?: boolean;
}

/** Name, allowlist, timezone, language, and screen (spec sections 7.2 and 10.2). */
export function SessionFields({
  value,
  onChange,
  errors,
  disabled = false,
  serverDefaults = false,
}: SessionFieldsProps) {
  const timezones = useId();
  const set = <K extends keyof SessionForm>(key: K, next: SessionForm[K]) => {
    onChange({ ...value, [key]: next });
  };
  return (
    <>
      <TextField
        label="Name"
        value={value.name}
        maxLength={64}
        disabled={disabled}
        error={errors.name}
        onChange={(event) => {
          set('name', event.target.value);
        }}
      />
      <ChipInput
        label="Allowlist"
        value={value.allowlist}
        placeholder="example.com"
        hint={ALLOWLIST_HELP}
        disabled={disabled}
        error={errors.allowlist}
        onChange={(next) => {
          set('allowlist', next);
        }}
      />
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <TextField
          label="Timezone"
          list={timezones}
          value={value.timezone}
          placeholder={serverDefaults ? 'Server default' : undefined}
          disabled={disabled}
          error={errors.timezone}
          onChange={(event) => {
            set('timezone', event.target.value);
          }}
        />
        <TextField
          label="Language"
          value={value.language}
          placeholder={serverDefaults ? 'Server default' : undefined}
          disabled={disabled}
          error={errors.language}
          onChange={(event) => {
            set('language', event.target.value);
          }}
        />
      </div>
      <datalist id={timezones}>
        {TIMEZONES.map((zone) => (
          <option key={zone} value={zone} />
        ))}
      </datalist>
      <RadioGroup
        label="Screen"
        value={value.screen}
        options={SCREEN_OPTIONS}
        disabled={disabled}
        onValueChange={(next) => {
          set('screen', next);
        }}
      />
    </>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/shell/NewSessionSheet.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessage, isApiError } from '../../api/client';
import { useCreateSession } from '../../api/sessions';
import { Banner, Button, Sheet, SheetBody, SheetFooter, Switch } from '../../components/os';
import {
  EMPTY_SESSION_FORM,
  SessionFields,
  serverFieldErrors,
  toCreateRequest,
  type SessionFormErrors,
} from '../sessions/session-form';

export interface NewSessionSheetProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
}

/** Name, allowlist, timezone, language, screen, and "Start now" (spec section 10.2). */
export function NewSessionSheet({ open, onOpenChange }: NewSessionSheetProps) {
  const create = useCreateSession();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_SESSION_FORM);
  const [start, setStart] = useState(true);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<SessionFormErrors>({});

  function close() {
    onOpenChange(false);
    setForm(EMPTY_SESSION_FORM);
    setStart(true);
    setErrors({});
    create.reset();
  }

  const fieldError = isApiError(create.error, 'validation_failed') && create.error.fields;
  const generalError = create.error && !fieldError ? errorMessage(create.error) : null;

  return (
    <Sheet
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (!next) close();
      }}
      title="New session"
    >
      <form
        noValidate
        className="flex min-h-0 flex-col"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          const result = toCreateRequest(form, start);
          if (!result.ok) {
            setErrors(result.errors);
            return;
          }
          setErrors({});
          create.mutate(result.data, {
            onSuccess: (session) => {
              close();
              void navigate(`/sessions/${session.id}/live`);
            },
            onError: (error) => {
              if (isApiError(error, 'validation_failed') && error.fields) {
                setErrors(serverFieldErrors(error.fields));
              }
            },
          });
        }}
      >
        <SheetBody>
          <SessionFields value={form} onChange={setForm} errors={errors} serverDefaults />
          <Switch label="Start now" checked={start} onCheckedChange={setStart} />
          {generalError ? (
            <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
              {generalError}
            </Banner>
          ) : null}
        </SheetBody>
        <SheetFooter>
          <Button onClick={close}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={create.isPending}>
            Create
          </Button>
        </SheetFooter>
      </form>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { IconLayoutGrid, IconPlus, IconUsers } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useSessions } from '../../api/sessions';
import {
  IconButton,
  OsIcon,
  Sidebar,
  SidebarItem,
  SidebarNote,
  SidebarSection,
} from '../../components/os';
import { useCurrentUser } from '../guards';
import { NewSessionSheet } from './NewSessionSheet';
import { UserMenu } from './UserMenu';

function SessionItems({
  sessions,
  empty,
}: {
  sessions: readonly SessionResource[] | undefined;
  empty: string;
}) {
  if (sessions === undefined) return null;
  if (sessions.length === 0) return <SidebarNote>{empty}</SidebarNote>;
  return sessions.map((session) => (
    <SidebarItem
      key={session.id}
      to={`/sessions/${session.id}`}
      label={session.name}
      state={session.state}
      agentConnected={session.agentActivity.activeAgentCount >= 1}
    />
  ));
}

/** "My sessions", "Shared with me", "Admin", and the user menu (spec section 10.2). */
export function AppSidebar() {
  const me = useCurrentUser();
  const sessions = useSessions();
  const [newOpen, setNewOpen] = useState(false);
  const mine = sessions.data?.filter((s) => s.myRole === 'owner');
  const shared = sessions.data?.filter((s) => s.myRole === 'operator' || s.myRole === 'viewer');

  return (
    <Sidebar footer={<UserMenu />}>
      <SidebarSection
        title="My sessions"
        action={
          <IconButton
            aria-label="New session"
            onClick={() => {
              setNewOpen(true);
            }}
          >
            <OsIcon icon={IconPlus} />
          </IconButton>
        }
      >
        {sessions.isPending ? <SidebarNote>Loading…</SidebarNote> : null}
        {sessions.isError ? <SidebarNote>The sessions cannot be loaded.</SidebarNote> : null}
        <SessionItems sessions={mine} empty="No sessions" />
      </SidebarSection>
      <SidebarSection title="Shared with me">
        <SessionItems sessions={shared} empty="Nothing is shared with you" />
      </SidebarSection>
      {me.role === 'admin' ? (
        <SidebarSection title="Admin">
          <SidebarItem to="/admin/users" label="Users" icon={IconUsers} />
          <SidebarItem to="/admin/sessions" label="All sessions" icon={IconLayoutGrid} />
        </SidebarSection>
      ) : null}
      <NewSessionSheet open={newOpen} onOpenChange={setNewOpen} />
    </Sidebar>
  );
}
```

### Task 14: Tokens view: table, New token sheet, and the one-time token sheet

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/token-sheets.tsx`, `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/tokens.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/web/src/routes/index.tsx` (adds the `tokens` view)
- Test: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/tokens.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3 to 12 (`useTokens`, `useCreateToken`, `useRevokeToken`, `CodeBlock`, `mcpEndpoint`, `claudeMcpAddCommand`, `mcpServersJson`, `useCurrentUser`, `useSessionResource`); `CreateTokenRequestSchema`, `TOKEN_EXPIRY_DAYS` of `@hydra/shared`.
- Produces: `NewTokenSheet({ sid, open, onOpenChange, onCreated })`, `TokenCreatedSheet({ session, created, onClose })`, `TokensView`.

Spec sections 6.6 and 10.2. The table has the columns name, prefix, creator, created, expires, last used, and Revoke. Revoke shows for a token that is not revoked, if the user has `token.revoke_any`, or `token.revoke_own` and created the token. The New token sheet asks for the name and the expiry (1, 7, 30, or 90 days, default 30) and checks the name with `CreateTokenRequestSchema` (plan 02: letters, digits, "-", "_"). After the create, a wide sheet (560px) shows the token one time, as the `claude mcp add` command and as the `mcpServers` JSON, each with a copy button. The token name is the MCP server name ("Index issues" 9), and `<HYDRA_PUBLIC_URL>` is `window.location.origin`. "Done" drops the secret from memory.

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  BEN_ID,

  MockEventSource.install();
});

    const sheet = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'New token' });

    await user.click(within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' }));
    expect(name).toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
    expect(api.calls(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/tokens`)).toHaveLength(0);
    await user.clear(name);
    await user.type(name, 'x');
    await user.click(within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' }));
    expect(await within(sheet).findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('You cannot create tokens.');
  });
});
```
