`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/token-sheets.tsx`:

```tsx
import {
  CreateTokenRequestSchema,
  TOKEN_EXPIRY_DAYS,
  type CreateTokenResponse,
  type SessionResource,
} from '@hydra/shared';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { errorMessage } from '../../api/client';
import { useCreateToken } from '../../api/tokens';
import {
  Banner,
  Button,
  CodeBlock,
  RadioGroup,
  Sheet,
  SheetBody,
  SheetFooter,
  TextField,
} from '../../components/os';
import { claudeMcpAddCommand, mcpEndpoint, mcpServersJson } from '../../lib/mcp-config';

type ExpiryValue = `${(typeof TOKEN_EXPIRY_DAYS)[number]}`;

/** The rule of the token name in @hydra/shared (CreateTokenRequestSchema). */
const NAME_RULE = 'Use 1 to 64 letters, digits, "-", or "_".';

const EXPIRY_OPTIONS = TOKEN_EXPIRY_DAYS.map((days) => ({
  value: String(days) as ExpiryValue,
  label: days === 1 ? '1 day' : `${String(days)} days`,
}));

export interface NewTokenSheetProps {
  sid: string;
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  onCreated: (created: CreateTokenResponse) => void;
}

/** Name and expiry of a new agent token (spec sections 6.6 and 10.2). */
export function NewTokenSheet({ sid, open, onOpenChange, onCreated }: NewTokenSheetProps) {
  const create = useCreateToken(sid);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [expiry, setExpiry] = useState<ExpiryValue>('30');
  const [nameError, setNameError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  function close() {
    onOpenChange(false);
    setName('');
    setExpiry('30');
    setNameError(null);
    create.reset();
  }

  return (
    <Sheet
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (!next) close();
      }}
      title="New token"
      description="An agent with this token controls the browser of this session through MCP."
    >
      <form
        noValidate
        className="flex min-h-0 flex-col"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          const parsed = CreateTokenRequestSchema.safeParse({
            name: name.trim(),
            expiresInDays: Number(expiry),
          });
          if (!parsed.success) {
            setNameError(NAME_RULE);
            return;
          }
          setNameError(null);
          create.mutate(parsed.data, {
            onSuccess: (created) => {
              close();
              onCreated(created);
            },
          });
        }}
      >
        <SheetBody>
          <TextField
            label="Name"
            placeholder="For example: laptop"
            hint="MCP clients show this name for the server."
            value={name}
            error={nameError}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setName(event.target.value);
            }}
          />
          <RadioGroup
            label="Expires after"
            value={expiry}
            options={EXPIRY_OPTIONS}
            onValueChange={setExpiry}
          />
          {create.error ? (
            <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
              {errorMessage(create.error)}
            </Banner>
          ) : null}
        </SheetBody>
        <SheetFooter>
          <Button onClick={close}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={create.isPending}>
            Create
          </Button>
        </SheetFooter>
      </form>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

export interface TokenCreatedSheetProps {
  session: SessionResource;
  created: CreateTokenResponse | null;
  onClose: () => void;
}

/** Shows the token one time, as the Claude Code command and as JSON (spec section 10.2). */
export function TokenCreatedSheet({ session, created, onClose }: TokenCreatedSheetProps) {
  const endpoint = mcpEndpoint(window.location.origin, session.id);
  // The token name is the MCP server name in both configs.
  const name = created?.token.name ?? '';
  const secret = created?.secret ?? '';
  return (
    <Sheet
      open={created !== null}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (!next) onClose();
      }}
      title={`Token “${created?.token.name ?? ''}” created`}
      description="Copy the token now. Hydra shows it only one time."
      wide
    >
      <SheetBody>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <h3 className="text-os-base font-semibold">Claude Code</h3>
          <CodeBlock
            label="Claude Code command"
            code={claudeMcpAddCommand(name, endpoint, secret)}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <h3 className="text-os-base font-semibold">Other MCP clients</h3>
          <CodeBlock label="mcpServers config" code={mcpServersJson(name, endpoint, secret)} />
        </div>
      </SheetBody>
      <SheetFooter>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
          Done
        </Button>
      </SheetFooter>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/tokens.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { CreateTokenResponse, Token } from '@hydra/shared';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { errorMessage } from '../../api/client';
import { useRevokeToken, useTokens } from '../../api/tokens';
import { Banner, Button, Table, type Column } from '../../components/os';
import { formatDateTime } from '../../lib/format';
import { useCurrentUser } from '../guards';
import { useSessionResource } from './outlet';
import { NewTokenSheet, TokenCreatedSheet } from './token-sheets';

/** The Tokens view (spec section 10.2). The route needs `token.create`. */
export function TokensView() {
  const session = useSessionResource();
  const me = useCurrentUser();
  const tokens = useTokens(session.id);
  const revoke = useRevokeToken(session.id);
  const [newOpen, setNewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [created, setCreated] = useState<CreateTokenResponse | null>(null);
  const [now] = useState(Date.now);

  const canRevoke = (token: Token) =>
    token.revokedAt === null &&
    (session.rights.includes('token.revoke_any') ||
      (session.rights.includes('token.revoke_own') && token.createdBy.id === me.id));

  const columns: Column<Token>[] = [
    { key: 'name', header: 'Name', cell: (t) => t.name },
    {
      key: 'prefix',
      header: 'Prefix',
      cell: (t) => <span className="font-mono text-os-sm">{t.prefix}</span>,
    },
    { key: 'creator', header: 'Creator', cell: (t) => t.createdBy.displayName },
    { key: 'created', header: 'Created', cell: (t) => formatDateTime(t.createdAt) },
    {
      key: 'expires',
      header: 'Expires',
      cell: (t) =>
        t.revokedAt !== null
          ? 'Revoked'
          : t.expiresAt <= now
            ? 'Expired'
            : formatDateTime(t.expiresAt),
    },
    { key: 'used', header: 'Last used', cell: (t) => formatDateTime(t.lastUsedAt) },
    {
      key: 'actions',
      header: 'Actions',
      align: 'right',
      cell: (t) =>
        canRevoke(t) ? (
          <Button
            size="small"
            aria-label={`Revoke ${t.name}`}
            disabled={revoke.isPending}
            onClick={() => {
              revoke.mutate(t.id);
            }}
          >
            Revoke
          </Button>
        ) : null,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-os-base font-semibold">Agent tokens</h2>
        <Button
          variant="primary"
          onClick={() => {
            setNewOpen(true);
          }}
        >
          New token
        </Button>
      </div>
      {tokens.isError ? (
        <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(tokens.error)}
        </Banner>
      ) : null}
      {revoke.error ? (
        <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(revoke.error)}
        </Banner>
      ) : null}
      <Table
        label="Agent tokens"
        columns={columns}
        rows={tokens.data ?? []}
        rowKey={(t) => t.id}
        empty={tokens.isPending ? 'Loading…' : 'No tokens'}
      />
      <NewTokenSheet
        sid={session.id}
        open={newOpen}
        onOpenChange={setNewOpen}
        onCreated={setCreated}
      />
      <TokenCreatedSheet
        session={session}
        created={created}
        onClose={() => {
          setCreated(null);
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { RouteObject } from 'react-router';

];
```

### Task 15: Sharing view: owner, members, role menu, Remove, and Add

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/sharing.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/web/src/routes/index.tsx` (adds the `sharing` view)
- Test: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/sharing.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3 to 12 (`useMembers`, `useAddMember`, `useUpdateMember`, `useRemoveMember`, `Menu`, `MenuRadioGroup`, `RadioGroup`, `Table`); `MEMBER_ROLES` of `@hydra/shared`.
- Produces: `SharingView`.

Spec sections 6.5 and 10.2. All users with `session.read` see the owner and the members. With `session.share`, each member has a role menu (Viewer, Operator) and Remove, and a form adds a member by email with a role. The owner cannot be changed or removed (spec section 6.5). Errors of the server (for example `conflict` or `not_found` for an unknown email) show their message.

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ANA_ID, BEN_ID, makeMember, makeSession, SID, VIEWER_RIGHTS } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, mockApi, type MockApi } from '../../test/mock-api';
import { MockEventSource } from '../../test/mock-event-source';
import { mockLoggedIn, renderApp } from '../../test/render';

let api: MockApi;

const MEMBERS = {
  owner: makeMember({
    userId: ANA_ID,
    email: 'ana@example.com',
    displayName: 'Ana',
    role: 'owner',
  }),
  members: [makeMember()],
};

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
  api.on(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/members`, { body: MEMBERS });
});

describe('Sharing view', () => {
  it('adds a member by email, changes a role, and removes a member', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/members`, {
      status: 201,
      body: makeMember({
        userId: 'u3',
        email: 'cleo@example.com',
        displayName: 'Cleo',
        role: 'operator',
      }),
    });
    api.on(`PATCH /api/sessions/${SID}/members/${BEN_ID}`, {
      body: makeMember({ role: 'operator' }),
    });
    api.on(`DELETE /api/sessions/${SID}/members/${BEN_ID}`, { status: 204 });
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/sharing`);
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'People with access' });
    expect(await within(table).findByText('Ben')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(table).getByRole('cell', { name: 'Owner' })).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), 'cleo@example.com');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Operator' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(api.calls(`POST /api/sessions/${SID}/members`)[0]?.body).toEqual({
        email: 'cleo@example.com',
        role: 'operator',
      });
    });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Role of Ben: Viewer' }));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('menuitemradio', { name: 'Operator' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(api.calls(`PATCH /api/sessions/${SID}/members/${BEN_ID}`)[0]?.body).toEqual({
        role: 'operator',
      });
    });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove Ben' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(api.calls(`DELETE /api/sessions/${SID}/members/${BEN_ID}`)).toHaveLength(1);
    });
  });

  it('is read-only without session.share', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession({ myRole: 'viewer', rights: VIEWER_RIGHTS })] });
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/sharing`);
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'People with access' });
    expect(await within(table).findByRole('cell', { name: 'Viewer' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Remove Ben' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Role of Ben/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Email')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the error of a failed add', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(
      `POST /api/sessions/${SID}/members`,
      apiError(409, 'conflict', 'This user is already a member.'),
    );
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/sharing`);
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Email'), 'ben@example.com');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('This user is already a member.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Email')).toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
  });
});
```

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/sharing.tsx`:

```tsx
import { MEMBER_ROLES, type Member, type MemberRole } from '@hydra/shared';
import { IconChevronDown } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { errorMessage } from '../../api/client';
import { useAddMember, useMembers, useRemoveMember, useUpdateMember } from '../../api/members';
import {
  Banner,
  Button,
  Menu,
  MenuContent,
  MenuRadioGroup,
  MenuTrigger,
  OsIcon,
  RadioGroup,
  Table,
  TextField,
  type Column,
} from '../../components/os';
import { useSessionResource } from './outlet';

const ROLE_LABELS: Record<Member['role'], string> = {
  owner: 'Owner',
  operator: 'Operator',
  viewer: 'Viewer',
};

const ROLE_OPTIONS = MEMBER_ROLES.map((role) => ({ value: role, label: ROLE_LABELS[role] }));

function isMemberRole(role: Member['role']): role is MemberRole {
  return role !== 'owner';
}

/** The Sharing view: owner and members, role menu, Remove, and Add (spec section 10.2). */
export function SharingView() {
  const session = useSessionResource();
  const members = useMembers(session.id);
  const add = useAddMember(session.id);
  const update = useUpdateMember(session.id);
  const remove = useRemoveMember(session.id);
  const canShare = session.rights.includes('session.share');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState<MemberRole>('viewer');

  const rows = members.data ? [members.data.owner, ...members.data.members] : [];
  const actionError = update.error ?? remove.error;

  const columns: Column<Member>[] = [
    {
      key: 'name',
      header: 'Name',
      cell: (m) => (
        <span className="flex flex-col">
          <span>{m.displayName}</span>
          <span className="text-os-xs text-os-gray-accessible">{m.email}</span>
        </span>
      ),
    },
    {
      key: 'role',
      header: 'Role',
      cell: (m) =>
        canShare && isMemberRole(m.role) ? (
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger asChild>
              <Button size="small" aria-label={`Role of ${m.displayName}: ${ROLE_LABELS[m.role]}`}>
                {ROLE_LABELS[m.role]}
                <OsIcon icon={IconChevronDown} size={12} />
              </Button>
            </MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent align="start">
              <MenuRadioGroup
                label="Role"
                value={m.role}
                options={ROLE_OPTIONS}
                onValueChange={(next) => {
                  update.mutate({ userId: m.userId, role: next });
                }}
              />
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        ) : (
          ROLE_LABELS[m.role]
        ),
    },
    {
      key: 'actions',
      header: 'Actions',
      align: 'right',
      cell: (m) =>
        canShare && isMemberRole(m.role) ? (
          <Button
            size="small"
            aria-label={`Remove ${m.displayName}`}
            disabled={remove.isPending}
            onClick={() => {
              remove.mutate(m.userId);
            }}
          >
            Remove
          </Button>
        ) : null,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-6">
      <h2 className="text-os-base font-semibold">People with access</h2>
      {members.isError ? (
        <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(members.error)}
        </Banner>
      ) : null}
      {actionError ? (
        <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(actionError)}
        </Banner>
      ) : null}
      <Table
        label="People with access"
        columns={columns}
        rows={rows}
        rowKey={(m) => m.userId}
        empty={members.isPending ? 'Loading…' : 'No people'}
      />
      {canShare ? (
        <form
          noValidate
          className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-os-menu border border-os-separator p-4"
          onSubmit={(event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            add.mutate(
              { email: email.trim(), role },
              {
                onSuccess: () => {
                  setEmail('');
                },
              },
            );
          }}
        >
          <h3 className="text-os-base font-semibold">Add a member</h3>
          <TextField
            label="Email"
            type="email"
            value={email}
            error={add.error ? errorMessage(add.error) : null}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setEmail(event.target.value);
            }}
          />
          <RadioGroup label="Role" value={role} options={ROLE_OPTIONS} onValueChange={setRole} />
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={add.isPending || email.trim() === ''}>
              Add
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 16: Activity view: audit table, filters, row details, Load older, live updates

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/activity.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/web/src/routes/index.tsx` (adds the `activity` view)
- Test: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/activity.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3 to 12 (`useAuditPages`, `useTokens`, `useSessionEvents`, `Menu`, `MenuRadioGroup`, `Sheet`, `CodeBlock`, `Table`, `formatEventTime`); `AUDIT_KINDS` of `@hydra/shared`.
- Produces: `ActivityView`.

Spec sections 8.6, 10.2, and 12.1. The route needs `audit.read`. The filters are kind, tool, status, and token (the token menu shows only with `token.create`). The tool filter applies on Enter. A click on a row (or on its time button, for the keyboard) opens a sheet with the `data` of the event. "Load older" fetches the next page with `before`. While the view is open, `GET /api/sessions/<sid>/events` adds new events at the top and replaces updated ones.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/activity.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeAuditEvent, makeSession, makeToken, SID, VIEWER_RIGHTS } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, mockApi, type MockApi, type RecordedRequest } from '../../test/mock-api';
import { MockEventSource } from '../../test/mock-event-source';
import { mockLoggedIn, renderApp } from '../../test/render';

let api: MockApi;

const FIRST_PAGE = {
  events: [
    makeAuditEvent({ id: 12 }),
    makeAuditEvent({
      id: 11,
      kind: 'session.state',
      actorType: 'system',
      actorId: null,
      actorName: null,
      status: null,
      data: { state: 'running' },
    }),
  ],
  nextBefore: 11,
};
const OLDER_PAGE = {
  events: [makeAuditEvent({ id: 3, data: { tool: 'browser_navigate', durationMs: 900 } })],
  nextBefore: null,
};

function auditAnswer(request: RecordedRequest) {
  return { body: request.query.get('before') === '11' ? OLDER_PAGE : FIRST_PAGE };
}

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
  api.on(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/tokens`, { body: [makeToken()] });
});

function rows() {
  const table = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Activity' });
  return within(table)
    .getAllByRole('row')
    .slice(1)
    .map((row) => row.textContent);
}

describe('Activity view', () => {
  it('lists events, loads older pages, and adds live events from the session SSE stream', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/audit`, auditAnswer);
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/activity`);
    expect(await screen.findByText('browser_click · 230 ms')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('laptop (token)')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('System')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(api.calls(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/audit`)[0]?.query.toString()).toBe('limit=50');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Load older' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('browser_navigate · 900 ms')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(api.calls(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/audit`)[1]?.query.get('before')).toBe('11');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Load older' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    const source = MockEventSource.latest(`/api/sessions/${SID}/events`);
    act(() => {
      source.open();
      source.emit(
        'audit',
        makeAuditEvent({ id: 13, status: 'pending', data: { tool: 'browser_type' } }),
      );
    });
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(rows()[0]).toContain('browser_type');
    });
    expect(rows()).toHaveLength(4);
  });

  it('sends the filters to the server', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/audit`, { body: { events: [], nextBefore: null } });
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/activity`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('cell', { name: 'No events' })).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Kind: All kinds' }));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('menuitemradio', { name: 'mcp.tool_call' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Tool'), 'browser_click{Enter}');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Status: Any status' }));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('menuitemradio', { name: 'error' }));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Token: Any token' }));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('menuitemradio', { name: 'laptop' }));

    await waitFor(() => {
      const last = api.calls(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/audit`).at(-1);
      expect(Object.fromEntries(last?.query ?? [])).toEqual({
        kind: 'mcp.tool_call',
        tool: 'browser_click',
        status: 'error',
        tokenId: makeToken().id,
        limit: '50',
      });
    });
  });

  it('shows the data of a clicked row', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/audit`, auditAnswer);
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/activity`);
    await user.click(await screen.findByText('browser_click · 230 ms'));
    const sheet = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: /^mcp\.tool_call · / });
    const data = within(sheet).getByRole('figure', { name: 'Event data' }).textContent;
    expect(JSON.parse(data)).toEqual({ tool: 'browser_click', durationMs: 230 });
  });

  it('shows the error of a failed load', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(
      `GET /api/sessions/${SID}/audit`,
      apiError(403, 'forbidden', 'You cannot read the audit log.'),
    );
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/activity`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('You cannot read the audit log.');
  });

  it('is not open to a viewer (no audit.read)', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession({ myRole: 'viewer', rights: VIEWER_RIGHTS })] });
    const { router } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/activity`);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe(`/sessions/${SID}/live`);
    });
    expect(api.calls(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/audit`)).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(MockEventSource.instances.map((s) => s.url)).not.toContain(
      `/api/sessions/${SID}/events`,
    );
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { AUDIT_KINDS, type AuditEvent, type AuditKind } from '@hydra/shared';
import { IconChevronDown } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useAuditPages } from '../../api/audit';
import { errorMessage } from '../../api/client';
import type { AuditFilters } from '../../api/keys';
import { useTokens } from '../../api/tokens';
import {
  Banner,
  Button,
  CodeBlock,
  Menu,
  MenuContent,
  MenuRadioGroup,
  MenuTrigger,
  OsIcon,
  Sheet,
  SheetBody,
  SheetFooter,
  Table,
  TextField,
  type Column,
} from '../../components/os';
import { useSessionEvents } from '../../lib/events';
import { formatEventTime } from '../../lib/format';
import { useSessionResource } from './outlet';

const ALL = 'all';
const STATUSES = ['pending', 'ok', 'error'] as const;

const STATUS_CLASS: Record<(typeof STATUSES)[number], string> = {
  pending: 'text-os-gray-accessible',
  ok: 'text-os-green-accessible',
  error: 'text-os-red-accessible',
};

function actorLabel(event: AuditEvent): string {
  if (event.actorType === 'system') return 'System';
  const name = event.actorName ?? event.actorId ?? 'Unknown';
  return event.actorType === 'token' ? `${name} (token)` : name;
}

/** One line about the event: the tool and its duration, or the data as short JSON. */
function summary(event: AuditEvent): string {
  const { tool, durationMs, error } = event.data;
  if (typeof tool === 'string') {
    const parts = [tool];
    if (typeof durationMs === 'number') parts.push(`${String(Math.round(durationMs))} ms`);
    if (typeof error === 'string') parts.push(error);
    return parts.join(' · ');
  }
  const json = JSON.stringify(event.data);
  return json.length > 80 ? `${json.slice(0, 79)}…` : json;
}

function FilterMenu<T extends string>({
  label,
  value,
  options,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: T;
  options: readonly { value: T; label: string }[];
  onChange: (value: T) => void;
}) {
  const current = options.find((o) => o.value === value)?.label ?? value;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <span className="text-os-base text-os-foreground">{label}</span>
      <Menu>
        <MenuTrigger asChild>
          <Button aria-label={`${label}: ${current}`} className="justify-between">
            {current}
            <OsIcon icon={IconChevronDown} size={12} />
          </Button>
        </MenuTrigger>
        <MenuContent align="start">
          <MenuRadioGroup label={label} value={value} options={options} onValueChange={onChange} />
        </MenuContent>
      </Menu>
    </div>
  );
}

/** The Activity view: audit table, filters, details, Load older, live updates (spec 10.2). */
export function ActivityView() {
  const session = useSessionResource();
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<AuditFilters>({});
  const [toolDraft, setToolDraft] = useState('');
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<AuditEvent | null>(null);
  const pages = useAuditPages(session.id, filters);
  const tokens = useTokens(session.id, session.rights.includes('token.create'));
  useSessionEvents(session.id);

  const events = pages.data?.pages.flatMap((page) => page.events) ?? [];

  // An undefined value removes the filter: the query key and the URL ignore undefined values.
  const setFilter = <K extends keyof AuditFilters>(key: K, value: AuditFilters[K] | undefined) => {
    setFilters((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: value }));
  };

  const columns: Column<AuditEvent>[] = [
    {
      key: 'time',
      header: 'Time',
      cell: (e) => (
        <button
          type="button"
          className="text-left whitespace-nowrap hover:underline"
          onClick={() => {
            setSelected(e);
          }}
        >
          {formatEventTime(e.ts)}
        </button>
      ),
    },
    {
      key: 'kind',
      header: 'Kind',
      cell: (e) => <span className="font-mono text-os-sm">{e.kind}</span>,
    },
    { key: 'actor', header: 'Actor', cell: actorLabel },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: 'Status',
      cell: (e) => (e.status ? <span className={STATUS_CLASS[e.status]}>{e.status}</span> : '—'),
    },
    {
      key: 'details',
      header: 'Details',
      cell: (e) => (
        <span className="block max-w-120 truncate font-mono text-os-sm">{summary(e)}</span>
      ),
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <FilterMenu<AuditKind | typeof ALL>
          label="Kind"
          value={filters.kind ?? ALL}
          options={[
            { value: ALL, label: 'All kinds' },
            ...AUDIT_KINDS.map((kind) => ({ value: kind, label: kind })),
          ]}
          onChange={(value) => {
            setFilter('kind', value === ALL ? undefined : value);
          }}
        />
        <form
          className="w-56"
          onSubmit={(event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            const tool = toolDraft.trim();
            setFilter('tool', tool === '' ? undefined : tool);
          }}
        >
          <TextField
            label="Tool"
            placeholder="browser_click, then Enter"
            value={toolDraft}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setToolDraft(event.target.value);
            }}
          />
        </form>
        <FilterMenu<(typeof STATUSES)[number] | typeof ALL>
          label="Status"
          value={filters.status ?? ALL}
          options={[
            { value: ALL, label: 'Any status' },
            ...STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: s })),
          ]}
          onChange={(value) => {
            setFilter('status', value === ALL ? undefined : value);
          }}
        />
        {tokens.data ? (
          <FilterMenu
            label="Token"
            value={filters.tokenId ?? ALL}
            options={[
              { value: ALL, label: 'Any token' },
              ...tokens.data.map((t) => ({ value: t.id, label: t.name })),
            ]}
            onChange={(value) => {
              setFilter('tokenId', value === ALL ? undefined : value);
            }}
          />
        ) : null}
      </div>
      {pages.isError ? (
        <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(pages.error)}
        </Banner>
      ) : null}
      <Table
        label="Activity"
        columns={columns}
        rows={events}
        rowKey={(e) => String(e.id)}
        empty={pages.isPending ? 'Loading…' : 'No events'}
        onRowClick={setSelected}
      />
      {pages.hasNextPage ? (
        <div className="flex justify-center">
          <Button
            disabled={pages.isFetchingNextPage}
            onClick={() => {
              void pages.fetchNextPage();
            }}
          >
            Load older
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <Sheet
        open={selected !== null}
        onOpenChange={(next) => {
          if (!next) setSelected(null);
        }}
        title={selected ? `${selected.kind} · ${formatEventTime(selected.ts)}` : 'Event'}
        description={
          selected
            ? `${actorLabel(selected)}${selected.status ? ` · ${selected.status}` : ''}`
            : undefined
        }
        wide
      >
        <SheetBody>
          {selected ? (
            <CodeBlock label="Event data" code={JSON.stringify(selected.data, null, 2)} />
          ) : null}
        </SheetBody>
        <SheetFooter>
          <Button
            variant="primary"
            onClick={() => {
              setSelected(null);
            }}
          >
            Close
          </Button>
        </SheetFooter>
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { RouteObject } from 'react-router';
import { ChangePasswordPage } from './change-password';
import { RequireLogin } from './guards';
import { LoginPage } from './login';
import { ActivityView } from './sessions/activity';
import { SessionIndex, SessionLayout } from './sessions/layout';
import { LiveView } from './sessions/live';
import { SharingView } from './sessions/sharing';

import { NotFoundPage } from './shell/NotFoundPage';

/** All routes (spec section 3.2). main.tsx and the tests build their routers from it. */
export const appRoutes: RouteObject[] = [

    path: '/change-password',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin allowPasswordChange>
        <ChangePasswordPage />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
  },
  {
    path: '/',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin>
        <AppShell />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
    children: [
      { index: true, element: <HomePage /> },
      {
        path: 'sessions/:sid',
        element: <SessionLayout />,
        children: [
          { index: true, element: <SessionIndex /> },
          { path: 'live', element: <LiveView /> },
          { path: 'tokens', element: <TokensView /> },
          { path: 'sharing', element: <SharingView /> },
          { path: 'activity', element: <ActivityView /> },
        ],
      },
      { path: '*', element: <NotFoundPage /> },
    ],
  },
];
```

### Task 17: Settings view: session settings, Restart required, and Diagnostics

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/settings.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/web/src/routes/index.tsx` (adds the `settings` view)
- Test: `packages/web/src/routes/sessions/settings.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3 to 13 (`useUpdateSession`, `useLogs`, `SessionFields`, `formFromSession`, `toUpdateRequest`, `serverFieldErrors`, `SegmentedControl`, `CodeBlock`); `MESSAGES` of `@hydra/shared`.
- Produces: `SettingsView`.

Spec sections 7.2 and 10.2. Name, allowlist (chip input), timezone, language, and screen. Only `session.update` can save; other users see the fields disabled. Save sends only the changed fields (`PATCH /api/sessions/:sid`). While `restartRequired` is true, the banner "Restart required" shows (variant `info`, STYLE.md section 5). With an allowlist `["*"]`, the warning "Allowlist permits all domains" shows (spec section 7.2). Users with `session.logs` also see Diagnostics: the last 50 lines of the `neko` and the `mcp` container, with a refresh button.

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeSession, OPERATOR_RIGHTS, SID, VIEWER_RIGHTS } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, mockApi, type MockApi } from '../../test/mock-api';
import { MockEventSource } from '../../test/mock-event-source';
import { mockLoggedIn, renderApp } from '../../test/render';

let api: MockApi;

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
  api.on(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/logs`, (request) => ({
    body: {
      container: request.query.get('container'),
      lines: [`${request.query.get('container') ?? ''} line 1`, 'line 2'],
    },
  }));
});

describe('Settings view', () => {
  it('saves only the changed fields and shows Restart required', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(`PATCH /api/sessions/${SID}`, {
      body: makeSession({
        restartRequired: true,
        settings: { ...makeSession().settings, allowlist: ['example.com', 'bank.example'] },
      }),
    });
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/settings`);
    const allowlist = await screen.findByLabelText('Allowlist');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Name')).toHaveValue('Banking');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Timezone')).toHaveValue('Europe/Rome');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: '1920 × 1080' })).toBeChecked();
    await user.type(allowlist, 'bank.example{Enter}');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Restart required/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Saved')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(api.calls(`PATCH /api/sessions/${SID}`)[0]?.body).toEqual({
      allowlist: ['example.com', 'bank.example'],
    });
  });

  it('shows the diagnostics of both containers', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/settings`);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/neko line 1/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'mcp' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/mcp line 1/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(api.calls(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/logs`).map((r) => r.query.get('container'))).toEqual(
      ['neko', 'mcp'],
    );
  });

  it('shows a field error of the server', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession()] });
    api.on(
      `PATCH /api/sessions/${SID}`,
      apiError(400, 'validation_failed', 'Invalid input', { language: 'Unknown language tag' }),
    );
    const { user } = renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/settings`);
    const language = await screen.findByLabelText('Language');
    await user.clear(language);
    await user.type(language, 'xx-YY');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Unknown language tag')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(language).toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
  });

  it('is read-only for a viewer and hides Diagnostics without session.logs', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession({ myRole: 'viewer', rights: VIEWER_RIGHTS })] });
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/settings`);
    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Name')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Save' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('heading', { name: 'Diagnostics' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(api.calls(`GET /api/sessions/${SID}/logs`)).toHaveLength(0);
    });
  });

  it('shows Diagnostics to an operator, who cannot change the settings', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { sessions: [makeSession({ myRole: 'operator', rights: OPERATOR_RIGHTS })] });
    renderApp(`/sessions/${SID}/settings`);
    const diagnostics = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Diagnostics' });
    expect(await within(diagnostics).findByText(/neko line 1/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Name')).toBeDisabled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the screen**

The form state starts from the session once (`key={session.id}`), so an SSE update does not replace what the user types. After a save, the form takes the values of the answer.

`packages/web/src/routes/sessions/settings.tsx`:

```tsx
import { MESSAGES, type SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { IconRefresh } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { errorMessage, isApiError } from '../../api/client';
import { useLogs, useUpdateSession } from '../../api/sessions';
import {
  Banner,
  Button,
  CodeBlock,
  IconButton,
  OsIcon,
  SegmentedControl,
} from '../../components/os';
import { permitsAllDomains } from '../../lib/format';
import { useSessionResource } from './outlet';
import {
  formFromSession,
  SessionFields,
  serverFieldErrors,
  toUpdateRequest,
  type SessionForm,
  type SessionFormErrors,
} from './session-form';

const CONTAINERS = [
  { value: 'neko', label: 'neko' },
  { value: 'mcp', label: 'mcp' },
] as const;

/** Diagnostics: the last 50 log lines of the neko and the mcp container (`session.logs`). */
function Diagnostics({ sid }: { sid: string }) {
  const [container, setContainer] = useState<'neko' | 'mcp'>('neko');
  const logs = useLogs(sid, container, true);
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="diagnostics" className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <h2 id="diagnostics" className="text-os-base font-semibold">
          Diagnostics
        </h2>
        <SegmentedControl
          label="Container"
          value={container}
          segments={CONTAINERS}
          onValueChange={setContainer}
        />
        <IconButton
          aria-label="Refresh logs"
          disabled={logs.isFetching}
          onClick={() => {
            void logs.refetch();
          }}
        >
          <OsIcon icon={IconRefresh} />
        </IconButton>
      </div>
      {logs.isError ? (
        <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(logs.error)}
        </Banner>
      ) : null}
      {logs.data ? (
        <CodeBlock
          label={`Last log lines of ${logs.data.container}`}
          code={logs.data.lines.length > 0 ? logs.data.lines.join('\n') : '(no log lines)'}
        />
      ) : null}
      {logs.isPending ? (
        <p role="status" className="text-os-base text-os-gray-accessible">
          Loading…
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

function SettingsForm({ session }: { session: SessionResource }) {
  const update = useUpdateSession(session.id);
  const editable = session.rights.includes('session.update');
  const [form, setForm] = useState<SessionForm>(() => formFromSession(session));
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<SessionFormErrors>({});
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const fieldError = isApiError(update.error, 'validation_failed') && update.error.fields;

  return (
    <form
      noValidate
      className="flex max-w-140 flex-col gap-4"
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        const result = toUpdateRequest(form, session);
        if (!result.ok) {
          setErrors(result.errors);
          return;
        }
        setErrors({});
        setSaved(false);
        if (Object.keys(result.data).length === 0) return;
        update.mutate(result.data, {
          onSuccess: (next) => {
            setForm(formFromSession(next));
            setSaved(true);
          },
          onError: (error) => {
            if (isApiError(error, 'validation_failed') && error.fields) {
              setErrors(serverFieldErrors(error.fields));
            }
          },
        });
      }}
    >
      <SessionFields
        value={form}
        onChange={(next) => {
          setForm(next);
          setSaved(false);
        }}
        errors={errors}
        disabled={!editable}
      />
      {update.error && !fieldError ? (
        <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(update.error)}
        </Banner>
      ) : null}
      {editable ? (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
          {saved ? (
            <span role="status" className="text-os-xs text-os-gray-accessible">
              Saved
            </span>
          ) : null}
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={update.isPending}>
            Save
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-os-xs text-os-gray-accessible">
          Only the owner and admins can change the settings.
        </p>
      )}
    </form>
  );
}

/** The Settings view (spec section 10.2). */
export function SettingsView() {
  const session = useSessionResource();
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 p-6">
      {session.restartRequired ? (
        <Banner variant="info">
          {MESSAGES.restartRequired}: the changes take effect at the next Restart.
        </Banner>
      ) : null}
      {permitsAllDomains(session.settings.allowlist) ? (
        <Banner variant="warning">{MESSAGES.allowAll}</Banner>
      ) : null}
      <SettingsForm key={session.id} session={session} />
      {session.rights.includes('session.logs') ? <Diagnostics sid={session.id} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- Produces: `AdminUsersPage`.

Spec sections 6.3 and 10.2. The table has the columns email, name, role, state (Active, Disabled, Locked), and last login. The Create user sheet asks for the email, the name, and "Administrator"; the one-time password shows one time in its own sheet. The row menu has Make admin or Make member, Change name (spec section 6.3: "change the display name"), Reset password (shows the new one-time password), Unlock (only for a locked user), and Disable or Enable. Members get Not found.

```tsx
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { apiError, mockApi, type MockApi } from '../../test/mock-api';

  MockEventSource.install();
});

const ADMIN = makeMe({ role: 'admin' });

describe('Admin → Users', () => {
  it('creates a user and shows the one-time password one time', async () => {
    mockLoggedIn(api, { me: ADMIN });
    api.on('GET /api/users', { body: [makeUser()] });

    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Not found' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(api.calls('GET /api/users')).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the screen**

The rename sheet gets a new `key` for each open, so its field starts with the current name without an effect.

`packages/web/src/routes/admin/users.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { UserSummary } from '@hydra/shared';
import { IconDots } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { errorMessage } from '../../api/client';
import {
  useCreateUser,
  useResetPassword,
  useUnlockUser,
  useUpdateUser,
  useUsers,
} from '../../api/users';
import {
  Banner,
  Button,
  Checkbox,
  CodeBlock,
  ContentPane,
  IconButton,
  Menu,
  MenuContent,
  MenuItem,
  MenuSeparator,
  MenuTrigger,
  OsIcon,
  Sheet,
  SheetBody,
  SheetFooter,
  Table,
  TextField,
  Toolbar,
  type Column,
} from '../../components/os';
import { formatDateTime } from '../../lib/format';

interface OneTimePassword {
  email: string;
  password: string;
}

function userState(user: UserSummary): string {
  if (user.status === 'disabled') return 'Disabled';
  return user.locked ? 'Locked' : 'Active';
}

function CreateUserSheet({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  onCreated,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  onCreated: (result: OneTimePassword) => void;
}) {
  const create = useCreateUser();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [admin, setAdmin] = useState(false);

  function close() {
    onOpenChange(false);
    setEmail('');
    setDisplayName('');
    setAdmin(false);
    create.reset();
  }

  return (
    <Sheet
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (!next) close();
      }}
      title="Create user"
      description="Hydra creates a one-time password. The user must change it at the first login."
    >
      <form
        noValidate
        className="flex min-h-0 flex-col"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          create.mutate(
            {
              email: email.trim(),
              displayName: displayName.trim(),
              role: admin ? 'admin' : 'member',
            },
            {
              onSuccess: (result) => {
                close();
                onCreated({ email: result.user.email, password: result.oneTimePassword });
              },
            },
          );
        }}
      >
        <SheetBody>
          <TextField
            label="Email"
            type="email"
            value={email}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setEmail(event.target.value);
            }}
          />
          <TextField
            label="Name"
            value={displayName}
            maxLength={64}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setDisplayName(event.target.value);
            }}
          />
          <Checkbox label="Administrator" checked={admin} onCheckedChange={setAdmin} />
          {create.error ? (
            <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
              {errorMessage(create.error)}
            </Banner>
          ) : null}
        </SheetBody>
        <SheetFooter>
          <Button onClick={close}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button
            type="submit"
            variant="primary"
            disabled={create.isPending || email.trim() === '' || displayName.trim() === ''}
          >
            Create
          </Button>
        </SheetFooter>
      </form>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

/** The parent gives it a new `key` for each open, so the form starts with the current name. */
function RenameUserSheet({ user, onClose }: { user: UserSummary | null; onClose: () => void }) {
  const update = useUpdateUser();
  const [name, setName] = useState(user?.displayName ?? '');

  return (
    <Sheet
      open={user !== null}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (!next) onClose();
      }}
      title="Change name"
      description={user ? `The name of ${user.email} in Hydra and in the live view.` : undefined}
    >
      <form
        noValidate
        className="flex min-h-0 flex-col"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          if (!user) return;
          update.mutate(
            { userId: user.id, patch: { displayName: name.trim() } },
            { onSuccess: onClose },
          );
        }}
      >
        <SheetBody>
          <TextField
            label="Name"
            value={name}
            maxLength={64}
            error={update.error ? errorMessage(update.error) : null}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setName(event.target.value);
            }}
          />
        </SheetBody>
        <SheetFooter>
          <Button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={update.isPending || name.trim() === ''}>
            Save
          </Button>
        </SheetFooter>
      </form>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function OneTimePasswordSheet({
  value,
  onClose,
}: {
  value: OneTimePassword | null;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Sheet
      open={value !== null}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (!next) onClose();
      }}
      title="One-time password"
      description={`Give this password to ${value?.email ?? ''}. Hydra shows it only one time. The user must change it at the first login.`}
    >
      <SheetBody>
        {value ? <CodeBlock label="One-time password" code={value.password} /> : null}
      </SheetBody>
      <SheetFooter>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
          Done
        </Button>
      </SheetFooter>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

/** Admin → Users (spec sections 6.3 and 10.2). */
export function AdminUsersPage() {
  const users = useUsers();
  const update = useUpdateUser();
  const reset = useResetPassword();
  const unlock = useUnlockUser();
  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
  const [oneTime, setOneTime] = useState<OneTimePassword | null>(null);
  const [renaming, setRenaming] = useState<UserSummary | null>(null);
  const [renameKey, setRenameKey] = useState(0);
  const actionError = update.error ?? reset.error ?? unlock.error;

  const columns: Column<UserSummary>[] = [
    { key: 'email', header: 'Email', cell: (u) => u.email },
    { key: 'name', header: 'Name', cell: (u) => u.displayName },
    { key: 'role', header: 'Role', cell: (u) => (u.role === 'admin' ? 'Admin' : 'Member') },
    { key: 'state', header: 'State', cell: userState },
    { key: 'login', header: 'Last login', cell: (u) => formatDateTime(u.lastLoginAt) },
    {
      key: 'actions',
      header: 'Actions',
      align: 'right',
      cell: (u) => (
        <Menu>
          <MenuTrigger asChild>
            <IconButton aria-label={`Actions for ${u.email}`}>
              <OsIcon icon={IconDots} />
            </IconButton>
          </MenuTrigger>
          <MenuContent>
            <MenuItem
              onSelect={() => {
                update.mutate({
                  userId: u.id,
                  patch: { role: u.role === 'admin' ? 'member' : 'admin' },
                });
              }}
            >
              {u.role === 'admin' ? 'Make member' : 'Make admin'}
            </MenuItem>
            <MenuItem
              onSelect={() => {
                setRenaming(u);
                setRenameKey((key) => key + 1);
              }}
            >
              Change name
            </MenuItem>
            <MenuItem
              onSelect={() => {
                reset.mutate(u.id, {
                  onSuccess: (result) => {
                    setOneTime({ email: u.email, password: result.oneTimePassword });
                  },
                });
              }}
            >
              Reset password
            </MenuItem>
            {u.locked ? (
              <MenuItem
                onSelect={() => {
                  unlock.mutate(u.id);
                }}
              >
                Unlock
              </MenuItem>
            ) : null}
            <MenuSeparator />
            <MenuItem
              destructive={u.status === 'active'}
              onSelect={() => {
                update.mutate({
                  userId: u.id,
                  patch: { status: u.status === 'active' ? 'disabled' : 'active' },
                });
              }}
            >
              {u.status === 'active' ? 'Disable' : 'Enable'}
            </MenuItem>
          </MenuContent>
        </Menu>
      ),
    },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <Toolbar
        title="Users"
        actions={
          <Button
            variant="primary"
            onClick={() => {
              setCreateOpen(true);
            }}
          >
            Create user
          </Button>
        }
      />
      <ContentPane className="gap-4 p-6">
        {users.isError ? (
          <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
            {errorMessage(users.error)}
          </Banner>
        ) : null}
        {actionError ? (
          <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
            {errorMessage(actionError)}
          </Banner>
        ) : null}
        <Table
          label="Users"
          columns={columns}
          rows={users.data ?? []}
          rowKey={(u) => u.id}
          empty={users.isPending ? 'Loading…' : 'No users'}
        />
      </ContentPane>
      <CreateUserSheet open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen} onCreated={setOneTime} />
      <RenameUserSheet
        key={renameKey}
        user={renaming}
        onClose={() => {
          setRenaming(null);
        }}
      />
      <OneTimePasswordSheet
        value={oneTime}
        onClose={() => {
          setOneTime(null);
        }}
      />
    </>
  );
}
```

`packages/web/src/routes/index.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { RouteObject } from 'react-router';
import { AdminUsersPage } from './admin/users';
import { ChangePasswordPage } from './change-password';
import { RequireAdmin, RequireLogin } from './guards';
import { LoginPage } from './login';
import { ActivityView } from './sessions/activity';
import { SessionIndex, SessionLayout } from './sessions/layout';
import { LiveView } from './sessions/live';
import { SettingsView } from './sessions/settings';
import { SharingView } from './sessions/sharing';
import { TokensView } from './sessions/tokens';
import { AppShell } from './shell/AppShell';
import { HomePage } from './shell/HomePage';
import { NotFoundPage } from './shell/NotFoundPage';

/** All routes (spec section 3.2). main.tsx and the tests build their routers from it. */
export const appRoutes: RouteObject[] = [
  { path: '/login', element: <LoginPage /> },
  {
    path: '/change-password',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin allowPasswordChange>
        <ChangePasswordPage />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
  },
  {
    path: '/',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin>
        <AppShell />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
    children: [
      { index: true, element: <HomePage /> },
      {
        path: 'sessions/:sid',
        element: <SessionLayout />,
        children: [
          { index: true, element: <SessionIndex /> },
          { path: 'live', element: <LiveView /> },
          { path: 'tokens', element: <TokensView /> },
          { path: 'sharing', element: <SharingView /> },
          { path: 'activity', element: <ActivityView /> },
          { path: 'settings', element: <SettingsView /> },
        ],
      },
      {
        path: 'admin/users',
        element: (
          <RequireAdmin>
            <AdminUsersPage />
          </RequireAdmin>
        ),
      },
      { path: '*', element: <NotFoundPage /> },
    ],
  },
];
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3 to 12 (`useSessions({ all: true })`, `RequireAdmin`, `StatusDot`, `Table`).
- Produces: `AdminSessionsPage`. This is the final route table.

Spec sections 10.2 and 12.1. `GET /api/sessions?all=1` lists all sessions with their owner, state, agents, and creation time; the name links to the session page. Members get Not found and the page sends no `all=1` request.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`packages/web/src/routes/admin/sessions.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeMe, makeSession, SID, SID_2 } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { mockApi, type MockApi } from '../../test/mock-api';
import { MockEventSource } from '../../test/mock-event-source';
import { renderApp } from '../../test/render';

let api: MockApi;

beforeEach(() => {
  api = mockApi();
  MockEventSource.install();
});

describe('Admin → All sessions', () => {
  it('lists all sessions with their owner and state', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', { body: makeMe({ role: 'admin' }) });
    api.on('GET /api/sessions', (request) => ({
      body:
        request.query.get('all') === '1'
          ? [
              makeSession(),
              makeSession({
                id: SID_2,
                name: 'Shop',
                state: 'stopped',
                myRole: 'admin',
                owner: { id: 'u9', email: 'cleo@example.com', displayName: 'Cleo' },
              }),
            ]
          : [makeSession()],
    }));
    renderApp('/admin/sessions');
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'All sessions' });
    const shop = await within(table).findByRole('link', { name: 'Shop' });
    expect(shop).toHaveAttribute('href', `/sessions/${SID_2}`);
    expect(within(table).getByText('cleo@example.com')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(table).getByText('Stopped')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(table).getByRole('link', { name: 'Banking' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'href',
      `/sessions/${SID}`,
    );
  });

  it('shows the error of a failed load', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', { body: makeMe({ role: 'admin' }) });
    api.on('GET /api/sessions', (request) =>
      request.query.get('all') === '1'
        ? { status: 503, body: 'Service Unavailable' }
        : { body: [] },
    );
    renderApp('/admin/sessions');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('The request failed with HTTP 503.');
  });

  it('is not found for members', async () => {
    api.on('GET /api/auth/me', { body: makeMe() });
    api.on('GET /api/sessions', { body: [] });
    renderApp('/admin/sessions');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Not found' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(api.requests.some((r) => r.query.get('all') === '1')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import type { SessionResource } from '@hydra/shared';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessage } from '../../api/client';
import { useSessions } from '../../api/sessions';
import { Banner, ContentPane, StatusDot, Table, Toolbar, type Column } from '../../components/os';
import { formatDateTime } from '../../lib/format';

const COLUMNS: Column<SessionResource>[] = [
  {
    key: 'name',
    header: 'Name',
    cell: (s) => (
      <Link to={`/sessions/${s.id}`} className="text-os-blue-accessible hover:underline">
        {s.name}
      </Link>
    ),
  },
  {
    key: 'owner',
    header: 'Owner',
    cell: (s) => (
      <span className="flex flex-col">
        <span>{s.owner.displayName}</span>
        <span className="text-os-xs text-os-gray-accessible">{s.owner.email}</span>
      </span>
    ),
  },
  { key: 'state', header: 'State', cell: (s) => <StatusDot state={s.state} showLabel /> },
  {
    key: 'agents',
    header: 'Agents',
    align: 'right',
    cell: (s) => s.agentActivity.activeAgentCount,
  },
  { key: 'created', header: 'Created', cell: (s) => formatDateTime(s.createdAt) },
];

/** Admin → All sessions: all sessions with their owner and state (spec section 10.2). */
export function AdminSessionsPage() {
  const sessions = useSessions({ all: true });
  return (
    <>
      <Toolbar title="All sessions" />
      <ContentPane className="gap-4 p-6">
        {sessions.isError ? (
          <Banner variant="warning" role="alert">
            {errorMessage(sessions.error)}
          </Banner>
        ) : null}
        <Table
          label="All sessions"
          columns={COLUMNS}
          rows={sessions.data ?? []}
          rowKey={(s) => s.id}
          empty={sessions.isPending ? 'Loading…' : 'No sessions'}
        />
      </ContentPane>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { RouteObject } from 'react-router';
import { AdminSessionsPage } from './admin/sessions';
import { AdminUsersPage } from './admin/users';
import { ChangePasswordPage } from './change-password';
import { RequireAdmin, RequireLogin } from './guards';
import { LoginPage } from './login';
import { ActivityView } from './sessions/activity';
import { SessionIndex, SessionLayout } from './sessions/layout';
import { LiveView } from './sessions/live';
import { SettingsView } from './sessions/settings';
import { SharingView } from './sessions/sharing';
import { TokensView } from './sessions/tokens';
import { AppShell } from './shell/AppShell';
import { HomePage } from './shell/HomePage';
import { NotFoundPage } from './shell/NotFoundPage';

/** All routes (spec section 3.2). main.tsx and the tests build their routers from it. */
export const appRoutes: RouteObject[] = [
  { path: '/login', element: <LoginPage /> },
  {
    path: '/change-password',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin allowPasswordChange>
        <ChangePasswordPage />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
  },
  {
    path: '/',
    element: (
      <RequireLogin>
        <AppShell />
      </RequireLogin>
    ),
    children: [
      { index: true, element: <HomePage /> },
      {
        path: 'sessions/:sid',
        element: <SessionLayout />,
        children: [
          { index: true, element: <SessionIndex /> },
          { path: 'live', element: <LiveView /> },
          { path: 'tokens', element: <TokensView /> },
          { path: 'sharing', element: <SharingView /> },
          { path: 'activity', element: <ActivityView /> },
          { path: 'settings', element: <SettingsView /> },
        ],
      },
      {
        path: 'admin/users',
        element: (
          <RequireAdmin>
            <AdminUsersPage />
          </RequireAdmin>
        ),
      },
      {
        path: 'admin/sessions',
        element: (
          <RequireAdmin>
            <AdminSessionsPage />
          </RequireAdmin>
        ),
      },
      { path: '*', element: <NotFoundPage /> },
    ],
  },
];
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Run the tests to verify that they pass**

Run: `pnpm --filter @hydra/web exec vitest run src/routes/admin/sessions.test.tsx`
Expected: `Test Files  1 passed (1)` and `Tests  3 passed (3)`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Checkpoint**
  Run: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm --filter @hydra/web test`
  Expected: all pass.
  Do not commit (AGENTS.md rule 1). Report the changed and the new files to the controller.
